+// ---- elevation (Phase 10 plan §4.2.1; FR-071, FR-090, FR-091; PD-9, PD-18) -----------------------------------------
+
+/// The one elevated launch of a batch (FR-076): the helper and its four arguments.
+#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub struct ElevatedCommand {
+    pub program: PathBuf,
+    pub args: Vec<String>,
+}
+
+/// How a launch ended (FR-071).
+#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub enum LaunchOutcome {
+    Exited(i32),
+    Refused,
+    Failed(&'static str),
+    TimedOut,
+}
+
+/// The elevation launcher (FR-071, PD-9): the resolved helper, one launch, and its own guard.
+pub trait Elevation: Sync {
+    fn helper(&self) -> Option<&Path>;
+    fn launch(&self, command: &ElevatedCommand, timeout: Duration) -> LaunchOutcome;
+    fn guard(&self) -> &ElevationGuard;
+}
+
+/// What an `ElevationGuard` holds (FR-078, FR-078a).
+enum Hold {
+    Free,
+    Running,
+    /// A timed-out batch: its job and the result it may still get (FR-078a).
+    Stale {
+        job: PathBuf,
+        result: PathBuf,
+    },
+}
+
+/// One elevated batch at a time per launcher, with the stale hold of FR-078a (PD-9).
+pub struct ElevationGuard {
+    hold: Mutex<Hold>,
+}
+
+impl ElevationGuard {
+    pub const fn new() -> ElevationGuard {
+        ElevationGuard {
+            hold: Mutex::new(Hold::Free),
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// FR-078, FR-078a: a running batch refuses `{ stale: false }`; a stale hold ends once its result file exists
+    /// (the job and result are removed), else refuses `{ stale: true }`. Lock poisoning is ignored, as `BUSY`.
+    fn enter(&self, io: &dyn InstallIo) -> Result<Held<'_>, InstallError> {
+        let mut hold = self.hold.lock().unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner);
+        match &*hold {
+            Hold::Running => return Err(InstallError::ElevationBusy { stale: false }),
+            Hold::Stale { job, result } => {
+                if !matches!(io.kind_of(result), Ok(Some(NodeKind::File))) {
+                    return Err(InstallError::ElevationBusy { stale: true });
+                }
+                remove_job_files(io, job, result);
+            }
+            Hold::Free => {}
+        }
+        *hold = Hold::Running;
+        Ok(Held {
+            guard: self,
+            stale: None,
+        })
+    }
+}
+
+/// A held guard: dropping it frees the guard (on unwind too, FR-078) unless `stale` recorded a timed-out job.
+struct Held<'a> {
+    guard: &'a ElevationGuard,
+    stale: Option<Hold>,
+}
+
+impl Held<'_> {
+    /// FR-078a: the guard stays held by the timed-out job until its result appears.
+    fn stale(mut self, job: PathBuf, result: PathBuf) {
+        self.stale = Some(Hold::Stale { job, result });
+    }
+}
+
+impl Drop for Held<'_> {
+    fn drop(&mut self) {
+        let next = self.stale.take().unwrap_or(Hold::Free);
+        *self
+            .guard
+            .hold
+            .lock()
+            .unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner) = next;
+    }
+}
+
+/// FR-073: the job and its result removed (step `job_removed`), each error one `warn!`; an absent result is not
+/// touched.
+fn remove_job_files(io: &dyn InstallIo, job: &Path, result: &Path) {
+    for (path, which) in [(job, "job"), (result, "result")] {
+        if matches!(io.kind_of(path), Ok(None)) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        if let Err(e) = io.remove_file(path) {
+            warn!(
+                target: LOG_TARGET,
+                "{}: the {which} file was not removed",
+                io_err(Step::JobRemoved, &e).code()
+            );
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+impl Default for ElevationGuard {
+    fn default() -> ElevationGuard {
+        ElevationGuard::new()
+    }
+}
+
+/// FR-090: the fixed AppleScript; no job, feed or UI string is ever part of the script text.
+pub const OSASCRIPT_LINES: [&str; 3] = [
+    "on run argv",
+    "do shell script (quoted form of item 1 of argv) & \" \" & (quoted form of item 2 of argv) & \" \" & (quoted form of item 3 of argv) & \" \" & (quoted form of item 4 of argv) & \" \" & (quoted form of item 5 of argv) with administrator privileges",
+    "end run",
+];
+
+/// FR-077: the registrar kill's bound.
+pub const KILLALL_WAIT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(5);
+
+/// FR-090: `osascript`'s status and stderr → the outcome: exit 0 → `Exited(0)`; `(-128)` (the user cancelled) →
+/// `Refused`; stderr ending in `(<n>)` → `Exited(n)` (`do shell script`'s error number is the helper's exit status,
+/// so only a non-negative number is one; a negative one is AppleScript's own error); otherwise
+/// `Failed("E_ELEVATION_OSASCRIPT")`.
+pub fn osascript_outcome(status: Option<i32>, stderr: &str) -> LaunchOutcome {
+    if status == Some(0) {
+        return LaunchOutcome::Exited(0);
+    }
+    if stderr.contains("(-128)") {
+        return LaunchOutcome::Refused;
+    }
+    let number = stderr
+        .trim_end()
+        .strip_suffix(')')
+        .and_then(|rest| rest.rsplit_once('('))
+        .map(|(_, n)| n)
+        .filter(|n| !n.is_empty() && n.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit()))
+        .and_then(|n| n.parse::<i32>().ok());
+    match number {
+        Some(n) => LaunchOutcome::Exited(n),
+        None => LaunchOutcome::Failed("E_ELEVATION_OSASCRIPT"),
+    }
+}
+
+/// FR-091: the `lpParameters` string for `args`, by the MS C argument rules: an argument holding `"` or NUL is
+/// refused; one holding a space or tab (or empty, which would otherwise vanish) is quoted with its trailing
+/// backslashes doubled; every other backslash, and the verbatim `\\?\` prefix, is ordinary text.
+pub fn windows_parameters(args: &[String]) -> Result<String, InstallError> {
+    let mut out = String::new();
+    for (i, arg) in args.iter().enumerate() {
+        if arg.contains(['"', '\0']) {
+            return Err(InstallError::Elevation {
+                code: "E_ELEVATION_ARGS",
+            });
+        }
+        if i > 0 {
+            out.push(' ');
+        }
+        if arg.is_empty() || arg.contains([' ', '\t']) {
+            let trailing = arg.len() - arg.trim_end_matches('\\').len();
+            out.push('"');
+            out.push_str(arg);
+            out.extend(std::iter::repeat_n('\\', trailing));
+            out.push('"');
+        } else {
+            out.push_str(arg);
+        }
+    }
+    Ok(out)
+}
+
+/// The helper's file name beside the manager, without the Windows `.exe` (FR-090).
+const HELPER_NAME: &str = "krate-manager-helper";
+
+/// FR-090, PD-23: the helper beside `exe` (`krate-manager-helper`, `.exe` on Windows) when it is a file; `None` on
+/// Linux, for no `exe`, or for an `exe` without a non-empty parent (a bare name never resolves against the CWD).
+pub fn helper_beside(os: Os, exe: Option<&Path>, io: &dyn InstallIo) -> Option<PathBuf> {
+    if os == Os::Linux {
+        return None;
+    }
+    let dir = exe?.parent().filter(|p| !p.as_os_str().is_empty())?;
+    let path = if os == Os::Windows {
+        dir.join(format!("{HELPER_NAME}.exe"))
+    } else {
+        dir.join(HELPER_NAME)
+    };
+    matches!(io.kind_of(&path), Ok(Some(NodeKind::File))).then_some(path)
+}
+

+    struct JobFile<'a> {
+        job_version: u32,
+        ops: &'a [JobOp],
+    }
+
+    /// A private mirror of the helper's result file (FR-003); any other key is refused.
+    #[derive(Deserialize)]
+    #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
+    struct ResultMirror {
+        result_version: u32,
+        job_sha256: String,
+        job_error: Option<String>,
+        ops: Vec<OpMirror>,
+    }
+
+    /// One op entry of the mirror.
+    #[derive(Deserialize)]
+    #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
+    struct OpMirror {
+        index: u32,
+        op: String,
+        outcome: String,
+        code: Option<String>,
+        exit_code: Option<i32>,
+        seconds: f64,
+        log_tail: Vec<String>,
+    }
+
+    /// The `op` value of each kind (FR-001).
+    fn kind_name(op: &JobOp) -> &'static str {
+        match op {
+            JobOp::RunInnoInstaller { .. } => "run_inno_installer",
+            JobOp::RunInnoUninstaller { .. } => "run_inno_uninstaller",
+            JobOp::RunPkgInstaller { .. } => "run_pkg_installer",
+            JobOp::UninstallPkg { .. } => "uninstall_pkg",
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// PD-19: `job-<32 lower hex>` from two `RandomState` hashers ("initialized with random keys") over a process
+    /// counter and the process id; collision-checked by `write_new`, not a secret.
+    fn job_stem() -> String {
+        static COUNTER: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
+        let n = COUNTER.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
+        let a = RandomState::new().hash_one((n, std::process::id()));
+        let b = RandomState::new().hash_one((std::process::id(), n));
+        format!("job-{a:016x}{b:016x}")
+    }
+
+    /// FR-073: `<state>/jobs/job-<32 hex>.json`, written new and synced, hashed over the written bytes.
+    pub fn write(
+        io: &dyn InstallIo,
+        state_dir: &Path,
+        ops: Vec<JobOp>,
+    ) -> Result<WrittenJob, InstallError> {
+        let err = at(Step::JobWritten);
+        let jobs = state_dir.join(JOBS_DIR);
+        match io.kind_of(&jobs).map_err(&err)? {
+            Some(NodeKind::Dir) => {}
+            None => match io.create_dir(&jobs) {
+                Ok(()) => {}
+                // Made meanwhile (another launcher of the same state dir): fine when it is a directory.
+                Err(e)
+                    if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::AlreadyExists
+                        && matches!(io.kind_of(&jobs), Ok(Some(NodeKind::Dir))) => {}
+                Err(e) => return Err(err(e)),
+            },
+            Some(_) => return Err(InstallError::Path { reason: NOT_A_DIR }),
+        }
+        let dir = io.canonicalize(&jobs).map_err(&err)?;
+        if dir.to_str().is_none() {
+            return Err(InstallError::PathNotUtf8 { which: "state" });
+        }
+        let bytes = serde_json::to_vec(&JobFile {
+            job_version: 1,
+            ops: &ops,
+        })
+        .map_err(|_| InstallError::Path {
+            reason: "the job does not serialise",
+        })?;
+        for _ in 0..NAME_TRIES {
+            let stem = job_stem();
+            let path = dir.join(format!("{stem}.json"));
+            if let Err(e) = io.write_new(&path, &bytes) {
+                if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::AlreadyExists {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                discard_partial(io, &path);
+                return Err(err(e));
+            }
+            if let Err(e) = io.sync_file(&path) {
+                discard_partial(io, &path);
+                return Err(err(e));
+            }
+            let mut sha = Sha256Stream::new();
+            sha.update(&bytes);
+            return Ok(WrittenJob {
+                result: dir.join(format!("{stem}.result.json")),
+                path,
+                stem,
+                sha256: hex(&sha.finish()),
+                ops,
+            });
+        }
+        Err(err(io::Error::from(io::ErrorKind::AlreadyExists)))
+    }
+
+    /// A job file a failed write left behind is removed (best effort; the next start's recovery removes it too).
+    fn discard_partial(io: &dyn InstallIo, path: &Path) {
+        if matches!(io.kind_of(path), Ok(Some(NodeKind::File))) {
+            let _ = io.remove_file(path);
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// D14, PD-17: the batch ceiling, the sum of the op ceilings plus `ELEVATION_MARGIN`.
+    pub fn ceiling(ops: &[JobOp]) -> Duration {
+        ops.iter()
+            .map(|op| match op {
+                JobOp::RunInnoInstaller { .. } => INNO_INSTALL_TIMEOUT,
+                JobOp::RunPkgInstaller { .. } => PKG_INSTALL_TIMEOUT,
+                // The uninstaller's own wait, then the poll (PD-24).
+                JobOp::RunInnoUninstaller { .. } => 2 * UNINSTALL_POLL_TIMEOUT,
+                // Three reads and `--forget`.
+                JobOp::UninstallPkg { .. } => 4 * PKGUTIL_TIMEOUT,
+            })
+            .sum::<Duration>()
+            + ELEVATION_MARGIN
+    }
+
+    /// FR-076, SI-11: the result's per-op reports, or why there are none: `result_version` 1 and the job's hash, else
+    /// `NoResult`; `job_error` → `Helper(code)`; then one entry per op, in order, of the job's kinds, else `NoResult`.
+    /// A code outside the catalogue is `NoResult` (PD-11).
+    pub fn read_result(bytes: &[u8], job: &WrittenJob) -> Result<Vec<OpReport>, ResultError> {
+        let mirror = parse(bytes).ok_or(ResultError::NoResult)?;
+        reports(&mirror, job)
+    }
+
+    /// `read_result` with FR-114's lines: one `info!` per op (catalogue code, exit code, seconds), the log tail as
+    /// `debug!`.
+    pub(super) fn read_logged(
+        bytes: &[u8],
+        job: &WrittenJob,
+    ) -> Result<Vec<OpReport>, ResultError> {
+        let mirror = parse(bytes).ok_or(ResultError::NoResult)?;
+        for op in &mirror.ops {
+            info!(
+                target: LOG_TARGET,
+                "helper op {} {}: {}, code {}, exit {}, {:.1} s",
+                op.index,
+                JOB_KINDS
+                    .iter()
+                    .find(|k| **k == op.op)
+                    .copied()
+                    .unwrap_or("unknown"),
+                OUTCOMES
+                    .iter()
+                    .find(|k| **k == op.outcome)
+                    .copied()
+                    .unwrap_or("unknown"),
+                op.code
+                    .as_deref()
+                    .map(|c| helper_code(c).unwrap_or("unknown"))
+                    .unwrap_or("none"),
+                op.exit_code
+                    .map_or_else(|| "none".to_owned(), |c| c.to_string()),
+                op.seconds
+            );
+            for line in &op.log_tail {
+                debug!(target: LOG_TARGET, "helper op {} log: {line}", op.index);
+            }
+        }
+        reports(&mirror, job)
+    }
+
+    /// The four `op` values and the five outcomes (FR-001, FR-003), for the log lines.
+    const JOB_KINDS: [&str; 4] = [
+        "run_inno_installer",
+        "run_inno_uninstaller",
+        "run_pkg_installer",
+        "uninstall_pkg",
+    ];
+    const OUTCOMES: [&str; 5] = ["done", "locked", "failed", "refused", "not_run"];
+
+    fn parse(bytes: &[u8]) -> Option<ResultMirror> {
+        if u64::try_from(bytes.len()).unwrap_or(u64::MAX) > MAX_RESULT_BYTES {
+            return None;
+        }
+        serde_json::from_slice(bytes).ok()
+    }
+
+    fn reports(mirror: &ResultMirror, job: &WrittenJob) -> Result<Vec<OpReport>, ResultError> {
+        if mirror.result_version != 1 || mirror.job_sha256 != job.sha256 {
+            return Err(ResultError::NoResult);
+        }
+        if let Some(code) = &mirror.job_error {
+            return Err(helper_code(code).map_or(ResultError::NoResult, ResultError::Helper));
+        }
+        if mirror.ops.len() != job.ops.len() {
+            return Err(ResultError::NoResult);
+        }
+        mirror
+            .ops
+            .iter()
+            .zip(&job.ops)
+            .enumerate()
+            .map(|(i, (entry, op))| {
+                let in_order = usize::try_from(entry.index).ok() == Some(i);
+                if !in_order || entry.op != kind_name(op) {
+                    return Err(ResultError::NoResult);
+                }
+                let code = || {
+                    entry
+                        .code
+                        .as_deref()
+                        .and_then(helper_code)
+                        .ok_or(ResultError::NoResult)
+                };
+                Ok(match entry.outcome.as_str() {
+                    "done" => OpReport::Done,
+                    "locked" => OpReport::Locked,
+                    "failed" => OpReport::Failed {
+                        code: code()?,
+                        exit_code: entry.exit_code,
+                    },
+                    "refused" => OpReport::Refused { code: code()? },
+                    "not_run" => OpReport::NotRun { code: code()? },
+                    _ => return Err(ResultError::NoResult),
+                })
+            })
+            .collect()
+    }
+
+    /// PD-11: the helper code catalogue; an unknown code is `None`.
+    pub fn helper_code(s: &str) -> Option<&'static str> {
+        HELPER_CODES.iter().find(|c| **c == s).copied()
+    }
+}
+
+// ---- the elevated drivers (Phase 10 plan §4.2.3; FR-070, FR-072 to FR-078a, FR-114; PD-16, PD-17) ------------------
+
+/// One action of an elevated batch: its entry, its kind and its op's index in the job, or why it has none (FR-072).
+struct Slot {
+    entry: PluginEntry,
+    kind: ActionKind,
+    op: Result<usize, InstallError>,
+}
+
+/// The Windows and macOS batch (plan §4.2.3's eleven steps): each action's op, at most one job and one launch, the
+/// result mapped per op, the registrar kill (macOS), the confirmation by detection, one log line per action.
+fn run_elevated(os: Os, env: InstallEnv<'_>, batch: Vec<Action>) -> Vec<ActionResult> {
+    let InstallEnv {
+        ctx,
+        io,
+        progress,
+        elevation,
+        host_io,
+        host,
+        ..
+    } = env;
+    // 1. Each action's op, or its refusal; nothing mutates.
+    let staging = io.canonicalize(ctx.roots.staging_dir()).ok();
+    let mut ops = Vec::new();
+    let slots: Vec<Slot> = batch
+        .into_iter()
+        .map(|action| {
+            let kind = action.kind();
+            let (entry, op) = match action {
+                Action::Install { entry, artifact } | Action::Update { entry, artifact } => {
+                    let op = installer_op(os, io, staging.as_deref(), &artifact);
+                    (entry, op)
+                }
+                Action::Uninstall { entry } => {
+                    let op = uninstall_op(os, ctx, io, host, &entry);
+                    (entry, op)
+                }
+            };
+            let op = op.map(|op| {
+                ops.push(op);
+                ops.len() - 1
+            });
+            Slot { entry, kind, op }
+        })
+        .collect();
+    // 2 to 8. The job, the launch and the result (no op, no job).
+    let reports = if ops.is_empty() {
+        Ok(Vec::new())
+    } else {
+        elevate(ctx, io, progress, elevation, &ops)
+    };
+    let report_of = |slot: &Slot| -> Result<&job::OpReport, InstallError> {
+        let index = slot.op.clone()?;
+        match &reports {
+            Ok(reports) => reports.get(index).ok_or(InstallError::NoResult),
+            Err(e) => Err(e.clone()),
+        }
+    };
+    // 9. macOS: the registrar kill once, keyed on the helper's `done` of an install or update (FR-077).
+    let installed = slots
+        .iter()
+        .any(|s| s.kind != ActionKind::Remove && matches!(report_of(s), Ok(job::OpReport::Done)));
+    if os == Os::Macos && installed {
+        let _ = host_io.run(&platform::KILLALL_ARGV, KILLALL_WAIT);
+    }
+    // 10, 11. Per action: the mapped report, a `done` confirmed by detection (FR-076a), one log line.
+    slots
+        .iter()
+        .map(|slot| {
+            let outcome = match report_of(slot) {
+                Err(e) => Err(e),
+                Ok(job::OpReport::Done) => {
+                    if confirmed(os, ctx, host, &slot.entry, slot.kind) {
+                        Ok(Outcome {
+                            version: (slot.kind != ActionKind::Remove)
+                                .then(|| slot.entry.version_text.clone()),
+                            placed: 0,
+                            removed: 0,
+                            kept: Vec::new(),
+                        })
+                    } else {
+                        Err(InstallError::NotApplied)
+                    }
+                }
+                Ok(job::OpReport::Locked) => Err(InstallError::Locked),
+                Ok(job::OpReport::Failed {
+                    code: "E_HELPER_EXIT",
+                    exit_code: Some(exit_code),
+                }) => Err(InstallError::InstallerFailed {
+                    exit_code: *exit_code,
+                }),
+                Ok(
+                    job::OpReport::Failed { code, .. }
+                    | job::OpReport::Refused { code }
+                    | job::OpReport::NotRun { code },
+                ) => Err(InstallError::Helper { code }),
+            };
+            let name = slot.entry.name.clone();
+            log_result(&name, &slot.entry.version_text, slot.kind, &outcome);
+            ActionResult {
+                name,
+                action: slot.kind,
+                outcome,
+            }
+        })
+        .collect()
+}
+
+/// FR-072: an installer op from a verified artifact of the OS's format inside the canonical staging dir; the op's
+/// `sha256` is the digest verification checked, never a re-hash (§6 "Verify before execute").
+fn installer_op(
+    os: Os,
+    io: &dyn InstallIo,
+    staging: Option<&Path>,
+    artifact: &VerifiedArtifact,
+) -> Result<job::JobOp, InstallError> {
+    let format = if os == Os::Windows {
+        AssetFormat::InnoExe
+    } else {
+        AssetFormat::Pkg
+    };
+    if artifact.format() != format {
+        return Err(InstallError::Format);
+    }
+    let outside = || InstallError::Path {
+        reason: "the artifact is not in staging",
+    };
+    let staging = staging.ok_or_else(outside)?;
+    let file = io.canonicalize(artifact.path()).map_err(|_| outside())?;
+    if file == staging || !file.starts_with(staging) {
+        return Err(outside());
+    }
+    let file = file
+        .to_str()
+        .ok_or(InstallError::PathNotUtf8 { which: "staging" })?
+        .to_owned();
+    let sha256 = hex(artifact.sha256());
+    Ok(if os == Os::Windows {
+        job::JobOp::RunInnoInstaller { file, sha256 }
+    } else {
+        job::JobOp::RunPkgInstaller { file, sha256 }
+    })
+}
+
+/// FR-072: an uninstall op from a fresh detection's gate: `Inno` / `Pkg`; `OutsideRoot` → `E_INSTALL_REMOVE_ELSEWHERE`;
+/// anything else `E_INSTALL_NOT_MANAGED`.
+fn uninstall_op(
+    os: Os,
+    ctx: &AppContext,
+    io: &dyn InstallIo,
+    host: &dyn HostFacts,
+    entry: &PluginEntry,
+) -> Result<job::JobOp, InstallError> {
+    let detection = detection_of(os, ctx, entry, host);
+    match action_gate(os, ctx, io, entry, &detection) {
+        ActionGate::Blocked(e) => Err(e),
+        ActionGate::Open { remove, .. } => match (os, remove) {
+            (Os::Windows, RemoveBasis::Inno { app_id }) => {
+                Ok(job::JobOp::RunInnoUninstaller { app_id })
+            }
+            (Os::Macos, RemoveBasis::Pkg { pkg_id }) => Ok(job::JobOp::UninstallPkg { pkg_id }),
+            (_, RemoveBasis::OutsideRoot) => Err(InstallError::RemoveElsewhere),
+            _ => Err(InstallError::NotManaged {
+                name: entry.name.clone(),
+            }),
+        },
+    }
+}
+
+/// One listing scan of the entry's bundle and its detection, read-only (FR-076a, FR-082).
+fn detection_of(os: Os, ctx: &AppContext, entry: &PluginEntry, host: &dyn HostFacts) -> Detection {
+    let bundle = entry
+        .release
+        .as_ref()
+        .and_then(|r| r.install.as_ref())
+        .map(|f| f.vst3_bundle.as_str());
+    let names: Vec<&str> = bundle.into_iter().collect();
+    let listing = Listing::scan(ctx.roots.plugin_roots(), &names, ListingLimits::PRODUCTION);
+    detect::detect_plugin(os, ctx, entry, host, &listing)
+}
+
+/// Steps 2 to 8: the helper, the guard, the job, `Elevating`, the one launch, its outcome, the job files removed
+/// (kept after a timeout, FR-078a), the guard released.
+fn elevate(
+    ctx: &AppContext,
+    io: &dyn InstallIo,
+    progress: &mut dyn FnMut(InstallProgress),
+    elevation: &dyn Elevation,
+    ops: &[job::JobOp],
+) -> Result<Vec<job::OpReport>, InstallError> {
+    let helper = elevation.helper().ok_or(InstallError::HelperMissing)?;
+    let held = elevation.guard().enter(io)?;
+    let state = ctx.roots.state_dir();
+    ctx.roots.check_sandboxed(&state.join(job::JOBS_DIR));
+    let written = job::write(io, state, ops.to_vec())?;
+    progress(InstallProgress::Elevating);
+    let command = ElevatedCommand {
+        program: helper.to_path_buf(),
+        args: vec![
+            "--job".to_owned(),
+            text(&written.path),
+            "--job-sha256".to_owned(),
+            written.sha256.clone(),
+        ],
+    };
+    let outcome = elevation.launch(&command, job::ceiling(ops));
+    info!(target: LOG_TARGET, "elevated batch of {} op(s): launch {outcome:?}", ops.len());
+    let reports = match outcome {
+        LaunchOutcome::Refused => Err(InstallError::Refused),
+        LaunchOutcome::Failed(code) => Err(InstallError::Elevation { code }),
+        LaunchOutcome::TimedOut => {
+            held.stale(written.path, written.result);
+            return Err(InstallError::NoResult);
+        }
+        LaunchOutcome::Exited(0 | 3) => read_reports(io, &written),
+        LaunchOutcome::Exited(_) => Err(InstallError::NoResult),
+    };
+    remove_job_files(io, &written.path, &written.result);
+    drop(held);
+    reports
+}
+
+/// FR-076: the result file, bounded to 1 MiB, through the reader.
+fn read_reports(
+    io: &dyn InstallIo,
+    written: &job::WrittenJob,
+) -> Result<Vec<job::OpReport>, InstallError> {
+    let bytes = io
+        .read_file(&written.result, job::MAX_RESULT_BYTES)
+        .map_err(|_| InstallError::NoResult)?;
+    job::read_logged(&bytes, written).map_err(|e| match e {
+        job::ResultError::NoResult => InstallError::NoResult,
+        job::ResultError::Helper(code) => InstallError::Helper { code },
+    })
+}
+
+/// FR-076a: an install or update is confirmed when the managed VST3 copy's version is the entry's; a remove when
+/// no managed copy and no installer record remain (an unreadable record confirms nothing).
+fn confirmed(
+    os: Os,
+    ctx: &AppContext,
+    host: &dyn HostFacts,
+    entry: &PluginEntry,
+    kind: ActionKind,
+) -> bool {
+    let Detection::Checked {
+        copies,
+        record,
+        record_error,
+        ..
+    } = detection_of(os, ctx, entry, host)
+    else {
+        return false;
+    };
+    match kind {
+        ActionKind::Remove => {
+            record.is_none() && record_error.is_none() && !copies.iter().any(|c| c.managed)
+        }
+        ActionKind::Install | ActionKind::Update => copies.iter().any(|c| {
+            c.format == CopyFormat::Vst3
+                && c.managed
+                && matches!(c.version, CopyVersion::Known { version, .. } if Some(version) == entry.version)
+        }),
+    }
+}

+/// FR-074: `job-<32 lower hex>.json` or `job-<32 lower hex>.result.json`.
+fn is_job_file_name(name: &str) -> bool {

@@ -2711,0 +3676,53 @@ pub fn action_gate(
+/// FR-080: Linux `Manifest` iff a manifest; Windows `Inno` iff the record is an Inno record whose install location's
+/// parent is `<Vst3Global>\Krate Audio` and the entry has an uninstall key (`app_id` the key up to its first `}`,
+/// PD-13), `OutsideRoot` for a location elsewhere; macOS `Pkg` iff a pkg record and a pkg id; else `NotManaged`.
+fn remove_basis(
+    os: Os,
+    ctx: &AppContext,
+    entry: &PluginEntry,
+    detection: &Detection,
+    has_manifest: bool,
+) -> RemoveBasis {
+    let facts = entry.release.as_ref().and_then(|r| r.install.as_ref());
+    let record = match detection {
+        Detection::Checked { record, .. } => record.as_ref(),
+        Detection::NotChecked => None,
+    };
+    match os {
+        Os::Linux if has_manifest => RemoveBasis::Manifest,
+        Os::Linux => RemoveBasis::NotManaged,
+        Os::Windows => {
+            let key = facts.and_then(|f| f.windows_uninstall_key.as_ref());
+            let (
+                Some(InstallerRecord::Inno {
+                    install_location: Some(location),
+                    ..
+                }),
+                Some(key),
+            ) = (record, key)
+            else {
+                return RemoveBasis::NotManaged;
+            };
+            let vendor = ctx.roots.vst3_global().join(platform::VENDOR_DIR);
+            if !location
+                .parent()
+                .is_some_and(|parent| detect::same_path(parent, &vendor))
+            {
+                return RemoveBasis::OutsideRoot;
+            }
+            match key.as_str().find('}') {
+                Some(end) => RemoveBasis::Inno {
+                    app_id: key.as_str()[..=end].to_owned(),
+                },
+                None => RemoveBasis::NotManaged,
+            }
+        }
+        Os::Macos => match (record, facts.and_then(|f| f.macos_pkg_id.as_ref())) {
+            (Some(InstallerRecord::Pkg { .. }), Some(pkg_id)) => RemoveBasis::Pkg {
+                pkg_id: pkg_id.as_str().to_owned(),
+            },
+            _ => RemoveBasis::NotManaged,
+        },
+    }
+}

+//! `install::loopback`: the `Elevation` that runs the helper library in-process against temp roots (owner T010; plan §4.4).
+//!
+//! `launch` parses the argv with `cli::parse_args` and calls `krate_manager_helper::run` over a `TempIo` on the test's
+//! base, a `FakeRegistry` and a runner that answers every child with a `ScriptedRunner` script whose effect
+//! "installs" or "uninstalls" under the temp roots. Every root it hands the helper lies under the base (tasks Rule 1),
+//! so a root left on a production value panics in `TempIo` instead of touching the machine.
+//!
+//! Spawn indices (`exit_for`, `never_exits`, `serve_log`) count every `ProcessRunner::spawn` the helper makes through
+//! this loopback, across launches, from 0; the `pkgutil` queries made through `output` are not counted.
+
+use super::job::{self, JobOp, WrittenJob};
+use super::{ElevatedCommand, Elevation, ElevationGuard, FsInstallIo, InstallIo, LaunchOutcome};
+use crate::detect::{InnoValues, PkgOutputs, RecordRead};
+use crate::download::DOWNLOADS_DIR;
+use crate::feed::{InstallFacts, PlatformKey, PluginEntry};
+use crate::platform::{Os, VENDOR_DIR};
+use crate::roots::{RootKind, Roots};
+use crate::test_signer::TestSigner;
+use crate::test_support::{LiveHostFacts, TestPlugin, fresh_elevated_roots, test_feed};
+use krate_manager_helper::classify::{InnoVerdict, classify_inno};
+use krate_manager_helper::roots::{
+    AllowedRoots, HELPER_FOLDER, MacosRoots, OsRoots, StateDirRule, WindowsRoots,
+};
+use krate_manager_helper::seams::fake::{
+    Event, FakeRegistry, Match, Script, ScriptedRunner, TempIo, World,
+};
+use krate_manager_helper::seams::{ChildId, ProcessRunner, Registry, Seams};
+use krate_manager_helper::{HelperOs, cli, digest, error, ops};
+use serde_json::Value;
+use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
+use std::ffi::OsString;
+use std::fs;
+use std::io;
+use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
+use std::sync::{Mutex, MutexGuard, PoisonError};
+use std::time::Duration;
+
+/// The helper path the loopback reports: a bare name, never executed.
+const LOOPBACK_HELPER: &str = "loopback-krate-manager-helper";
+/// The Windows run base under the test's base (plan §4.4).
+const RUN_DIR: &str = "run";
+/// The macOS `/Applications` stand-in under the base.
+const APPLICATIONS_DIR: &str = "Applications";
+/// The macOS run ancestors under the base: `<base>/Library`, `<base>/Library/Application Support`.
+const LIBRARY_DIR: &str = "Library";
+const APP_SUPPORT_DIR: &str = "Application Support";
+/// The components root beside the temp VST3 global root when the roots carry no AU root (off macOS).
+const AU_FALLBACK_DIR: &str = "au-global";
+/// The uninstaller an Inno setup leaves in its install directory (FR-031).
+const UNINS_NAME: &str = "unins000.exe";
+/// The Inno argv's log argument prefix (FR-030 step 3).
+const LOG_ARG: &str = "/LOG=";
+/// The `pkgutil` query flags (FR-033).
+const PKG_INFO: &str = "--pkg-info";
+const FILES: &str = "--files";
+const ONLY_FILES: &str = "--only-files";
+const ONLY_DIRS: &str = "--only-dirs";
+/// The usage exit of the helper binary (FR-005).
+const USAGE_EXIT: i32 = 2;
+
+/// What one spawn does instead of its default effect.
+#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+enum Plan {
+    Exit(i32),
+    NeverExits,
+}
+
+/// A Windows install the loopback made: its record key, registry subkey and install directory.
+#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
+struct InnoInstall {
+    key: String,
+    subkey: String,
+    dir: PathBuf,
+}
+
+/// A macOS receipt the loopback made: the three `pkgutil` outputs.
+#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
+struct Receipt {
+    info: String,
+    files: String,
+    dirs: String,
+}
+
+/// What a spawn's script does to the temp world when it runs.
+enum Act {
+    InnoInstall(PluginEntry),
+    PkgInstall(PluginEntry),
+    InnoUninstall(String),
+}
+
+#[derive(Default)]
+struct State {
+    /// Installer digest (lower hex) → the entry a run of it installs.
+    installs: BTreeMap<String, PluginEntry>,
+    plans: BTreeMap<usize, Plan>,
+    logs: BTreeMap<usize, Vec<u8>>,
+    launches: Vec<ElevatedCommand>,
+    jobs: Vec<Vec<u8>>,
+    spawns: Vec<Vec<OsString>>,
+    /// Keyed by the uninstaller path as the registry holds it (unquoted).
+    inno: BTreeMap<String, InnoInstall>,
+    /// Keyed by pkg id.
+    pkgs: BTreeMap<String, Receipt>,
+}
+
+/// The loopback `Elevation` (plan §4.4, PD-8, SI-19; tasks TA-14).
+pub(super) struct Loopback<'a> {
+    os: Os,
+    base: PathBuf,
+    roots: AllowedRoots,
+    world: World,
+    host: &'a LiveHostFacts,
+    guard: ElevationGuard,
+    classify: fn(i32, &[String]) -> InnoVerdict,
+    helper: PathBuf,
+    state: Mutex<State>,
+}
+
+impl<'a> Loopback<'a> {
+    /// The loopback for `os` over `roots` (from `fresh_elevated_roots()`), `base` its `TempDir`; records go to `host`.
+    pub(super) fn new(os: Os, roots: &Roots, base: &Path, host: &'a LiveHostFacts) -> Loopback<'a> {
+        let base = fs::canonicalize(base)
+            .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("loopback cannot canonicalize {}: {e}", base.display()));
+        let world = World::new();
+        let made = |dir: &Path| {
+            assert!(
+                dir != base && dir.starts_with(&base),
+                "loopback: {} is outside the test sandbox {}",
+                dir.display(),
+                base.display()
+            );
+            fs::create_dir_all(dir)
+                .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("loopback cannot create {}: {e}", dir.display()));
+        };
+        let os_roots = match os {
+            Os::Windows => {
+                let run_base = base.join(RUN_DIR);
+                made(&run_base);
+                OsRoots::Windows(WindowsRoots {
+                    vst3_vendor: roots.vst3_global().join(VENDOR_DIR),
+                    preset_vendor: roots.presets().factory.clone(),
+                    run_base,
+                })
+            }
+            Os::Macos => {
+                let library = base.join(LIBRARY_DIR);
+                let app_support = library.join(APP_SUPPORT_DIR);
+                let run_base = app_support.join(HELPER_FOLDER);
+                made(&app_support);
+                for dir in [&library, &app_support, &run_base] {
+                    world.root_owned(dir);
+                }
+                let components = roots
+                    .plugin_roots()
+                    .iter()
+                    .find(|r| matches!(r.kind, RootKind::AuGlobal))
+                    .map(|r| r.path.clone())
+                    .unwrap_or_else(|| roots.vst3_global().with_file_name(AU_FALLBACK_DIR));
+                OsRoots::Macos(MacosRoots {
+                    vst3: roots.vst3_global().to_path_buf(),
+                    components,
+                    presets: roots.presets().factory.clone(),
+                    applications: base.join(APPLICATIONS_DIR),
+                    volume: base.clone(),
+                    run_base,
+                    run_ancestors: vec![library, app_support],
+                })
+            }
+            Os::Linux => OsRoots::None,
+        };
+        Loopback {
+            os,
+            roots: AllowedRoots {
+                os: os_roots,
+                state_dir: StateDirRule::Exact(roots.state_dir().to_path_buf()),
+            },
+            base,
+            world,
+            host,
+            guard: ElevationGuard::new(),
+            classify: classify_inno,
+            helper: PathBuf::from(LOOPBACK_HELPER),
+            state: Mutex::new(State::default()),
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// The Inno outcome rule the helper runs with (default `classify_inno`).
+    pub(super) fn classify(self, f: fn(i32, &[String]) -> InnoVerdict) -> Self {
+        Loopback {
+            classify: f,
+            ..self
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// A run of the installer whose digest is `sha256_hex` installs `entry` at its version: Windows an Inno record in
+    /// `host.inno`, the registry key with its `UninstallString` and that file, and the VST3 bundle under the vendor
+    /// root; macOS a pkg record in `host.pkg`, the receipt `pkgutil` reports, and the VST3 and component bundles.
+    pub(super) fn installs(&self, sha256_hex: &str, entry: &PluginEntry) {
+        self.state()
+            .installs
+            .insert(sha256_hex.to_owned(), entry.clone());
+    }
+
+    /// Spawn `spawn_index` exits `code` and installs nothing.
+    pub(super) fn exit_for(&self, spawn_index: usize, code: i32) {
+        self.state().plans.insert(spawn_index, Plan::Exit(code));
+    }
+
+    /// Spawn `spawn_index` never exits (the helper's wait times out) and installs nothing.
+    pub(super) fn never_exits(&self, spawn_index: usize) {
+        self.state().plans.insert(spawn_index, Plan::NeverExits);
+    }
+
+    /// Spawn `spawn_index`, an Inno setup, writes `log` as its `/LOG=` file (default: an empty log).
+    pub(super) fn serve_log(&self, spawn_index: usize, log: Vec<u8>) {
+        self.state().logs.insert(spawn_index, log);
+    }
+
+    /// Every launch, in order.
+    pub(super) fn launches(&self) -> Vec<ElevatedCommand> {
+        self.state().launches.clone()
+    }
+
+    /// The job bytes of every launch, read before the helper ran.
+    pub(super) fn jobs(&self) -> Vec<Vec<u8>> {
+        self.state().jobs.clone()
+    }
+
+    /// The argv of every spawn, in spawn-index order.
+    pub(super) fn spawns(&self) -> Vec<Vec<OsString>> {
+        self.state().spawns.clone()
+    }
+
+    fn state(&self) -> MutexGuard<'_, State> {
+        self.state.lock().unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner)
+    }
+
+    fn helper_os(&self) -> HelperOs {
+        match self.os {
+            Os::Windows => HelperOs::Windows,
+            Os::Macos => HelperOs::Macos,
+            Os::Linux => HelperOs::Linux,
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// Panics unless `path` is strictly under the base (lexical; for paths the loopback builds itself).
+    fn check(&self, path: &Path) {
+        assert!(
+            path != self.base && path.starts_with(&self.base),
+            "loopback: {} is outside the test sandbox {}",
+            path.display(),
+            self.base.display()
+        );
+    }
+
+    /// A path the helper named, with its parent canonicalized, checked strictly under the base.
+    fn sandboxed(&self, path: &Path) -> PathBuf {
+        let (Some(parent), Some(name)) = (path.parent(), path.file_name()) else {
+            panic!("loopback: {} has no parent", path.display());
+        };
+        let parent = fs::canonicalize(parent)
+            .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("loopback cannot canonicalize {}: {e}", parent.display()));
+        let out = parent.join(name);
+        self.check(&out);
+        out
+    }
+
+    fn write(&self, path: &Path, bytes: &[u8]) {
+        self.check(path);
+        if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
+            fs::create_dir_all(parent)
+                .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("loopback cannot create {}: {e}", parent.display()));
+        }
+        fs::write(path, bytes)
+            .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("loopback cannot write {}: {e}", path.display()));
+    }
+
+    /// A VST3 bundle with a `moduleinfo.json` of `version`; returns the file written.
+    fn write_vst3(&self, bundle: &Path, name: &str, version: &str) -> PathBuf {
+        let info = bundle
+            .join("Contents")
+            .join("Resources")
+            .join("moduleinfo.json");
+        let text = serde_json::json!({ "Name": name, "Version": version });
+        self.write(&info, text.to_string().as_bytes());
+        info
+    }
+
+    /// An AU component with an XML `Info.plist` of `version`; returns the file written.
+    fn write_component(&self, bundle: &Path, version: &str) -> PathBuf {
+        let plist = bundle.join("Contents").join("Info.plist");
+        let text = format!(
+            "<?xml version=\"1.0\" encoding=\"UTF-8\"?>\n<plist version=\"1.0\"><dict>\
+             <key>CFBundleShortVersionString</key><string>{version}</string></dict></plist>\n"
+        );
+        self.write(&plist, text.as_bytes());
+        plist
+    }
+
+    /// `path` relative to the volume (the base), `/`-joined, as a `pkgutil --files` line.
+    fn receipt_line(&self, path: &Path) -> String {
+        let rel = path
+            .strip_prefix(&self.base)
+            .unwrap_or_else(|_| panic!("loopback: {} is not under the volume", path.display()));
+        rel.components()
+            .map(|c| c.as_os_str().to_string_lossy().into_owned())
+            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
+            .join("/")
+    }
+
+    /// The script of spawn `argv`, with its index recorded, its log written and its effect chosen.
+    fn script_for(&self, argv: &[OsString]) -> Script<'_> {
+        let (plan, log) = {
+            let mut st = self.state();
+            let index = st.spawns.len();
+            st.spawns.push(argv.to_vec());
+            (
+                st.plans.get(&index).copied(),
+                st.logs.remove(&index).unwrap_or_default(),
+            )
+        };
+        // An Inno setup's argv ends with its `/LOG=` argument (FR-030 step 3).
+        if let Some(log_path) = argv
+            .get(ops::INNO_FLAGS.len() + 1)
+            .and_then(|a| a.to_str())
+            .and_then(|a| a.strip_prefix(LOG_ARG))
+        {
+            let log_path = self.sandboxed(Path::new(log_path));
+            self.write(&log_path, &log);
+        }
+        match plan {
+            Some(Plan::Exit(code)) => {
+                return Script {
+                    exit: code,
+                    ..Script::default()
+                };
+            }
+            Some(Plan::NeverExits) => {
+                return Script {
+                    never_exits: true,
+                    ..Script::default()
+                };
+            }
+            None => {}
+        }
+        if argv.first().is_some_and(|a| a == ops::PKGUTIL) {
+            return self.pkgutil(argv);
+        }
+        let act = self.act_of(argv);
+        Script {
+            effect: Some(Box::new(move |_argv: &[OsString]| self.apply(&act))),
+            ..Script::default()
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// The default effect of an installer or uninstaller spawn; panics on any other argv.
+    fn act_of(&self, argv: &[OsString]) -> Act {
+        let text: Vec<&str> = argv.iter().filter_map(|a| a.to_str()).collect();
+        assert_eq!(
+            text.len(),
+            argv.len(),
+            "loopback: a non-UTF-8 argv {argv:?}"
+        );
+        let inno_install = text.len() == ops::INNO_FLAGS.len() + 2
+            && text.get(1..=ops::INNO_FLAGS.len()) == Some(&ops::INNO_FLAGS[..]);
+        let pkg_install = text.first() == Some(&ops::INSTALLER);
+        let inno_uninstall = text.len() == ops::UNINSTALL_FLAGS.len() + 1
+            && text.get(1..) == Some(&ops::UNINSTALL_FLAGS[..]);
+        if inno_install || pkg_install {
+            let copy = if inno_install {
+                text[0]
+            } else {
+                text.get(2).copied().unwrap_or_default()
+            };
+            let copy = self.sandboxed(Path::new(copy));
+            let bytes = fs::read(&copy)
+                .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("loopback cannot read {}: {e}", copy.display()));
+            let hex = digest::hex(&digest::sha256(&bytes));
+            let entry = self.state().installs.get(&hex).cloned().unwrap_or_else(|| {
+                panic!("loopback: no `installs` names the installer digest {hex}")
+            });
+            return if inno_install {
+                Act::InnoInstall(entry)
+            } else {
+                Act::PkgInstall(entry)
+            };
+        }
+        if inno_uninstall {
+            return Act::InnoUninstall(text[0].to_owned());
+        }
+        panic!("loopback: no effect for the spawn {argv:?}")
+    }
+
+    fn apply(&self, act: &Act) {
+        match act {
+            Act::InnoInstall(entry) => self.inno_install(entry),
+            Act::PkgInstall(entry) => self.pkg_install(entry),
+            Act::InnoUninstall(unins) => self.inno_uninstall(unins),
+        }
+    }
+
+    fn inno_install(&self, entry: &PluginEntry) {
+        let OsRoots::Windows(w) = &self.roots.os else {
+            panic!("loopback: an Inno install on a non-Windows loopback");
+        };
+        let facts = facts(entry);
+        let key = facts
+            .windows_uninstall_key
+            .as_ref()
+            .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("loopback: {} has no windows_uninstall_key", entry.name))
+            .as_str()
+            .to_owned();
+        let end = key
+            .find('}')
+            .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("loopback: the key {key} has no app id"));
+        let subkey = format!(
+            "{}\\{}{}",
+            ops::UNINSTALL_KEY,
+            &key[..=end],
+            ops::INNO_KEY_SUFFIX
+        );
+        let dir = w.vst3_vendor.join(&entry.name);
+        self.write_vst3(
+            &dir.join(facts.vst3_bundle.as_str()),
+            &entry.name,
+            &entry.version_text,
+        );
+        let unins = dir.join(UNINS_NAME);
+        self.write(&unins, b"");
+        let unins_text = path_text(&unins);
+        let location = format!("{}\\", path_text(&dir));
+        FakeRegistry::new(&self.world).set(
+            &subkey,
+            Some(&format!("\"{unins_text}\"")),
+            Some(&location),
+        );
+        lock(&self.host.inno).insert(
+            key.clone(),
+            RecordRead::Found(InnoValues {
+                display_version: Some(entry.version_text.clone()),
+                install_location: Some(location),
+            }),
+        );
+        self.state()
+            .inno
+            .insert(unins_text, InnoInstall { key, subkey, dir });
+    }
+
+    fn inno_uninstall(&self, unins: &str) {
+        let Some(install) = self.state().inno.remove(unins) else {
+            panic!("loopback: no install left the uninstaller {unins}");
+        };
+        self.check(&install.dir);
+        fs::remove_dir_all(&install.dir)
+            .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("loopback cannot remove {}: {e}", install.dir.display()));
+        // The key goes when the helper's poll next reads the fake clock (D6).
+        self.world
+            .at(self.world.now(), Event::RemoveKey(install.subkey));
+        lock(&self.host.inno).remove(&install.key);
+    }
+
+    fn pkg_install(&self, entry: &PluginEntry) {
+        let OsRoots::Macos(m) = &self.roots.os else {
+            panic!("loopback: a pkg install on a non-macOS loopback");
+        };
+        let facts = facts(entry);
+        let id = facts
+            .macos_pkg_id
+            .as_ref()
+            .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("loopback: {} has no macos_pkg_id", entry.name))
+            .as_str()
+            .to_owned();
+        let mut files = Vec::new();
+        let mut dirs = BTreeSet::new();
+        let mut bundles = vec![(m.vst3.clone(), m.vst3.join(facts.vst3_bundle.as_str()))];
+        if let Some(component) = &facts.au_component {
+            bundles.push((m.components.clone(), m.components.join(component.as_str())));
+        }
+        for (i, (root, bundle)) in bundles.iter().enumerate() {
+            let file = if i == 0 {
+                self.write_vst3(bundle, &entry.name, &entry.version_text)
+            } else {
+                self.write_component(bundle, &entry.version_text)
+            };
+            // pkgutil lists every directory of the payload, the structural ones above the roots included (D16).
+            for dir in file.ancestors().skip(1) {
+                if dir == self.base {
+                    break;
+                }
+                if dir.starts_with(root) || root.starts_with(dir) {
+                    dirs.insert(self.receipt_line(dir));
+                }
+            }
+            files.push(self.receipt_line(&file));
+        }
+        let lines = |set: Vec<String>| set.iter().map(|l| format!("{l}\n")).collect::<String>();
+        let receipt = Receipt {
+            info: format!(
+                "package-id: {id}\nversion: {}\nvolume: /\n",
+                entry.version_text
+            ),
+            files: lines(files),
+            dirs: lines(dirs.into_iter().collect()),
+        };
+        lock(&self.host.pkg).insert(
+            id.clone(),
+            RecordRead::Found(PkgOutputs {
+                pkg_info: receipt.info.clone(),
+                files: receipt.files.clone(),
+            }),
+        );
+        self.state().pkgs.insert(id, receipt);
+    }
+
+    /// The `pkgutil` answers (FR-033): the receipt queries, and the one other call the helper makes, which drops the
+    /// receipt and the pkg record at once.
+    fn pkgutil(&self, argv: &[OsString]) -> Script<'_> {
+        let text: Vec<&str> = argv.iter().skip(1).filter_map(|a| a.to_str()).collect();
+        let receipt = |id: &str| self.state().pkgs.get(id).cloned();
+        let answer = |out: Option<String>| match out {
+            Some(stdout) => Script {
+                stdout: stdout.into_bytes(),
+                ..Script::default()
+            },
+            None => Script {
+                exit: 1,
+                ..Script::default()
+            },
+        };
+        match text.as_slice() {
+            [PKG_INFO, id] => answer(receipt(id).map(|r| r.info)),
+            [FILES, id, ONLY_FILES] => answer(receipt(id).map(|r| r.files)),
+            [FILES, id, ONLY_DIRS] => answer(receipt(id).map(|r| r.dirs)),
+            [_, id] => {
+                let gone = self.state().pkgs.remove(*id);
+                lock(&self.host.pkg).remove(*id);
+                answer(gone.map(|_| String::new()))
+            }
+            _ => panic!("loopback: no pkgutil answer for {argv:?}"),
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+impl Elevation for Loopback<'_> {
+    fn helper(&self) -> Option<&Path> {
+        Some(&self.helper)
+    }
+
+    fn launch(&self, command: &ElevatedCommand, _timeout: Duration) -> LaunchOutcome {
+        let job = command
+            .args
+            .get(1)
+            .map(PathBuf::from)
+            .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("loopback: no job argument in {:?}", command.args));
+        let bytes = fs::read(&job)
+            .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("loopback cannot read the job {}: {e}", job.display()));
+        {
+            let mut st = self.state();
+            st.jobs.push(bytes);
+            st.launches.push(command.clone());
+        }
+        let argv: Vec<OsString> = command.args.iter().map(OsString::from).collect();
+        let Some(args) = cli::parse_args(&argv) else {
+            return LaunchOutcome::Exited(USAGE_EXIT);
+        };
+        let io = TempIo::new(&self.world, &self.base);
+        let runner = LoopbackRunner {
+            loopback: self,
+            children: Mutex::new(Vec::new()),
+        };
+        let registry = FakeRegistry::new(&self.world);
+        let seams = Seams {
+            io: &io,
+            runner: &runner,
+            registry: &registry,
+            classify: self.classify,
+        };
+        let exit = krate_manager_helper::run(&args, self.helper_os(), &self.roots, &seams);
+        LaunchOutcome::Exited(i32::from(exit.status))
+    }
+
+    fn guard(&self) -> &ElevationGuard {
+        &self.guard
+    }
+}
+
+/// The helper's `ProcessRunner` in a launch: each call is answered by a fresh `ScriptedRunner` holding the one script
+/// the loopback chose for it, so the spawn index is the loopback's own.
+struct LoopbackRunner<'l, 'a> {
+    loopback: &'l Loopback<'a>,
+    children: Mutex<Vec<(ScriptedRunner<'l>, ChildId)>>,
+}
+
+impl ProcessRunner for LoopbackRunner<'_, '_> {
+    fn spawn(&self, argv: &[OsString]) -> io::Result<ChildId> {
+        let inner = ScriptedRunner::new(&self.loopback.world);
+        inner.on(Match::Any, self.loopback.script_for(argv));
+        let child = inner.spawn(argv)?;
+        let mut children = lock(&self.children);
+        children.push((inner, child));
+        Ok(ChildId(
+            u64::try_from(children.len() - 1).unwrap_or(u64::MAX),
+        ))
+    }
+
+    fn wait(&self, child: ChildId, timeout: Duration) -> io::Result<Option<i32>> {
+        let children = lock(&self.children);
+        let (inner, id) = usize::try_from(child.0)
+            .ok()
+            .and_then(|i| children.get(i))
+            .ok_or_else(|| io::Error::from(io::ErrorKind::NotFound))?;
+        inner.wait(*id, timeout)
+    }
+
+    fn output(&self, argv: &[OsString], timeout: Duration) -> io::Result<Option<(i32, Vec<u8>)>> {
+        assert!(
+            argv.first().is_some_and(|a| a == ops::PKGUTIL),
+            "loopback: an output call that is not pkgutil: {argv:?}"
+        );
+        let inner = ScriptedRunner::new(&self.loopback.world);
+        inner.on(Match::Any, self.loopback.pkgutil(argv));
+        inner.output(argv, timeout)
+    }
+}
+
+fn lock<T>(m: &Mutex<T>) -> MutexGuard<'_, T> {
+    m.lock().unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner)
+}
+
+fn facts(entry: &PluginEntry) -> &InstallFacts {
+    entry
+        .release
+        .as_ref()
+        .and_then(|r| r.install.as_ref())
+        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("loopback: {} has no install facts", entry.name))
+}
+
+fn path_text(path: &Path) -> String {
+    path.to_str()
+        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("loopback: {} is not UTF-8", path.display()))
+        .to_owned()
+}
+
+// ---- the self-test (tasks TA-13) -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+const ALPHA: &str = "Alpha";
+const V12: &str = "1.2.0";
+/// The test classifier's locked exit and log line (the loopback's own; the production rule is M-1's).
+const TEST_LOCKED_EXIT: i32 = 99;
+const TEST_LOCKED_LINE: &str = "loopback: a file is in use";
+
+fn test_classify(code: i32, log: &[String]) -> InnoVerdict {
+    if code == 0 {
+        InnoVerdict::Installed
+    } else if code == TEST_LOCKED_EXIT && log.iter().any(|l| l.contains(TEST_LOCKED_LINE)) {
+        InnoVerdict::Locked
+    } else {
+        InnoVerdict::Failed
+    }
+}
+
+/// `bytes` staged at `<staging>/downloads/<sha hex>/<asset>`, with its feed entry; returns (entry, canonical file, hex).
+fn staged(
+    roots: &Roots,
+    key: PlatformKey,
+    asset: &str,
+    bytes: &[u8],
+) -> (PluginEntry, String, String) {
+    let sha = digest::sha256(bytes);
+    let hex = digest::hex(&sha);
+    let signature = TestSigner::new().sign_c3(bytes, ALPHA, V12, key, asset);
+    let feed = test_feed(&[TestPlugin {
+        name: ALPHA,
+        version: V12,
+        key,
+        asset,
+        size: bytes.len() as u64,
+        sha256: sha,
+        signature,
+        min_os: None,
+    }]);
+    let entry = feed.plugins.get(ALPHA).expect("the entry").clone();
+    let path = roots
+        .staging_dir()
+        .join(DOWNLOADS_DIR)
+        .join(&hex)
+        .join(asset);
+    fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().expect("a parent")).expect("the downloads dir");
+    fs::write(&path, bytes).expect("the staged artifact");
+    let file = FsInstallIo
+        .canonicalize(&path)
+        .expect("canonical staged file");
+    (entry, path_text(&file), hex)
+}
+
+/// `ops` written by `job::write` and launched through the loopback.
+fn run(lb: &Loopback<'_>, roots: &Roots, ops: Vec<JobOp>) -> (LaunchOutcome, WrittenJob) {
+    let job = job::write(&FsInstallIo, roots.state_dir(), ops).expect("job::write");
+    let command = ElevatedCommand {
+        program: lb.helper.clone(),
+        args: vec![
+            "--job".to_owned(),
+            path_text(&job.path),
+            "--job-sha256".to_owned(),
+            job.sha256.clone(),
+        ],
+    };
+    (lb.launch(&command, job::ceiling(&job.ops)), job)
+}
+
+/// `(outcome, code)` per op of the job's result file.
+fn outcomes(job: &WrittenJob) -> Vec<(String, Option<String>)> {
+    let bytes = fs::read(&job.result).expect("a result file");
+    let doc: Value = serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).expect("result JSON");
+    assert_eq!(doc["job_sha256"].as_str(), Some(job.sha256.as_str()));
+    doc["ops"]
+        .as_array()
+        .expect("ops")
+        .iter()
+        .map(|op| {
+            (
+                op["outcome"].as_str().unwrap_or_default().to_owned(),
+                op["code"].as_str().map(str::to_owned),
+            )
+        })
+        .collect()
+}
+
+fn done(n: usize) -> Vec<(String, Option<String>)> {
+    vec![("done".to_owned(), None); n]
+}
+
+/// Rule 1: every root the loopback hands the helper, the state dir included, lies under the base.
+fn assert_roots_under_base(lb: &Loopback<'_>) {
+    let mut paths: Vec<&Path> = match &lb.roots.os {
+        OsRoots::Windows(w) => vec![&w.vst3_vendor, &w.preset_vendor, &w.run_base],
+        OsRoots::Macos(m) => {
+            let mut v: Vec<&Path> = vec![
+                &m.vst3,
+                &m.components,
+                &m.presets,
+                &m.applications,
+                &m.volume,
+                &m.run_base,
+            ];
+            v.extend(m.run_ancestors.iter().map(PathBuf::as_path));
+            v
+        }
+        OsRoots::None => panic!("the smoke test runs Windows and macOS only"),
+    };
+    match &lb.roots.state_dir {
+        StateDirRule::Exact(dir) => paths.push(dir),
+        other => panic!("the loopback state dir rule is {other:?}, not Exact"),
+    }
+    for path in paths {
+        assert!(
+            path.starts_with(&lb.base),
+            "{} is not under {}",
+            path.display(),
+            lb.base.display()
+        );
+    }
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn loopback_smoke() {
+    // Windows: install, uninstall, then one batch of three spawns scripted by exit_for / serve_log / never_exits.
+    {
+        let (td, roots) = fresh_elevated_roots();
+        let host = LiveHostFacts::new(None);
+        let lb = Loopback::new(Os::Windows, &roots, td.path(), &host).classify(test_classify);
+        assert_roots_under_base(&lb);
+        let asset = "Alpha-1.2.0-Windows-x64.exe";
+        let (entry, file, hex) = staged(&roots, PlatformKey::WindowsX86_64, asset, b"an installer");
+        lb.installs(&hex, &entry);
+        let key = facts(&entry)
+            .windows_uninstall_key
+            .as_ref()
+            .expect("a key")
+            .as_str()
+            .to_owned();
+        let app_id = key[..=key.find('}').expect("an app id")].to_owned();
+        let subkey = format!("{}\\{app_id}{}", ops::UNINSTALL_KEY, ops::INNO_KEY_SUFFIX);
+        let OsRoots::Windows(w) = &lb.roots.os else {
+            panic!("Windows roots");
+        };
+        let dir = w.vst3_vendor.join(ALPHA);
+        let bundle = dir.join(facts(&entry).vst3_bundle.as_str());
+        let install = || JobOp::RunInnoInstaller {
+            file: file.clone(),
+            sha256: hex.clone(),
+        };
+
+        let (outcome, job) = run(&lb, &roots, vec![install()]);
+        assert_eq!(outcome, LaunchOutcome::Exited(0));
+        assert_eq!(outcomes(&job), done(1));
+        assert!(matches!(
+            lock(&host.inno).get(&key),
+            Some(RecordRead::Found(_))
+        ));
+        assert!(FakeRegistry::new(&lb.world).key_exists(&subkey));
+        assert!(
+            bundle
+                .join("Contents")
+                .join("Resources")
+                .join("moduleinfo.json")
+                .is_file()
+        );
+        assert!(dir.join(UNINS_NAME).is_file());
+
+        let (outcome, job) = run(
+            &lb,
+            &roots,
+            vec![JobOp::RunInnoUninstaller {
+                app_id: app_id.clone(),
+            }],
+        );
+        assert_eq!(outcome, LaunchOutcome::Exited(0));
+        assert_eq!(outcomes(&job), done(1));
+        assert!(lock(&host.inno).is_empty());
+        assert!(!FakeRegistry::new(&lb.world).key_exists(&subkey));
+        assert!(!dir.exists());
+
+        lb.exit_for(2, 1);
+        lb.exit_for(3, TEST_LOCKED_EXIT);
+        lb.serve_log(
+            3,
+            format!("line one\r\n{TEST_LOCKED_LINE}\r\n").into_bytes(),
+        );
+        lb.never_exits(4);
+        let (outcome, job) = run(&lb, &roots, vec![install(), install(), install()]);
+        assert_eq!(outcome, LaunchOutcome::Exited(0));
+        let code = |e: error::HelperError| Some(e.code.to_owned());
+        assert_eq!(
+            outcomes(&job),
+            vec![
+                ("failed".to_owned(), code(error::EXIT)),
+                ("locked".to_owned(), code(error::LOCKED)),
+                ("failed".to_owned(), code(error::TIMEOUT)),
+            ]
+        );
+        assert!(
+            lock(&host.inno).is_empty(),
+            "a scripted spawn installs nothing"
+        );
+        assert!(!bundle.exists());
+
+        let launches = lb.launches();
+        let jobs = lb.jobs();
+        assert_eq!((launches.len(), jobs.len()), (3, 3));
+        assert_eq!(jobs[2], fs::read(&job.path).expect("the job"));
+        let spawns = lb.spawns();
+        assert_eq!(spawns.len(), 5);
+        let flags: Vec<OsString> = ops::UNINSTALL_FLAGS.iter().map(OsString::from).collect();
+        assert_eq!(spawns[1].get(1..), Some(&flags[..]));
+    }
+    // macOS: install, uninstall.
+    {
+        let (td, roots) = fresh_elevated_roots();
+        let host = LiveHostFacts::new(None);
+        let lb = Loopback::new(Os::Macos, &roots, td.path(), &host);
+        assert_roots_under_base(&lb);
+        let asset = "Alpha-1.2.0-macOS.pkg";
+        let (entry, file, hex) = staged(&roots, PlatformKey::DarwinUniversal, asset, b"a pkg");
+        lb.installs(&hex, &entry);
+        let id = facts(&entry)
+            .macos_pkg_id
+            .as_ref()
+            .expect("a pkg id")
+            .as_str()
+            .to_owned();
+        let OsRoots::Macos(m) = &lb.roots.os else {
+            panic!("macOS roots");
+        };
+        let vst3 = m.vst3.join(facts(&entry).vst3_bundle.as_str());
+        let component = m.components.join(
+            facts(&entry)
+                .au_component
+                .as_ref()
+                .expect("a component")
+                .as_str(),
+        );
+
+        let (outcome, job) = run(
+            &lb,
+            &roots,
+            vec![JobOp::RunPkgInstaller { file, sha256: hex }],
+        );
+        assert_eq!(outcome, LaunchOutcome::Exited(0));
+        assert_eq!(outcomes(&job), done(1));
+        assert!(matches!(
+            lock(&host.pkg).get(&id),
+            Some(RecordRead::Found(_))
+        ));
+        assert!(
+            vst3.join("Contents")
+                .join("Resources")
+                .join("moduleinfo.json")
+                .is_file()
+        );
+        assert!(component.join("Contents").join("Info.plist").is_file());
+
+        let (outcome, job) = run(
+            &lb,
+            &roots,
+            vec![JobOp::UninstallPkg { pkg_id: id.clone() }],
+        );
+        assert_eq!(outcome, LaunchOutcome::Exited(0));
+        assert_eq!(outcomes(&job), done(1));
+        assert!(lock(&host.pkg).is_empty());
+        assert!(!vst3.exists());
+        assert!(!component.exists());
+        assert!(m.vst3.is_dir() && m.components.is_dir(), "the roots stay");
+        assert_eq!(lb.spawns().len(), 1);
+    }
+}

+// ---- Phase 10: the elevated drivers (plan §7.1; SC-003 core twin, SC-009 to SC-015, SC-022, SC-024, SC-028 to SC-030) -
+
+const GAMMA: &str = "Gamma";
+/// The test classifier's locked exit and log line (SC-003's twin; the production rule is M-1's).
+const TEST_LOCKED_EXIT: i32 = 99;
+const TEST_LOCKED_LINE: &str = "a plug-in file is in use (the test classifier's line)";
+
+/// `exit 0` installed; `TEST_LOCKED_EXIT` with `TEST_LOCKED_LINE` locked; anything else failed.
+fn locked_classify(code: i32, log: &[String]) -> InnoVerdict {
+    if code == 0 {
+        InnoVerdict::Installed
+    } else if code == TEST_LOCKED_EXIT && log.iter().any(|l| l.contains(TEST_LOCKED_LINE)) {
+        InnoVerdict::Locked
+    } else {
+        InnoVerdict::Failed
+    }
+}
+
+/// One OS the elevated drivers serve, with the architecture, feed key and running version its tests use.
+#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
+struct Plat {
+    os: Os,
+    arch: Arch,
+    key: PlatformKey,
+    running: &'static str,
+}
+
+const WIN: Plat = Plat {
+    os: Os::Windows,
+    arch: Arch::X86_64,
+    key: PlatformKey::WindowsX86_64,
+    running: "10.0.19045",
+};
+const MAC: Plat = Plat {
+    os: Os::Macos,
+    arch: Arch::Aarch64,
+    key: PlatformKey::DarwinAarch64,
+    running: "15.0",
+};
+
+impl Plat {
+    fn asset(self, name: &str, version: &str) -> String {
+        match self.os {
+            Os::Windows => format!("{name}-{version}-Windows-x64.exe"),
+            _ => format!("{name}-{version}-macOS.pkg"),
+        }
+    }
+
+    fn installer_op(self) -> &'static str {
+        match self.os {
+            Os::Windows => "run_inno_installer",
+            _ => "run_pkg_installer",
+        }
+    }
+
+    fn driver(self) -> &'static dyn InstallDriver {
+        match self.os {
+            Os::Windows => &WindowsDriver,
+            _ => &MacosDriver,
+        }
+    }
+
+    fn notice(self) -> Notice {
+        match self.os {
+            Os::Macos => Notice::RescanDawMacos,
+            _ => Notice::RescanDaw,
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+/// The installer bytes of one release (any bytes: the drivers never look inside).
+fn installer(name: &str, version: &str) -> Vec<u8> {
+    format!("the {name} {version} installer").into_bytes()
+}
+
+/// The file names left in `<state>/jobs/` (none when the folder is absent).
+fn jobs_left(state: &Path) -> Vec<String> {
+    match fs::read_dir(state.join(job::JOBS_DIR)) {
+        Ok(entries) => entries
+            .map(|e| {
+                e.expect("a jobs entry")
+                    .file_name()
+                    .to_string_lossy()
+                    .into_owned()
+            })
+            .collect(),
+        Err(_) => Vec::new(),
+    }
+}
+
+/// The installer a real setup would leave for one plugin (SC-014, SC-030): its managed VST3 bundle with a
+/// `moduleinfo.json` and its record in the shared `LiveHostFacts`. `Clone + Send` so a scripted launch can run it.
+#[derive(Clone)]
+struct Machine {
+    os: Os,
+    live: Arc<LiveHostFacts>,
+    name: String,
+    /// The Windows uninstall key or the macOS pkg id: the record's key.
+    record_key: String,
+    install_dir: PathBuf,
+    bundle: PathBuf,
+}
+
+impl Machine {
+    fn place(&self, version: &str) {
+        let info = self
+            .bundle
+            .join("Contents")
+            .join("Resources")
+            .join("moduleinfo.json");
+        fs::create_dir_all(info.parent().expect("a parent")).expect("the bundle dirs");
+        fs::write(&info, moduleinfo_for(&self.name, version)).expect("the moduleinfo");
+    }
+
+    fn record(&self, version: &str) {
+        match self.os {
+            Os::Windows => {
+                let location = format!("{}{}", text(&self.install_dir), std::path::MAIN_SEPARATOR);
+                self.live
+                    .inno
+                    .lock()
+                    .unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner)
+                    .insert(
+                        self.record_key.clone(),
+                        detect::RecordRead::Found(detect::InnoValues {
+                            display_version: Some(version.to_owned()),
+                            install_location: Some(location),
+                        }),
+                    );
+            }
+            _ => {
+                self.live
+                    .pkg
+                    .lock()
+                    .unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner)
+                    .insert(
+                        self.record_key.clone(),
+                        detect::RecordRead::Found(detect::PkgOutputs {
+                            pkg_info: format!(
+                                "package-id: {}\nversion: {version}\n",
+                                self.record_key
+                            ),
+                            files: format!("{}.vst3\n", self.name),
+                        }),
+                    );
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    fn install(&self, version: &str) {
+        self.place(version);
+        self.record(version);
+    }
+
+    fn unplace(&self) {
+        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&self.bundle);
+    }
+
+    fn unrecord(&self) {
+        self.live
+            .inno
+            .lock()
+            .unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner)
+            .remove(&self.record_key);
+        self.live
+            .pkg
+            .lock()
+            .unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner)
+            .remove(&self.record_key);
+    }
+
+    fn uninstall(&self) {
+        self.unplace();
+        self.unrecord();
+    }
+}
+
+/// Phase 10's rig: `Fx` over `fresh_elevated_roots()` (state dir `<base>/<APP_IDENTIFIER>`, SI-21), one OS, and
+/// the `LiveHostFacts` every detection of the test reads.
+struct Ev {
+    fx: Fx,
+    plat: Plat,
+    live: Arc<LiveHostFacts>,
+}
+
+impl Ev {
+    fn new(plat: Plat) -> Ev {
+        let (td, roots) = fresh_elevated_roots();
+        Ev::over(Fx::over(td, roots, None), plat)
+    }
+
+    fn over(fx: Fx, plat: Plat) -> Ev {
+        Ev {
+            fx,
+            plat,
+            live: Arc::new(LiveHostFacts::new(OsVersion::parse(plat.running))),
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// A contract-valid feed of `releases` ((name, version)), one signed `plat` installer asset each.
+    fn doc_for(&self, plat: Plat, releases: &[(&str, &str)]) -> Vec<u8> {
+        let assets: Vec<String> = releases.iter().map(|&(n, v)| plat.asset(n, v)).collect();
+        let bodies: Vec<Vec<u8>> = releases.iter().map(|&(n, v)| installer(n, v)).collect();
+        let plugins: Vec<crate::test_support::TestPlugin<'_>> = releases
+            .iter()
+            .zip(&assets)
+            .zip(&bodies)
+            .map(
+                |((&(name, version), asset), body)| crate::test_support::TestPlugin {
+                    name,
+                    version,
+                    key: plat.key,
+                    asset,
+                    size: body.len() as u64,
+                    sha256: sha(body),
+                    signature: self.fx.signer.sign_c3(body, name, version, plat.key, asset),
+                    min_os: None,
+                },
+            )
+            .collect();
+        test_feed_document(&plugins)
+    }
+
+    /// Serves `releases` as the feed and each installer at the route its URL names.
+    fn offer(&self, releases: &[(&str, &str)]) {
+        self.fx.server.set_route(
+            FEED_ROUTE,
+            Reply::Ok {
+                body: self.doc_for(self.plat, releases),
+                content_type: "application/json",
+            },
+        );
+        for &(name, version) in releases {
+            let route = format!(
+                "/{}/v{version}/{}",
+                name.to_ascii_lowercase(),
+                self.plat.asset(name, version)
+            );
+            self.fx.server.set_route(
+                &route,
+                Reply::Ranged {
+                    body: installer(name, version),
+                },
+            );
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// Each release's entry and a real `VerifiedArtifact` at `<staging>/downloads/<sha hex>/<asset>` (SI-20), for
+    /// `plat`'s asset format.
+    fn staged_as(
+        &self,
+        plat: Plat,
+        releases: &[(&str, &str)],
+    ) -> Vec<(PluginEntry, VerifiedArtifact)> {
+        let doc = self.doc_for(plat, releases);
+        releases
+            .iter()
+            .map(|&(name, version)| {
+                let entry = Fx::entry_in(&doc, name);
+                let body = installer(name, version);
+                let platform = entry
+                    .release
+                    .as_ref()
+                    .and_then(|r| r.platforms.get(&plat.key))
+                    .expect("the asset")
+                    .clone();
+                let dir = self
+                    .fx
+                    .ctx()
+                    .roots
+                    .staging_dir()
+                    .join(DOWNLOADS_DIR)
+                    .join(hex_lower(&sha(&body)));
+                fs::create_dir_all(&dir).expect("the staged dir");
+                let path = dir.join(platform.asset_name.as_str());
+                fs::write(&path, &body).expect("stage the installer");
+                let expected = Expected {
+                    name: &entry.name,
+                    version: &entry.version_text,
+                    platform: plat.key,
+                    asset: &platform.asset_name,
+                    format: platform.format,
+                    size: platform.size,
+                    sha256: platform.sha256,
+                    signature: &platform.signature,
+                };
+                let artifact = verify_file(&path, &expected, &self.fx.signer.key())
+                    .expect("the staged installer verifies");
+                (entry, artifact)
+            })
+            .collect()
+    }
+
+    fn staged(&self, releases: &[(&str, &str)]) -> Vec<(PluginEntry, VerifiedArtifact)> {
+        self.staged_as(self.plat, releases)
+    }
+
+    fn entry(&self, name: &str, version: &str) -> PluginEntry {
+        Fx::entry_in(&self.doc_for(self.plat, &[(name, version)]), name)
+    }
+
+    /// The installer state of `entry`'s plugin on this OS (the managed VST3 copy detection reads).
+    fn machine(&self, entry: &PluginEntry) -> Machine {
+        let facts = entry
+            .release
+            .as_ref()
+            .and_then(|r| r.install.as_ref())
+            .expect("install facts");
+        let record_key = match self.plat.os {
+            Os::Windows => facts.windows_uninstall_key.as_ref(),
+            _ => facts.macos_pkg_id.as_ref(),
+        }
+        .expect("a record key")
+        .as_str()
+        .to_owned();
+        let global = self.fx.ctx().roots.vst3_global().to_path_buf();
+        let install_dir = global.join(platform::VENDOR_DIR).join(&entry.name);
+        let bundle = match self.plat.os {
+            Os::Windows => install_dir.join(facts.vst3_bundle.as_str()),
+            _ => global.join(facts.vst3_bundle.as_str()),
+        };
+        Machine {
+            os: self.plat.os,
+            live: Arc::clone(&self.live),
+            name: entry.name.clone(),
+            record_key,
+            install_dir,
+            bundle,
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// One run of this OS's driver.
+    fn drive(
+        &self,
+        elevation: &dyn Elevation,
+        io: &dyn InstallIo,
+        batch: Vec<Action>,
+        progress: &mut dyn FnMut(InstallProgress),
+    ) -> Vec<ActionResult> {
+        self.drive_with(elevation, io, &*self.live, batch, progress)
+    }
+
+    fn drive_with(
+        &self,
+        elevation: &dyn Elevation,
+        io: &dyn InstallIo,
+        host: &dyn HostFacts,
+        batch: Vec<Action>,
+        progress: &mut dyn FnMut(InstallProgress),
+    ) -> Vec<ActionResult> {

+                extract: self.fx.rig.extract_env(&FsExtractIo),
+                progress,
+                elevation,
+                host_io: &self.fx.rig.host_io,
+                host,
+            },
+            batch,
+        )
+    }
+
+    /// The commands' `CommandEnv` for this OS with `elevation`, the rig's `host_io` and the fixture seam.
+    fn env<'a>(
+        &'a self,
+        io: &'a dyn InstallIo,
+        elevation: &'a dyn Elevation,
+        seam: &'a Seam<'a>,
+    ) -> CommandEnv<'a> {

+            io,
+            &FsExtractIo,
+            &NativeDrivers,
+            (elevation, &self.fx.rig.host_io),
+        );
+        env.request_seam = Some(seam);
+        env
+    }
+
+    fn jobs_dir(&self) -> PathBuf {
+        self.fx.state().join(job::JOBS_DIR)
+    }
+
+    fn jobs_left(&self) -> Vec<String> {
+        jobs_left(&self.fx.state())
+    }
+
+    /// How often the rig's `host_io` ran `platform::KILLALL_ARGV` (FR-077).
+    fn killalls(&self) -> usize {
+        let want = format!("run {}", platform::KILLALL_ARGV.join(" "));
+        self.fx
+            .rig
+            .host_io
+            .lock()
+            .unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner)
+            .calls
+            .borrow()
+            .iter()
+            .filter(|c| **c == want)
+            .count()
+    }
+
+    /// Every `host_io` call of the rig.
+    fn host_calls(&self) -> Vec<String> {
+        self.fx
+            .rig
+            .host_io
+            .lock()
+            .unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner)
+            .calls
+            .borrow()
+            .clone()
+    }

+    fn view(&self) -> status::StatusView {

+            self.plat.os,
