+//! The four operations and the batch runner (plan §4.1.10; FR-030 to FR-036, FR-040 to FR-043; PD-17, PD-24).
+
+use crate::HelperOs;
+use crate::classify::InnoVerdict;
+use crate::digest::Sha256Stream;
+use crate::error::{self, HelperError};
+use crate::job::Op;
+use crate::result::{self, OpEntry, OpOutcome};
+use crate::roots::{CanonicalRoots, OsRoots};
+use crate::seams::{HelperIo, Seams, WriteSync};
+use crate::validate::{self, JobPlace, LstatFacts};
+use std::ffi::OsString;
+use std::io::{self, Read};
+use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
+use std::time::Duration;
+
+/// FR-030 step 4 (D14).
+pub const INNO_INSTALL_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(600);
+/// FR-032 (D14).
+pub const PKG_INSTALL_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(600);
+/// D6, PD-24: the uninstaller's own wait and the poll bound.
+pub const UNINSTALL_POLL_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(120);
+/// D6.
+pub const UNINSTALL_POLL_INTERVAL: Duration = Duration::from_millis(500);
+/// FR-033.
+pub const PKGUTIL_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(30);
+/// FR-030 step 3.
+pub const INNO_FLAGS: [&str; 4] = [
+    "/VERYSILENT",
+    "/SUPPRESSMSGBOXES",
+    "/NORESTART",
+    "/NOCLOSEAPPLICATIONS",
+];
+/// FR-031.
+pub const UNINSTALL_FLAGS: [&str; 3] = ["/VERYSILENT", "/SUPPRESSMSGBOXES", "/NORESTART"];
+/// FR-032.
+pub const INSTALLER: &str = "/usr/sbin/installer";
+/// FR-033.
+pub const PKGUTIL: &str = "/usr/sbin/pkgutil";
+/// FR-031: the HKLM parent of every Inno uninstall key.
+pub const UNINSTALL_KEY: &str = "SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows\\CurrentVersion\\Uninstall";
+/// FR-031: the suffix Inno gives its uninstall key.
+pub const INNO_KEY_SUFFIX: &str = "_is1";
+
+/// FR-035: the copy's chunk size.
+const CHUNK: usize = 64 * 1024;
+/// SI-28: the installer log is read from its last MiB.
+const LOG_TAIL_BYTES: u64 = 1024 * 1024;
+/// PD-19: a name collision on the run directory is retried up to four times.
+const RUN_DIR_ATTEMPTS: usize = 5;
+
+/// What every op of one batch borrows.
+pub struct OpCtx<'a> {
+    pub os: HelperOs,
+    pub roots: &'a CanonicalRoots,
+    pub place: &'a JobPlace,
+}
+
+/// FR-035: 64 KiB chunks, the digest over exactly the bytes written, `sync_all` before returning. The source is opened
+/// through `HelperIo::open_source` (PD-6: no final link, no blocking, a regular file), failing `E_HELPER_SOURCE`; a read
+/// or write error removes the partial copy and is `E_HELPER_COPY`.
+pub fn copy_hashed(io: &dyn HelperIo, src: &Path, dest: &Path) -> Result<[u8; 32], HelperError> {
+    let mut reader = io.open_source(src).map_err(|_| error::SOURCE)?;
+    let mut out = io.create_new(dest).map_err(|_| error::COPY)?;
+    let copied = copy_into(&mut *reader, &mut *out);
+    drop(out);
+    drop(reader);
+    copied.map_err(|_| {
+        // The partial copy is the helper's own file in the admin-only run directory; a failed removal is left to
+        // the run directory's cleanup (FR-043).
+        let _ = io.remove_file(dest);
+        error::COPY
+    })
+}
+
+/// The copy loop of `copy_hashed`: every chunk written, then hashed, then the destination synced.
+fn copy_into(reader: &mut dyn Read, out: &mut dyn WriteSync) -> io::Result<[u8; 32]> {
+    let mut buf = vec![0u8; CHUNK];
+    let mut digest = Sha256Stream::new();
+    loop {
+        let n = match reader.read(&mut buf) {
+            Ok(0) => break,
+            Ok(n) => n,
+            Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::Interrupted => continue,
+            Err(e) => return Err(e),
+        };
+        out.write_all(&buf[..n])?;
+        digest.update(&buf[..n]);
+    }
+    out.sync_all()?;
+    Ok(digest.finish())
+}
+
+/// One op's outcome before its index, kind and time are added.
+struct Step {
+    outcome: OpOutcome,
+    code: Option<&'static str>,
+    exit_code: Option<i32>,
+    log_tail: Vec<String>,
+    /// FR-042: an installer still running at its timeout stops the batch.
+    stops: bool,
+}
+
+impl Step {
+    fn of(outcome: OpOutcome, e: HelperError) -> Step {
+        Step {
+            outcome,
+            code: Some(e.code),
+            exit_code: None,
+            log_tail: Vec::new(),
+            stops: false,
+        }
+    }
+
+    fn done(exit_code: Option<i32>) -> Step {
+        Step {
+            outcome: OpOutcome::Done,
+            code: None,
+            exit_code,
+            log_tail: Vec::new(),
+            stops: false,
+        }
+    }
+
+    fn failed(e: HelperError) -> Step {
+        Step::of(OpOutcome::Failed, e)
+    }
+
+    fn refused(e: HelperError) -> Step {
+        Step::of(OpOutcome::Refused, e)
+    }
+
+    fn exited(mut self, code: i32) -> Step {
+        self.exit_code = Some(code);
+        self
+    }
+}
+
+/// The job's one run directory (D7), created lazily before its first installer op.
+enum RunDir {
+    NotYet,
+    Ready(PathBuf),
+    /// Creation or the ancestor check failed; the path when the directory itself was created.
+    Failed(Option<PathBuf>),
+}
+
+/// The ops strictly in order, one entry each (FR-040 to FR-043).
+pub fn run_batch(ops: &[Op], ctx: &OpCtx<'_>, seams: &Seams<'_>) -> Vec<OpEntry> {
+    let io = seams.io;
+    let mut run_dir = RunDir::NotYet;
+    let mut stopped = false;
+    let mut entries = Vec::with_capacity(ops.len());
+    for (i, op) in ops.iter().enumerate() {
+        let index = u32::try_from(i).unwrap_or(u32::MAX);
+        if stopped {
+            entries.push(entry(
+                index,
+                op,
+                Step::of(OpOutcome::NotRun, error::AFTER_TIMEOUT),
+                0.0,
+            ));
+            continue;
+        }
+        let start = io.now();
+        let step = match op {
+            Op::RunInnoInstaller { file, sha256 } | Op::RunPkgInstaller { file, sha256 } => {
+                if matches!(run_dir, RunDir::NotYet) {
+                    run_dir = create_run_dir(ctx, io);
+                }
+                match &run_dir {
+                    RunDir::Ready(run) => installer(i, op, file, sha256, run, ctx, seams),
+                    _ => Step::failed(error::RUN_DIR),
+                }
+            }
+            Op::RunInnoUninstaller { app_id } => inno_uninstaller(app_id, ctx, seams),
+            Op::UninstallPkg { pkg_id } => uninstall_pkg(pkg_id, ctx, seams),
+        };
+        let seconds = io.now().saturating_sub(start).as_secs_f64();
+        stopped = step.stops;
+        entries.push(entry(index, op, step, seconds));
+    }
+    remove_run_dir(io, run_dir);
+    entries
+}
+
+fn entry(index: u32, op: &Op, step: Step, seconds: f64) -> OpEntry {
+    OpEntry {
+        index,
+        op: op.kind(),
+        outcome: step.outcome,
+        code: step.code,
+        exit_code: step.exit_code,
+        seconds,
+        log_tail: step.log_tail,
+    }
+}
+
+/// `run-<random_hex>` under `run_base` (D7); an existing name is retried with a new one (PD-19), never reused.
+fn fresh_run_dir(io: &dyn HelperIo, run_base: &Path) -> Option<PathBuf> {
+    for _ in 0..RUN_DIR_ATTEMPTS {
+        let run = run_base.join(format!("run-{}", io.random_hex()));
+        match io.create_run_dir(&run) {
+            Ok(()) => return Some(run),
+            Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::AlreadyExists => {}
+            Err(_) => return None,
+        }
+    }
+    None
+}
+
+/// D7: Windows creates the run directory with its DACL (the seam's job); macOS creates the helper folder when absent,
+/// then the run directory, then checks `run_ancestors` + `run_base` + the new directory (PD-5, PD-26).
+fn create_run_dir(ctx: &OpCtx<'_>, io: &dyn HelperIo) -> RunDir {
+    match (ctx.os, &ctx.roots.roots.os) {
+        (HelperOs::Windows, OsRoots::Windows(w)) => match fresh_run_dir(io, &w.run_base) {
+            Some(run) => RunDir::Ready(run),
+            None => RunDir::Failed(None),
+        },
+        (HelperOs::Macos, OsRoots::Macos(m)) => {
+            if io.create_helper_folder(&m.run_base).is_err() {
+                return RunDir::Failed(None);
+            }
+            let Some(run) = fresh_run_dir(io, &m.run_base) else {
+                return RunDir::Failed(None);
+            };
+            let chain: Option<Vec<LstatFacts>> = m
+                .run_ancestors
+                .iter()
+                .chain([&m.run_base, &run])
+                .map(|p| io.lstat(p).ok().flatten())
+                .collect();
+            match chain.map(|facts| validate::run_dir_ancestors_ok(&facts)) {
+                Some(Ok(())) => RunDir::Ready(run),
+                _ => RunDir::Failed(Some(run)),
+            }
+        }
+        _ => RunDir::Failed(None),
+    }
+}
+
+/// FR-043: every entry of the run directory, then the directory; failures are ignored. A directory whose ancestor
+/// check failed is only `remove_dir`ed: it may sit under a path someone else controls, so it is never listed.
+fn remove_run_dir(io: &dyn HelperIo, run_dir: RunDir) {
+    match run_dir {
+        RunDir::Ready(run) => {
+            for name in io.list_dir(&run).unwrap_or_default() {
+                let _ = io.remove_file(&run.join(name));
+            }
+            let _ = io.remove_dir(&run);
+        }
+        RunDir::Failed(Some(run)) => {
+            let _ = io.remove_dir(&run);
+        }
+        RunDir::Failed(None) | RunDir::NotYet => {}
+    }
+}
+
+/// FR-030, FR-032: validate, copy with the hash, spawn the copy, wait, map the outcome.
+fn installer(
+    i: usize,
+    op: &Op,
+    file: &str,
+    sha256: &[u8; 32],
+    run: &Path,
+    ctx: &OpCtx<'_>,
+    seams: &Seams<'_>,
+) -> Step {
+    let io = seams.io;
+    let ext = match op {
+        Op::RunInnoInstaller { .. } => "exe",
+        _ => "pkg",
+    };
+    let src = match validate::source(io, file, ctx.place, ctx.os, ext) {
+        Ok(src) => src,
+        Err(e) => return Step::refused(e),
+    };
+    let copy = run.join(format!("installer-{i}.{ext}"));
+    match copy_hashed(io, &src, &copy) {
+        Ok(digest) if digest == *sha256 => {}
+        Ok(_) => {
+            let _ = io.remove_file(&copy);
+            return Step::failed(error::HASH);
+        }
+        // FR-020: a source the no-follow open refuses is a source refusal like the lstat check's.
+        Err(e) if e == error::SOURCE => return Step::refused(e),
+        Err(e) => return Step::failed(e),
+    }
+    let (argv, timeout, log) = match op {
+        Op::RunInnoInstaller { .. } => {
+            let log = run.join(format!("inno-{i}.log"));
+            let mut log_arg = OsString::from("/LOG=");
+            log_arg.push(log.as_os_str());
+            let mut argv = vec![copy.into_os_string()];
+            argv.extend(INNO_FLAGS.iter().map(OsString::from));
+            argv.push(log_arg);
+            (argv, INNO_INSTALL_TIMEOUT, Some(log))
+        }
+        _ => {
+            let argv = vec![
+                OsString::from(INSTALLER),
+                OsString::from("-pkg"),
+                copy.into_os_string(),
+                OsString::from("-target"),
+                OsString::from("/"),
+            ];
+            (argv, PKG_INSTALL_TIMEOUT, None)
+        }
+    };
+    let Ok(child) = seams.runner.spawn(&argv) else {
+        return Step::failed(error::SPAWN);
+    };
+    // FR-042: never killed; a wait error is treated as a child that may still run, so the batch stops too.
+    let Ok(Some(code)) = seams.runner.wait(child, timeout) else {
+        return Step {
+            stops: true,
+            ..Step::failed(error::TIMEOUT)
+        };
+    };
+    let Some(log) = log else {
+        return if code == 0 {
+            Step::done(Some(code))
+        } else {
+            Step::failed(error::EXIT).exited(code)
+        };
+    };
+    // SI-28: the last MiB, every line of it to the classifier, the last 40 to the result. A missing log reads empty.
+    let bytes = io.read_tail(&log, LOG_TAIL_BYTES).unwrap_or_default();
+    let mut step = match (seams.classify)(code, &result::log_lines(&bytes)) {
+        InnoVerdict::Installed => Step::done(Some(code)),
+        InnoVerdict::Locked => Step::of(OpOutcome::Locked, error::LOCKED).exited(code),
+        InnoVerdict::Failed => Step::failed(error::EXIT).exited(code),
+    };
+    step.log_tail = result::log_tail(&bytes);
+    step
+}
+
+/// FR-031, D6, PD-24.
+fn inno_uninstaller(app_id: &str, ctx: &OpCtx<'_>, seams: &Seams<'_>) -> Step {
+    let io = seams.io;
+    if !validate::is_app_id(app_id) {
+        return Step::refused(error::APP_ID);
+    }
+    let OsRoots::Windows(roots) = &ctx.roots.roots.os else {
+        return Step::refused(error::UNINSTALLER);
+    };
+    let subkey = format!("{UNINSTALL_KEY}\\{app_id}{INNO_KEY_SUFFIX}");
+    let Some(values) = seams.registry.uninstall_values(&subkey) else {
+        return Step::failed(error::NO_KEY);
+    };
+    let unins = match validate::uninstaller(io, roots, values) {
+        Ok(unins) => unins,
+        Err(e) => return Step::refused(e),
+    };
+    // FR-027: `exe` is the registry's own (unquoted) string, whose canonical form passed FR-031.
+    let mut argv = vec![unins.exe.clone().into_os_string()];
+    argv.extend(UNINSTALL_FLAGS.iter().map(OsString::from));
+    let Ok(child) = seams.runner.spawn(&argv) else {
+        return Step::failed(error::SPAWN);
+    };
+    match seams.runner.wait(child, UNINSTALL_POLL_TIMEOUT) {
+        Ok(Some(0)) => {}
+        Ok(Some(code)) => return Step::failed(error::EXIT).exited(code),
+        Ok(None) | Err(_) => return Step::failed(error::UNINSTALL_TIMEOUT),
+    }
+    let poll_start = io.now();
+    loop {
+        // All three are read on every check, so each check is one `key_exists` (SC-004 counts them).
+        let exe_gone = matches!(io.lstat(&unins.exe), Ok(None));
+        let no_bundle = no_vst3_entry(io, &unins.install_dir);
+        let key_gone = !seams.registry.key_exists(&subkey);
+        if exe_gone && no_bundle && key_gone {
+            return Step::done(Some(0));
+        }
+        if io.now().saturating_sub(poll_start) >= UNINSTALL_POLL_TIMEOUT {
+            return Step::failed(error::UNINSTALL_TIMEOUT).exited(0);
+        }
+        io.sleep(UNINSTALL_POLL_INTERVAL);
+    }
+}
+
+/// D6: no entry of `dir` ends in `.vst3` (ASCII case-insensitive); an absent directory has none, any other listing
+/// error counts as "still there".
+fn no_vst3_entry(io: &dyn HelperIo, dir: &Path) -> bool {
+    match io.list_dir(dir) {
+        Ok(names) => !names
+            .iter()
+            .any(|n| n.to_string_lossy().to_ascii_lowercase().ends_with(".vst3")),
+        Err(e) => e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound,
+    }
+}
+
+/// One bounded `pkgutil` run: its stdout on exit 0; `E_HELPER_SPAWN` when it cannot be started (plan SI-12);
+/// `otherwise` on another exit or the timeout.
+fn pkgutil(
+    seams: &Seams<'_>,
+    args: &[&str],
+    otherwise: HelperError,
+) -> Result<Vec<u8>, HelperError> {
+    let argv: Vec<OsString> = std::iter::once(PKGUTIL)
+        .chain(args.iter().copied())
+        .map(OsString::from)
+        .collect();
+    match seams.runner.output(&argv, PKGUTIL_TIMEOUT) {
+        Ok(Some((0, stdout))) => Ok(stdout),
+        Ok(_) => Err(otherwise),
+        Err(_) => Err(error::SPAWN),
+    }
+}
+
+/// FR-033: the receipt's `location` and its two listings (files, dirs).
+fn read_receipt(
+    seams: &Seams<'_>,
+    pkg_id: &str,
+) -> Result<(String, Vec<u8>, Vec<u8>), HelperError> {
+    let info = pkgutil(seams, &["--pkg-info", pkg_id], error::PKG_RECEIPT)?;
+    let location = receipt_location(&info, pkg_id).ok_or(error::PKG_RECEIPT)?;
+    let files = pkgutil(
+        seams,
+        &["--files", pkg_id, "--only-files"],
+        error::PKG_RECEIPT,
+    )?;
+    let dirs = pkgutil(
+        seams,
+        &["--files", pkg_id, "--only-dirs"],
+        error::PKG_RECEIPT,
+    )?;
+    Ok((location, files, dirs))
+}
+
+/// `--pkg-info`'s `location` when its `package-id` is `pkg_id` (absent location = empty, FR-033).
+fn receipt_location(info: &[u8], pkg_id: &str) -> Option<String> {
+    let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(info);
+    let mut id = None;
+    let mut location = String::new();
+    for line in text.lines() {
+        if let Some(v) = line.strip_prefix("package-id:") {
+            id = Some(v.trim().to_owned());
+        } else if let Some(v) = line.strip_prefix("location:") {
+            v.trim().clone_into(&mut location);
+        }
+    }
+    (id.as_deref() == Some(pkg_id)).then_some(location)
+}
+
+/// FR-033, FR-034: read the receipt, validate every line, remove, then forget only when every file is gone.
+fn uninstall_pkg(pkg_id: &str, ctx: &OpCtx<'_>, seams: &Seams<'_>) -> Step {
+    let io = seams.io;
+    if !validate::is_pkg_id(pkg_id) {
+        return Step::refused(error::PKG_ID);
+    }
+    let OsRoots::Macos(roots) = &ctx.roots.roots.os else {
+        return Step::refused(error::PKG_PATH);
+    };
+    let (location, files, dirs) = match read_receipt(seams, pkg_id) {
+        Ok(receipt) => receipt,
+        Err(e) => return Step::failed(e),
+    };
+    let plan = match validate::pkg_plan(io, roots, &location, &files, &dirs) {
+        Ok(plan) => plan,
+        Err(e) => return Step::refused(e),
+    };
+    for file in &plan.files {
+        // A link is removed as the link; an absent entry counts as removed (checked below).
+        let _ = io.remove_file(file);
+    }
+    for dir in &plan.dirs {
+        // Deepest first; a non-empty directory is left.
+        let _ = io.remove_dir(dir);
+    }
+    if plan.files.iter().any(|f| !matches!(io.lstat(f), Ok(None))) {
+        return Step::failed(error::REMOVE);
+    }
+    match pkgutil(seams, &["--forget", pkg_id], error::FORGET) {
+        Ok(_) => Step::done(None),
+        Err(e) => Step::failed(e),
+    }
+}
+

+//! `ops::tests` (owner T006; plan §7.1 SC-002 to SC-006, SC-027): the four ops, the batch runner and the fake
+//! sandbox, over `TempDir` roots through `seams::fake` with an explicit `HelperOs` (never the real system).
+
+use super::{
+    INNO_INSTALL_TIMEOUT, INNO_KEY_SUFFIX, OpCtx, PKGUTIL, UNINSTALL_KEY, UNINSTALL_POLL_INTERVAL,
+    UNINSTALL_POLL_TIMEOUT, run_batch,
+};
+use crate::classify::{InnoVerdict, classify_inno};
+use crate::cli::Args;
+use crate::digest;
+use crate::job::Op;
+use crate::result::{OpEntry, OpOutcome};
+use crate::roots::{
+    AllowedRoots, CanonicalRoots, HELPER_FOLDER, MacosRoots, OsRoots, STATE_DIR_NAME, StateDirRule,
+    WindowsRoots,
+};
+use crate::seams::fake::{
+    Call, Event, FakeRegistry, Fault, Hook, Match, Script, ScriptedRunner, TempIo, World,
+};
+use crate::seams::{HelperIo, Seams};
+use crate::validate::JobPlace;

+use serde_json::{Value, json};
+use std::any::Any;
+use std::cell::RefCell;
+use std::ffi::OsString;
+use std::fs;
+use std::io;
+use std::panic::{AssertUnwindSafe, catch_unwind};
+use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
+use std::sync::Mutex;
+use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
+use tempfile::TempDir;
+
+const APP_A: &str = "{00000001-0000-4000-8000-000000000001}";
+const APP_B: &str = "{00000002-0000-4000-8000-000000000002}";
+const PKG_A: &str = "com.krateaudio.alpha";
+const STEM: &str = "job-0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef";
+
+/// A temp tree with a state dir, staging, the Windows roots and a macOS volume, every path canonical.
+struct Rig {
+    _dir: TempDir,
+    base: PathBuf,
+    state: PathBuf,
+    staging: PathBuf,
+    win: WindowsRoots,
+    mac: MacosRoots,
+}
+
+fn mkdirs(p: &Path) {
+    fs::create_dir_all(p).expect("create a temp directory");
+}
+
+/// One Inno install as the registry and the disk show it.
+struct Unins {
+    subkey: String,
+    install_dir: PathBuf,
+    bundle: PathBuf,
+    exe: PathBuf,
+}
+
+impl Rig {
+    fn new() -> Rig {
+        let dir = TempDir::new().expect("temp dir");
+        let base = fs::canonicalize(dir.path()).expect("canonical temp dir");
+        let state = base.join(STATE_DIR_NAME);
+        let staging = state.join("staging");
+        mkdirs(&state.join("jobs"));
+        mkdirs(&staging.join("downloads"));
+        let win = WindowsRoots {
+            vst3_vendor: base.join("common").join("VST3").join("Krate Audio"),
+            preset_vendor: base.join("data").join("Krate Audio"),
+            run_base: base.join("wintemp"),
+        };
+        let vol = base.join("vol");
+        let library = vol.join("Library");
+        let support = library.join("Application Support");
+        let plugins = library.join("Audio").join("Plug-Ins");
+        let mac = MacosRoots {
+            vst3: plugins.join("VST3"),
+            components: plugins.join("Components"),
+            presets: support.join("Krate Audio"),
+            applications: vol.join("Applications"),
+            volume: vol.clone(),
+            run_base: support.join(HELPER_FOLDER),
+            run_ancestors: vec![library, support],
+        };
+        for p in [&win.vst3_vendor, &win.preset_vendor, &win.run_base] {
+            mkdirs(p);
+        }
+        for p in [
+            &mac.vst3,
+            &mac.components,
+            &mac.presets,
+            &mac.applications,
+            &mac.run_base,
+        ] {
+            mkdirs(p);
+        }
+        Rig {
+            _dir: dir,
+            base,
+            state,
+            staging,
+            win,
+            mac,
+        }
+    }
+
+    fn place(&self) -> JobPlace {
+        JobPlace {
+            stem: STEM.to_owned(),
+            result_path: self.state.join("jobs").join(format!("{STEM}.result.json")),
+            staging_root: self.staging.clone(),
+        }
+    }
+
+    fn roots(&self, os: HelperOs) -> AllowedRoots {
+        AllowedRoots {
+            os: match os {
+                HelperOs::Windows => OsRoots::Windows(self.win.clone()),
+                HelperOs::Macos => OsRoots::Macos(self.mac.clone()),
+                HelperOs::Linux => OsRoots::None,
+            },
+            state_dir: StateDirRule::Exact(self.state.clone()),
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// The macOS run-directory chain marked root-owned (PD-5): both ancestors and the helper folder.
+    fn root_own(&self, world: &World) {
+        for d in self.mac.run_ancestors.iter().chain([&self.mac.run_base]) {
+            world.root_owned(d);
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// A staged artifact under `<staging>/downloads/`: its canonical path string and its SHA-256.
+    fn stage(&self, name: &str, bytes: &[u8]) -> (String, [u8; 32]) {
+        let p = self.staging.join("downloads").join(name);
+        fs::write(&p, bytes).expect("stage an artifact");
+        (
+            p.to_str().expect("UTF-8 temp path").to_owned(),
+            digest::sha256(bytes),
+        )
+    }
+
+    fn inno(&self, name: &str, bytes: &[u8]) -> Op {
+        let (file, sha256) = self.stage(name, bytes);
+        Op::RunInnoInstaller { file, sha256 }
+    }
+
+    fn pkg(&self, name: &str, bytes: &[u8]) -> Op {
+        let (file, sha256) = self.stage(name, bytes);
+        Op::RunPkgInstaller { file, sha256 }
+    }
+
+    /// An Inno install of `plugin` under the vendor root, with its uninstaller and its registry key.
+    fn inno_install(&self, registry: &FakeRegistry<'_>, app_id: &str, plugin: &str) -> Unins {
+        let install_dir = self.win.vst3_vendor.join(plugin);
+        let bundle = install_dir.join(format!("{plugin}.vst3"));
+        mkdirs(&bundle);
+        let exe = install_dir.join("unins000.exe");
+        fs::write(&exe, b"uninstaller").expect("write the uninstaller");
+        let subkey = format!("{UNINSTALL_KEY}\\{app_id}{INNO_KEY_SUFFIX}");
+        let quoted = format!("\"{}\"", exe.to_str().expect("UTF-8 temp path"));
+        registry.set(
+            &subkey,
+            Some(&quoted),
+            Some(install_dir.to_str().expect("UTF-8 temp path")),
+        );
+        Unins {
+            subkey,
+            install_dir,
+            bundle,
+            exe,
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// `rel` (a receipt line) under the macOS volume.
+    fn on_vol(&self, rel: &str) -> PathBuf {
+        rel.split('/')
+            .fold(self.mac.volume.clone(), |p, segment| p.join(segment))
+    }
+
+    /// Creates the receipt's directories and files and returns `(--only-files, --only-dirs)` stdout.
+    fn receipt(&self, dirs: &[&str], files: &[&str]) -> (Vec<u8>, Vec<u8>) {
+        for d in dirs {
+            mkdirs(&self.on_vol(d));
+        }
+        for f in files {
+            fs::write(self.on_vol(f), b"receipt file").expect("write a receipt file");
+        }
+        let listing = |lines: &[&str]| lines.iter().map(|l| format!("{l}\n")).collect::<String>();
+        (listing(files).into_bytes(), listing(dirs).into_bytes())
+    }
+}
+
+/// The installer uninstall: the exe, the bundle and the key vanish at `at` on the fake clock.
+fn vanish_at(world: &World, unins: &Unins, at: Duration) {
+    world.at(at, Event::RemovePath(unins.exe.clone()));
+    world.at(at, Event::RemovePath(unins.bundle.clone()));
+    world.at(at, Event::RemoveKey(unins.subkey.clone()));
+}
+
+fn batch(rig: &Rig, os: HelperOs, ops: &[Op], seams: &Seams<'_>) -> Vec<OpEntry> {
+    let roots = CanonicalRoots {
+        roots: rig.roots(os),
+    };
+    let place = rig.place();
+    run_batch(
+        ops,
+        &OpCtx {
+            os,
+            roots: &roots,
+            place: &place,
+        },
+        seams,
+    )
+}
+
+fn job_json(ops: &[Op]) -> Vec<u8> {
+    let ops: Vec<Value> = ops
+        .iter()
+        .map(|op| match op {
+            Op::RunInnoInstaller { file, sha256 } | Op::RunPkgInstaller { file, sha256 } => {
+                json!({ "op": op.kind(), "file": file, "sha256": digest::hex(sha256) })
+            }
+            Op::RunInnoUninstaller { app_id } => json!({ "op": op.kind(), "app_id": app_id }),
+            Op::UninstallPkg { pkg_id } => json!({ "op": op.kind(), "pkg_id": pkg_id }),
+        })
+        .collect();
+    serde_json::to_vec(&json!({ "job_version": 1, "ops": ops })).expect("job json")
+}
+
+/// The whole `run` over a job written at `<S>/jobs/<STEM>.json`: its `Exit` and the result file, parsed.
+fn run_job(rig: &Rig, os: HelperOs, ops: &[Op], seams: &Seams<'_>) -> (Exit, Value) {
+    let bytes = job_json(ops);
+    let job = rig.state.join("jobs").join(format!("{STEM}.json"));
+    fs::write(&job, &bytes).expect("write the job");
+    let args = Args {
+        job,
+        job_sha256: digest::sha256(&bytes),
+        #[cfg(feature = "ci-temp-roots")]
+        ci_roots: None,
+    };
+    let exit = run(&args, os, &rig.roots(os), seams);
+    let written = fs::read(rig.place().result_path).expect("the result file was written");
+    (exit, serde_json::from_slice(&written).expect("result json"))
+}
+
+fn strs(v: &[&str]) -> Vec<OsString> {
+    v.iter().map(OsString::from).collect()
+}
+
+fn summary(entries: &[OpEntry]) -> Vec<(OpOutcome, Option<&'static str>)> {
+    entries.iter().map(|e| (e.outcome, e.code)).collect()
+}
+
+fn spawned(world: &World) -> Vec<Vec<OsString>> {
+    world
+        .calls()
+        .into_iter()
+        .filter_map(|c| match c {
+            Call::Spawn(argv) => Some(argv),
+            _ => None,
+        })
+        .collect()
+}
+
+/// Every child the helper started, `spawn` and `output` alike, in order.
+fn launched(world: &World) -> Vec<Vec<OsString>> {
+    world
+        .calls()
+        .into_iter()
+        .filter_map(|c| match c {
+            Call::Spawn(argv) | Call::Output(argv) => Some(argv),
+            _ => None,
+        })
+        .collect()
+}
+
+fn count(world: &World, f: impl Fn(&Call) -> bool) -> usize {
+    world.calls().iter().filter(|c| f(c)).count()
+}
+
+fn position(calls: &[Call], f: impl Fn(&Call) -> bool) -> usize {
+    calls.iter().position(f).expect("the call was recorded")
+}
+
+/// The run directory a recorded installer spawn names (its copy's parent).
+fn run_dir_of(argv: &[OsString]) -> PathBuf {
+    argv.iter()
+        .map(Path::new)
+        .find(|p| {
+            p.file_name()
+                .and_then(|n| n.to_str())
+                .is_some_and(|n| n.starts_with("installer-"))
+        })
+        .and_then(Path::parent)
+        .expect("the spawn names a run copy")
+        .to_path_buf()
+}
+
+/// A script whose child hashes every `installer-*` file it is handed, as the installer would read it.
+fn hashing(seen: &Mutex<Vec<[u8; 32]>>) -> Script<'_> {
+    Script {
+        effect: Some(Box::new(move |argv: &[OsString]| {
+            for a in argv {
+                let p = Path::new(a);
+                let copy = p
+                    .file_name()
+                    .and_then(|n| n.to_str())
+                    .is_some_and(|n| n.starts_with("installer-"));
+                if copy {
+                    let bytes = fs::read(p).expect("read the run copy");
+                    seen.lock()
+                        .expect("digest list")
+                        .push(digest::sha256(&bytes));
+                }
+            }
+        })),
+        ..Script::default()
+    }
+}
+
+/// A script whose child writes `log` to its `/LOG=` path, as Inno does.
+fn writes_log(log: Vec<u8>, exit: i32, never_exits: bool) -> Script<'static> {
+    Script {
+        exit,
+        never_exits,
+        effect: Some(Box::new(move |argv: &[OsString]| {
+            let path = argv
+                .iter()
+                .find_map(|a| a.to_str()?.strip_prefix("/LOG=").map(PathBuf::from));
+            if let Some(path) = path {
+                fs::write(path, &log).expect("write the installer log");
+            }
+        })),
+        ..Script::default()
+    }
+}
+
+fn info(pkg_id: &str) -> Vec<u8> {
+    format!(
+        "package-id: {pkg_id}\nversion: 1.0.0\nvolume: /\nlocation: \ninstall-time: 1700000000\n"
+    )
+    .into_bytes()
+}
+
+fn pkgutil_argv(args: &[&str]) -> Vec<OsString> {
+    std::iter::once(PKGUTIL)
+        .chain(args.iter().copied())
+        .map(OsString::from)
+        .collect()
+}
+
+/// Scripts the three receipt reads of `pkg_id` (stdout per argv).
+fn script_receipt(
+    runner: &ScriptedRunner<'_>,
+    pkg_id: &str,
+    info: Vec<u8>,
+    files: Vec<u8>,
+    dirs: Vec<u8>,
+) {
+    for (args, stdout) in [
+        (vec!["--pkg-info", pkg_id], info),
+        (vec!["--files", pkg_id, "--only-files"], files),
+        (vec!["--files", pkg_id, "--only-dirs"], dirs),
+    ] {
+        runner.on(
+            Match::Prefix(pkgutil_argv(&args)),
+            Script {
+                stdout,
+                ..Script::default()
+            },
+        );
+    }
+}
+
+/// A test classifier (SC-003): exit 99 is `Locked`, everything else the production rule.
+fn locked_on_99(code: i32, log: &[String]) -> InnoVerdict {
+    if code == 99 {
+        InnoVerdict::Locked
+    } else {
+        classify_inno(code, log)
+    }
+}
+
+thread_local! {
+    /// What `recording_classifier` was handed, per call, on this test's thread (no process-wide lock).
+    static SEEN: RefCell<Vec<Vec<String>>> = const { RefCell::new(Vec::new()) };
+}
+
+fn recording_classifier(code: i32, log: &[String]) -> InnoVerdict {
+    SEEN.with(|s| s.borrow_mut().push(log.to_vec()));
+    classify_inno(code, log)
+}
+
+const FLAGS4: [&str; 4] = [
+    "/VERYSILENT",
+    "/SUPPRESSMSGBOXES",
+    "/NORESTART",
+    "/NOCLOSEAPPLICATIONS",
+];
+
+#[test]
+fn exact_argv() {
+    // Windows: one installer, one uninstaller.
+    {
+        let rig = Rig::new();
+        let world = World::new();
+        let io = TempIo::new(&world, &rig.base);
+        let registry = FakeRegistry::new(&world);
+        let unins = rig.inno_install(&registry, APP_A, "Alpha");
+        vanish_at(&world, &unins, Duration::from_secs(1));
+        let digests = Mutex::new(Vec::new());
+        let runner = ScriptedRunner::new(&world);
+        runner.on(Match::Any, hashing(&digests));
+        let seams = Seams {
+            io: &io,
+            runner: &runner,
+            registry: &registry,
+            classify: classify_inno,
+        };
+        let (file, sha256) = rig.stage("alpha-setup.exe", b"inno setup bytes");
+        let installer = Op::RunInnoInstaller { file, sha256 };
+        let entries = batch(
+            &rig,
+            HelperOs::Windows,
+            &[
+                installer,
+                Op::RunInnoUninstaller {
+                    app_id: APP_A.to_owned(),
+                },
+            ],
+            &seams,
+        );
+        assert_eq!(
+            summary(&entries),
+            [(OpOutcome::Done, None), (OpOutcome::Done, None)]
+        );
+        let spawns = spawned(&world);
+        assert_eq!(spawns.len(), 2, "{spawns:?}");
+        let run = run_dir_of(&spawns[0]);
+        assert_eq!(run.parent(), Some(rig.win.run_base.as_path()));
+        let name = run
+            .file_name()
+            .and_then(|n| n.to_str())
+            .expect("run dir name");
+        let hex = name.strip_prefix("run-").expect("run-<hex>");
+        assert!(
+            hex.len() == 32
+                && hex
+                    .bytes()
+                    .all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit() || (b'a'..=b'f').contains(&b)),
+            "{name}"
+        );
+        let mut want = vec![run.join("installer-0.exe").into_os_string()];
+        want.extend(FLAGS4.iter().map(OsString::from));
+        let mut log = OsString::from("/LOG=");
+        log.push(run.join("inno-0.log"));
+        want.push(log);
+        assert_eq!(spawns[0], want);
+        let mut want = vec![unins.exe.clone().into_os_string()];
+        want.extend(strs(&["/VERYSILENT", "/SUPPRESSMSGBOXES", "/NORESTART"]));
+        assert_eq!(spawns[1], want);
+        assert!(world.calls().contains(&Call::UninstallValues(
+            "SOFTWARE\\Microsoft\\Windows\\CurrentVersion\\Uninstall\\{00000001-0000-4000-8000-000000000001}_is1"
+                .to_owned()
+        )));
+        assert_eq!(*digests.lock().expect("digests"), [sha256]);
+        for arg in spawns.iter().flatten() {
+            let text = arg.to_str().expect("UTF-8 argv");
+            let path = Path::new(text.strip_prefix("/LOG=").unwrap_or(text));
+            assert!(!path.starts_with(&rig.staging), "{text} is under staging");
+        }
+    }
+    // macOS: one pkg installer, one pkg uninstall.
+    {
+        let rig = Rig::new();
+        let world = World::new();
+        rig.root_own(&world);
+        let io = TempIo::new(&world, &rig.base);
+        let registry = FakeRegistry::new(&world);
+        let (files, dirs) = rig.receipt(
+            &[
+                "Library/Audio/Plug-Ins/VST3/Alpha.vst3",
+                "Library/Audio/Plug-Ins/VST3/Alpha.vst3/Contents",
+            ],
+            &["Library/Audio/Plug-Ins/VST3/Alpha.vst3/Contents/Info.plist"],
+        );
+        let digests = Mutex::new(Vec::new());
+        let runner = ScriptedRunner::new(&world);
+        for (args, stdout) in [
+            (
+                vec!["/usr/sbin/pkgutil", "--pkg-info", "com.krateaudio.alpha"],
+                info(PKG_A),
+            ),
+            (
+                vec![
+                    "/usr/sbin/pkgutil",
+                    "--files",
+                    "com.krateaudio.alpha",
+                    "--only-files",
+                ],
+                files,
+            ),
+            (
+                vec![
+                    "/usr/sbin/pkgutil",
+                    "--files",
+                    "com.krateaudio.alpha",
+                    "--only-dirs",
+                ],
+                dirs,
+            ),
+        ] {
+            runner.on(
+                Match::Prefix(strs(&args)),
+                Script {
+                    stdout,
+                    ..Script::default()
+                },
+            );
+        }
+        runner.on(Match::Any, hashing(&digests));
+        let seams = Seams {
+            io: &io,
+            runner: &runner,
+            registry: &registry,
+            classify: classify_inno,
+        };
+        let (file, sha256) = rig.stage("alpha.pkg", b"pkg bytes");
+        let installer = Op::RunPkgInstaller { file, sha256 };
+        let entries = batch(
+            &rig,
+            HelperOs::Macos,
+            &[
+                installer,
+                Op::UninstallPkg {
+                    pkg_id: PKG_A.to_owned(),
+                },
+            ],
+            &seams,
+        );
+        assert_eq!(
+            summary(&entries),
+            [(OpOutcome::Done, None), (OpOutcome::Done, None)]
+        );
+        let all = launched(&world);
+        let run = run_dir_of(&all[0]);
+        assert_eq!(run.parent(), Some(rig.mac.run_base.as_path()));
+        let copy = run.join("installer-0.pkg").into_os_string();
+        let want = [
+            vec![
+                OsString::from("/usr/sbin/installer"),
+                OsString::from("-pkg"),
+                copy,
+                OsString::from("-target"),
+                OsString::from("/"),
+            ],
+            strs(&["/usr/sbin/pkgutil", "--pkg-info", "com.krateaudio.alpha"]),
+            strs(&[
+                "/usr/sbin/pkgutil",
+                "--files",
+                "com.krateaudio.alpha",
+                "--only-files",
+            ]),
+            strs(&[
+                "/usr/sbin/pkgutil",
+                "--files",
+                "com.krateaudio.alpha",
+                "--only-dirs",
+            ]),
+            strs(&["/usr/sbin/pkgutil", "--forget", "com.krateaudio.alpha"]),
+        ];
+        assert_eq!(all, want);
+        assert_eq!(*digests.lock().expect("digests"), [sha256]);
+        for arg in all.iter().flatten() {
+            assert!(
+                !Path::new(arg).starts_with(&rig.staging),
+                "{arg:?} is under staging"
+            );
+        }
+        assert!(
+            !rig.on_vol("Library/Audio/Plug-Ins/VST3/Alpha.vst3")
+                .exists()
+        );
+        assert!(rig.mac.vst3.is_dir());
+    }
+}

+#[test]
+fn batch_of_three_middle_locked() {
+    let rig = Rig::new();
+    let world = World::new();
+    let io = TempIo::new(&world, &rig.base);
+    let registry = FakeRegistry::new(&world);
+    let runner = ScriptedRunner::new(&world);
+    runner.on(
+        Match::Nth(1),
+        Script {
+            exit: 99,
+            ..Script::default()
+        },
+    );
+    let seams = Seams {
+        io: &io,
+        runner: &runner,
+        registry: &registry,
+        classify: locked_on_99,
+    };
+    let ops = [
+        rig.inno("alpha.exe", b"alpha"),
+        rig.inno("beta.exe", b"beta"),
+        rig.inno("gamma.exe", b"gamma"),
+    ];
+    let (exit, result) = run_job(&rig, HelperOs::Windows, &ops, &seams);
+    assert_eq!(
+        exit,
+        Exit {
+            status: 0,
+            code: None
+        }
+    );
+    let field = |key: &str| -> Vec<Value> {
+        result["ops"]
+            .as_array()
+            .expect("ops")
+            .iter()
+            .map(|e| e[key].clone())
+            .collect()
+    };
+    assert_eq!(
+        field("outcome"),
+        [json!("done"), json!("locked"), json!("done")]
+    );
+    assert_eq!(
+        field("code"),
+        [Value::Null, json!("E_HELPER_LOCKED"), Value::Null]
+    );
+    assert_eq!(field("exit_code"), [json!(0), json!(99), json!(0)]);
+    let calls = world.calls();
+    let spawns: Vec<usize> = (0..calls.len())
+        .filter(|&i| matches!(calls[i], Call::Spawn(_)))
+        .collect();
+    let waits: Vec<usize> = (0..calls.len())
+        .filter(|&i| matches!(calls[i], Call::Wait { .. }))
+        .collect();
+    assert_eq!((spawns.len(), waits.len()), (3, 3), "{calls:?}");
+    assert!(
+        waits[1] < spawns[2],
+        "the third installer starts after the second exited"
+    );
+    let creates: Vec<usize> = (0..calls.len())
+        .filter(|&i| matches!(calls[i], Call::CreateRunDir(_)))
+        .collect();
+    assert_eq!(creates.len(), 1, "{calls:?}");
+    assert!(creates[0] < spawns[0]);
+    let Call::Spawn(first) = &calls[spawns[0]] else {
+        unreachable!()
+    };
+    let run = run_dir_of(first);
+    let removed: Vec<usize> = (0..calls.len())
+        .filter(|&i| matches!(&calls[i], Call::RemoveDir(_)))
+        .collect();
+    assert_eq!(removed.len(), 1, "{calls:?}");
+    assert_eq!(calls[removed[0]], Call::RemoveDir(run.clone()));
+    assert!(removed[0] > waits[2]);
+    assert!(!run.exists());
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn batch_timeout_stops_the_rest() {
+    let rig = Rig::new();
+    let world = World::new();
+    let io = TempIo::new(&world, &rig.base);
+    let registry = FakeRegistry::new(&world);
+    let runner = ScriptedRunner::new(&world);
+    runner.on(
+        Match::Nth(1),
+        Script {
+            never_exits: true,
+            ..Script::default()
+        },
+    );
+    let seams = Seams {
+        io: &io,
+        runner: &runner,
+        registry: &registry,
+        classify: classify_inno,
+    };
+    let ops = [
+        rig.inno("alpha.exe", b"alpha"),
+        rig.inno("beta.exe", b"beta"),
+        rig.inno("gamma.exe", b"gamma"),
+    ];
+    let (exit, result) = run_job(&rig, HelperOs::Windows, &ops, &seams);
+    assert_eq!(
+        exit,
+        Exit {
+            status: 0,
+            code: None
+        }
+    );
+    let entries = result["ops"].as_array().expect("ops");
+    let pairs: Vec<(Value, Value)> = entries
+        .iter()
+        .map(|e| (e["outcome"].clone(), e["code"].clone()))
+        .collect();
+    assert_eq!(
+        pairs,
+        [
+            (json!("done"), Value::Null),
+            (json!("failed"), json!("E_HELPER_TIMEOUT")),
+            (json!("not_run"), json!("E_HELPER_AFTER_TIMEOUT")),
+        ]
+    );
+    // The fake clock moved by exactly the installer timeout during op 2; nothing else advances it.
+    assert_eq!(
+        entries[1]["seconds"].as_f64(),
+        Some(INNO_INSTALL_TIMEOUT.as_secs_f64())
+    );
+    let spawns = spawned(&world);
+    assert_eq!(spawns.len(), 2, "no installer starts beside a running one");
+    assert_eq!(
+        count(
+            &world,
+            |c| matches!(c, Call::Wait { timeout, .. } if *timeout == INNO_INSTALL_TIMEOUT)
+        ),
+        2
+    );
+    // No third copy was made; `ProcessRunner` has no kill, and no call but these two waits touched a child.
+    assert_eq!(
+        count(
+            &world,
+            |c| matches!(c, Call::CreateNew(p) if p.ends_with("installer-2.exe"))
+        ),
+        0
+    );
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn uninstall_only_job_has_no_run_dir() {
+    let rig = Rig::new();
+    let world = World::new();
+    let io = TempIo::new(&world, &rig.base);
+    let registry = FakeRegistry::new(&world);
+    let alpha = rig.inno_install(&registry, APP_A, "Alpha");
+    let beta = rig.inno_install(&registry, APP_B, "Beta");
+    vanish_at(&world, &alpha, Duration::from_secs(1));
+    vanish_at(&world, &beta, Duration::from_secs(3));
+    let runner = ScriptedRunner::new(&world);
+    let seams = Seams {
+        io: &io,
+        runner: &runner,
+        registry: &registry,
+        classify: classify_inno,
+    };
+    let ops = [APP_A, APP_B].map(|id| Op::RunInnoUninstaller {
+        app_id: id.to_owned(),
+    });
+    let entries = batch(&rig, HelperOs::Windows, &ops, &seams);
+    assert_eq!(
+        summary(&entries),
+        [(OpOutcome::Done, None), (OpOutcome::Done, None)]
+    );
+    assert_eq!(
+        count(&world, |c| matches!(
+            c,
+            Call::CreateRunDir(_) | Call::CreateHelperFolder(_)
+        )),
+        0
+    );
+    assert_eq!(
+        fs::read_dir(&rig.win.run_base).expect("run base").count(),
+        0
+    );
+}

+#[test]
+fn run_dir_failure_fails_installers() {
+    let rig = Rig::new();
+    let world = World::new();
+    world.fault(Fault::CreateRunDir(io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied));
+    let io = TempIo::new(&world, &rig.base);
+    let registry = FakeRegistry::new(&world);
+    let alpha = rig.inno_install(&registry, APP_A, "Alpha");
+    vanish_at(&world, &alpha, Duration::from_secs(1));
+    let runner = ScriptedRunner::new(&world);
+    let seams = Seams {
+        io: &io,
+        runner: &runner,
+        registry: &registry,
+        classify: classify_inno,
+    };
+    let ops = [
+        rig.inno("alpha.exe", b"alpha"),
+        Op::RunInnoUninstaller {
+            app_id: APP_A.to_owned(),
+        },
+        rig.inno("beta.exe", b"beta"),
+    ];
+    let entries = batch(&rig, HelperOs::Windows, &ops, &seams);
+    assert_eq!(
+        summary(&entries),
+        [
+            (OpOutcome::Failed, Some("E_HELPER_RUN_DIR")),
+            (OpOutcome::Done, None),
+            (OpOutcome::Failed, Some("E_HELPER_RUN_DIR")),
+        ]
+    );
+    let spawns = spawned(&world);
+    assert_eq!(spawns.len(), 1, "only the uninstaller ran: {spawns:?}");
+    assert_eq!(spawns[0][0], alpha.exe.into_os_string());
+    assert_eq!(count(&world, |c| matches!(c, Call::CreateNew(_))), 0);
+}

+#[test]
+fn copy_error_is_named() {
+    let rig = Rig::new();
+    let world = World::new();
+    world.fault(Fault::WriteAfter {
+        name: OsString::from("installer-0.exe"),
+        bytes: 1000,
+        kind: io::ErrorKind::StorageFull,
+    });
+    let io = TempIo::new(&world, &rig.base);
+    let registry = FakeRegistry::new(&world);
+    let runner = ScriptedRunner::new(&world);
+    let seams = Seams {
+        io: &io,
+        runner: &runner,
+        registry: &registry,
+        classify: classify_inno,
+    };
+    let ops = [
+        rig.inno("alpha.exe", &vec![7u8; 200 * 1024]),
+        rig.inno("beta.exe", b"beta"),
+    ];
+    let entries = batch(&rig, HelperOs::Windows, &ops, &seams);
+    assert_eq!(
+        summary(&entries),
+        [
+            (OpOutcome::Failed, Some("E_HELPER_COPY")),
+            (OpOutcome::Done, None)
+        ]
+    );
+    let spawns = spawned(&world);
+    assert_eq!(spawns.len(), 1, "{spawns:?}");
+    let run = run_dir_of(&spawns[0]);
+    assert_eq!(Path::new(&spawns[0][0]), run.join("installer-1.exe"));
+    let calls = world.calls();
+    let partial = run.join("installer-0.exe");
+    let removed = position(&calls, |c| *c == Call::RemoveFile(partial.clone()));
+    let spawn = position(&calls, |c| matches!(c, Call::Spawn(_)));
+    assert!(
+        removed < spawn,
+        "the partial copy is removed before the next op"
+    );
+}

+#[test]
+fn spawn_error_is_named() {
+    let rig = Rig::new();
+    let world = World::new();
+    let io = TempIo::new(&world, &rig.base);
+    let registry = FakeRegistry::new(&world);
+    let runner = ScriptedRunner::new(&world);
+    runner.on(
+        Match::Nth(0),
+        Script {
+            spawn_error: Some(io::ErrorKind::NotFound),
+            ..Script::default()
+        },
+    );
+    let seams = Seams {
+        io: &io,
+        runner: &runner,
+        registry: &registry,
+        classify: classify_inno,
+    };
+    let ops = [
+        rig.inno("alpha.exe", b"alpha"),
+        rig.inno("beta.exe", b"beta"),
+    ];
+    let entries = batch(&rig, HelperOs::Windows, &ops, &seams);
+    assert_eq!(
+        summary(&entries),
+        [
+            (OpOutcome::Failed, Some("E_HELPER_SPAWN")),
+            (OpOutcome::Done, None)
+        ]
+    );
+    let last = spawned(&world)
+        .pop()
+        .expect("the second installer was spawned");
+    assert!(Path::new(&last[0]).ends_with("installer-1.exe"));
+}
