+//! The allowed roots (plan §4.1.6; D3, D7, D17, FR-026, FR-051; PD-5).
+
+use crate::error::{self, HelperError};
+use crate::seams::HelperIo;
+use std::ffi::OsStr;
+use std::io::ErrorKind;
+use std::path::{Component, Path, PathBuf};
+
+/// The last component of the manager's state dir; equal to the core's `APP_IDENTIFIER` (SC-011).
+pub const STATE_DIR_NAME: &str = "com.krateaudio.krate-manager";
+/// The macOS helper folder under `/Library/Application Support` (D7, PD-26).
+pub const HELPER_FOLDER: &str = "com.krateaudio.krate-manager-helper";
+/// The vendor folder below the plug-in and preset roots (D3).
+#[cfg(any(windows, target_os = "macos"))]
+const VENDOR: &str = "Krate Audio";
+
+/// How the job's state dir `<S>` is accepted (FR-025, D17).
+#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub enum StateDirRule {
+    UnderProfiles(PathBuf),
+    SameOwnerNonRoot,
+    Exact(PathBuf),
+}
+
+/// The Windows roots (D3, D7).
+#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub struct WindowsRoots {
+    pub vst3_vendor: PathBuf,
+    pub preset_vendor: PathBuf,
+    pub run_base: PathBuf,
+}
+
+/// The macOS roots (D3, D7, PD-5).
+#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub struct MacosRoots {
+    pub vst3: PathBuf,
+    pub components: PathBuf,
+    pub presets: PathBuf,
+    pub applications: PathBuf,
+    pub volume: PathBuf,
+    pub run_base: PathBuf,
+    pub run_ancestors: Vec<PathBuf>,
+}
+
+/// The per-OS roots; `None` on Linux (every op refused, FR-036).
+#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub enum OsRoots {
+    Windows(WindowsRoots),
+    Macos(MacosRoots),
+    None,
+}
+
+/// Every root the helper may act under (PD-5).
+#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub struct AllowedRoots {
+    pub os: OsRoots,
+    pub state_dir: StateDirRule,
+}
+
+impl AllowedRoots {
+    /// The production roots; takes no argument and reads no job, settings file or environment (FR-051, SC-007).
+    /// The only `target_os` branch of the helper (PD-7).
+    pub fn production() -> Result<AllowedRoots, HelperError> {
+        production_roots()
+    }
+}
+
+/// Windows (D3, D7, D17): the known folders, `E_HELPER_ROOTS` when a lookup fails (FR-051, tasks TA-6).
+#[cfg(windows)]
+fn production_roots() -> Result<AllowedRoots, HelperError> {
+    use crate::os::{KnownFolder, known_folder};
+    let folder = |id| known_folder(id).ok_or(error::ROOTS);
+    Ok(AllowedRoots {
+        os: OsRoots::Windows(WindowsRoots {
+            vst3_vendor: folder(KnownFolder::ProgramFilesCommon)?
+                .join("VST3")
+                .join(VENDOR),
+            preset_vendor: folder(KnownFolder::ProgramData)?.join(VENDOR),
+            run_base: folder(KnownFolder::Windows)?.join("Temp"),
+        }),
+        state_dir: StateDirRule::UnderProfiles(folder(KnownFolder::UserProfiles)?),
+    })
+}
+
+/// macOS (D3, D7, D17, PD-5): literals only.
+#[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
+fn production_roots() -> Result<AllowedRoots, HelperError> {
+    let library = Path::new("/Library");
+    let support = library.join("Application Support");
+    let plug_ins = library.join("Audio").join("Plug-Ins");
+    Ok(AllowedRoots {
+        os: OsRoots::Macos(MacosRoots {
+            vst3: plug_ins.join("VST3"),
+            components: plug_ins.join("Components"),
+            presets: support.join(VENDOR),
+            applications: PathBuf::from("/Applications"),
+            volume: PathBuf::from("/"),
+            run_base: support.join(HELPER_FOLDER),
+            run_ancestors: vec![library.to_path_buf(), support],
+        }),
+        state_dir: StateDirRule::SameOwnerNonRoot,
+    })
+}
+
+/// Linux and every other target: no roots, every op refused (D18, FR-036).
+#[cfg(not(any(windows, target_os = "macos")))]
+fn production_roots() -> Result<AllowedRoots, HelperError> {
+    Ok(AllowedRoots {
+        os: OsRoots::None,
+        state_dir: StateDirRule::SameOwnerNonRoot,
+    })
+}
+
+/// The `--ci-roots` file's read bound.
+#[cfg(feature = "ci-temp-roots")]
+const CI_ROOTS_MAX_BYTES: u64 = 64 * 1024;
+
+/// The Windows `--ci-roots` file (FR-052).
+#[cfg(feature = "ci-temp-roots")]
+#[derive(serde::Deserialize)]
+#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
+struct CiWindows {
+    state_dir: String,
+    vst3_vendor: String,
+    preset_vendor: String,
+}
+
+/// The macOS `--ci-roots` file (FR-052).
+#[cfg(feature = "ci-temp-roots")]
+#[derive(serde::Deserialize)]
+#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
+struct CiMacos {
+    state_dir: String,
+    vst3: String,
+    components: String,
+    presets: String,
+    applications: String,
+}
+
+/// The `--ci-roots` file where production has no roots (Linux): the state dir only.
+#[cfg(feature = "ci-temp-roots")]
+#[derive(serde::Deserialize)]
+#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
+struct CiNone {
+    state_dir: String,
+}
+
+/// The roots a `--ci-roots` file names (FR-052): the plug-in, preset and application roots and the state dir
+/// (`Exact`); `run_base`, `run_ancestors` and `volume` stay production (PD-5). Every path must be absolute and its
+/// canonical form (FR-026) must lie strictly under the canonical directory holding the file; the paths are returned
+/// as spelled, so a linked spelling reaches `run`'s own canonicalization. Any defect is `E_HELPER_ROOTS`.
+#[cfg(feature = "ci-temp-roots")]
+pub fn from_ci_file(io: &dyn HelperIo, file: &Path) -> Result<AllowedRoots, HelperError> {
+    let bytes = io
+        .read_bounded(file, CI_ROOTS_MAX_BYTES)
+        .map_err(|_| error::ROOTS)?;
+    if bytes.len() as u64 > CI_ROOTS_MAX_BYTES {
+        return Err(error::ROOTS);
+    }
+    let dir = file
+        .parent()
+        .filter(|d| !d.as_os_str().is_empty())
+        .ok_or(error::ROOTS)?;
+    let dir = io.canonicalize(dir).map_err(|_| error::ROOTS)?;
+    let bounded = |spelled: String| -> Result<PathBuf, HelperError> {
+        let path = PathBuf::from(spelled);
+        let canon = canonical_path(io, &path)?;
+        if canon != dir && canon.starts_with(&dir) {
+            Ok(path)
+        } else {
+            Err(error::ROOTS)
+        }
+    };
+    let (os, state_dir) = match AllowedRoots::production()?.os {
+        OsRoots::Windows(w) => {
+            let f: CiWindows = serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).map_err(|_| error::ROOTS)?;
+            let os = OsRoots::Windows(WindowsRoots {
+                vst3_vendor: bounded(f.vst3_vendor)?,
+                preset_vendor: bounded(f.preset_vendor)?,
+                run_base: w.run_base,
+            });
+            (os, f.state_dir)
+        }
+        OsRoots::Macos(m) => {
+            let f: CiMacos = serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).map_err(|_| error::ROOTS)?;
+            let os = OsRoots::Macos(MacosRoots {
+                vst3: bounded(f.vst3)?,
+                components: bounded(f.components)?,
+                presets: bounded(f.presets)?,
+                applications: bounded(f.applications)?,
+                volume: m.volume,
+                run_base: m.run_base,
+                run_ancestors: m.run_ancestors,
+            });
+            (os, f.state_dir)
+        }
+        OsRoots::None => {
+            let f: CiNone = serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).map_err(|_| error::ROOTS)?;
+            (OsRoots::None, f.state_dir)
+        }
+    };
+    Ok(AllowedRoots {
+        os,
+        state_dir: StateDirRule::Exact(bounded(state_dir)?),
+    })
+}
+
+/// `AllowedRoots` with every plug-in, preset and application root and the state-dir path in canonical form
+/// (FR-026). `run_base`, `run_ancestors` and `volume` stay as given: D7 builds the run directory from the known
+/// folder or the literal and never canonicalizes it (its plain form goes onto the installer's argv, FR-027), and the
+/// ancestors are `lstat`ed, never followed.
+#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub struct CanonicalRoots {
+    pub roots: AllowedRoots,
+}
+
+/// Each root canonicalized once; a missing root as its deepest existing ancestor plus the plain missing components
+/// (FR-026); failure `E_HELPER_ROOTS`.
+pub fn canonical(io: &dyn HelperIo, roots: &AllowedRoots) -> Result<CanonicalRoots, HelperError> {
+    let canon = |p: &Path| canonical_path(io, p);
+    let os = match &roots.os {
+        OsRoots::Windows(w) => OsRoots::Windows(WindowsRoots {
+            vst3_vendor: canon(&w.vst3_vendor)?,
+            preset_vendor: canon(&w.preset_vendor)?,
+            run_base: w.run_base.clone(),
+        }),
+        OsRoots::Macos(m) => OsRoots::Macos(MacosRoots {
+            vst3: canon(&m.vst3)?,
+            components: canon(&m.components)?,
+            presets: canon(&m.presets)?,
+            applications: canon(&m.applications)?,
+            volume: m.volume.clone(),
+            run_base: m.run_base.clone(),
+            run_ancestors: m.run_ancestors.clone(),
+        }),
+        OsRoots::None => OsRoots::None,
+    };
+    let state_dir = match &roots.state_dir {
+        StateDirRule::UnderProfiles(p) => StateDirRule::UnderProfiles(canon(p)?),
+        StateDirRule::SameOwnerNonRoot => StateDirRule::SameOwnerNonRoot,
+        StateDirRule::Exact(d) => StateDirRule::Exact(canon(d)?),
+    };
+    Ok(CanonicalRoots {
+        roots: AllowedRoots { os, state_dir },
+    })
+}
+
+/// FR-026 for one path: absolute, no `..` / `.` component, then the canonical form of its longest existing ancestor
+/// with the missing components re-joined. Only `NotFound` walks up; any other error, or no existing ancestor, is
+/// `E_HELPER_ROOTS`.
+fn canonical_path(io: &dyn HelperIo, path: &Path) -> Result<PathBuf, HelperError> {
+    let plain = path
+        .components()
+        .all(|c| !matches!(c, Component::ParentDir | Component::CurDir));
+    if !path.is_absolute() || !plain {
+        return Err(error::ROOTS);
+    }
+    let mut existing = path;
+    let mut missing: Vec<&OsStr> = Vec::new();
+    loop {
+        match io.canonicalize(existing) {
+            Ok(canon) => return Ok(missing.iter().rev().fold(canon, |p, c| p.join(c))),
+            Err(e) if e.kind() == ErrorKind::NotFound => {
+                let (Some(name), Some(parent)) = (existing.file_name(), existing.parent()) else {
+                    return Err(error::ROOTS);
+                };
+                missing.push(name);
+                existing = parent;
+            }
+            Err(_) => return Err(error::ROOTS),
+        }
+    }
+}
+

+#[path = "roots_tests.rs"]
+mod tests;

+//! `roots::tests` (owner T004; plan §7.1): SC-007's roots rows and FR-026's canonical form.
+
+use super::*;
+use crate::classify::InnoVerdict;
+use crate::cli::{self, Args};
+use crate::error;
+use crate::seams::Seams;
+use crate::seams::fake::{FakeRegistry, ScriptedRunner, TempIo, World};
+use crate::{Exit, HelperOs, digest};
+use std::cell::RefCell;
+use std::ffi::OsString;
+use std::fs;
+
+thread_local! {
+    /// The roots the recording `RunFn` was handed, per test thread (no shared lock).
+    static SEEN: RefCell<Vec<AllowedRoots>> = const { RefCell::new(Vec::new()) };
+}
+
+fn recording(_args: &Args, _os: HelperOs, roots: &AllowedRoots, _seams: &Seams<'_>) -> Exit {
+    SEEN.with(|seen| seen.borrow_mut().push(roots.clone()));
+    Exit {
+        status: 0,
+        code: None,
+    }
+}
+
+fn never_classified(_exit_code: i32, _log: &[String]) -> InnoVerdict {
+    InnoVerdict::Failed
+}
+
+/// SC-007: a job and the manager's settings file beside it, both naming an extra root in every key FR-001 and the
+/// settings know; the roots `main_with` hands its `RunFn` are still exactly `AllowedRoots::production()`.
+#[test]
+fn binary_roots_ignore_job_and_settings() {
+    let tmp = tempfile::TempDir::new().expect("a temp dir");
+    let state = tmp.path().join(STATE_DIR_NAME);
+    let jobs = state.join("jobs");
+    fs::create_dir_all(&jobs).expect("jobs/");
+    let decoy = tmp.path().join("decoy");
+    fs::create_dir_all(&decoy).expect("decoy/");
+    let job = jobs.join("job-0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef.json");
+    let settings = state.join("settings.json");
+    let production = AllowedRoots::production().expect("the production roots resolve");
+    let decoy = decoy.to_str().expect("a UTF-8 temp path").to_owned();
+
+    for extra in [r"C:\Windows", "/etc", decoy.as_str()] {
+        let ops = serde_json::json!([
+            { "op": "run_inno_installer", "file": extra, "sha256": extra },
+            { "op": "run_inno_uninstaller", "app_id": extra },
+            { "op": "run_pkg_installer", "file": extra, "sha256": extra },
+            { "op": "uninstall_pkg", "pkg_id": extra },
+        ]);
+        let bytes = serde_json::to_vec(&serde_json::json!({ "job_version": 1, "ops": ops }))
+            .expect("job JSON");
+        fs::write(&job, &bytes).expect("the job fixture");
+        let settings_bytes = serde_json::to_vec(&serde_json::json!({
+            "schema_version": 1,
+            "linux_plugin_root": extra,
+            "linux_graphics_workaround": false,
+        }))
+        .expect("settings JSON");
+        fs::write(&settings, settings_bytes).expect("the settings fixture");
+
+        let world = World::new();
+        let io = TempIo::new(&world, tmp.path());
+        let runner = ScriptedRunner::new(&world);
+        let registry = FakeRegistry::new(&world);
+        let seams = Seams {
+            io: &io,
+            runner: &runner,
+            registry: &registry,
+            classify: never_classified,
+        };
+        let args: Vec<OsString> = vec![
+            "--job".into(),
+            job.clone().into_os_string(),
+            "--job-sha256".into(),
+            digest::hex(&digest::sha256(&bytes)).into(),
+        ];
+        let exit = cli::main_with(args, &seams, recording);
+        assert_eq!(
+            exit,
+            Exit {
+                status: 0,
+                code: None
+            },
+            "{extra}"
+        );
+        let seen: Vec<AllowedRoots> = SEEN.with(|seen| seen.borrow_mut().drain(..).collect());
+        assert_eq!(seen, vec![production.clone()], "{extra}");
+    }
+}

+/// FR-026: an existing root is its canonical form; a missing tail is the longest existing ancestor's canonical form
+/// with the plain missing components re-joined; the run base, its ancestors and the volume are never canonicalized
+/// (D7: the run directory's path is built from the known folder or the literal).
+#[test]
+fn canonical_keeps_missing_tails() {
+    let tmp = tempfile::TempDir::new().expect("a temp dir");
+    let base = tmp.path();
+    fs::create_dir_all(base.join("vst3")).expect("vst3/");
+    let canon = fs::canonicalize(base).expect("the temp dir canonicalizes");
+    let world = World::new();
+    let io = TempIo::new(&world, base);
+
+    let macos = AllowedRoots {
+        os: OsRoots::Macos(MacosRoots {
+            vst3: base.join("vst3"),
+            components: base.join("missing").join("Components"),
+            presets: base.join("missing").join("Krate Audio"),
+            applications: base.join("Applications"),
+            volume: base.to_path_buf(),
+            run_base: base.join("Library").join(HELPER_FOLDER),
+            run_ancestors: vec![base.join("Library")],
+        }),
+        state_dir: StateDirRule::Exact(base.join(STATE_DIR_NAME)),
+    };
+    let MacosRoots {
+        volume,
+        run_base,
+        run_ancestors,
+        ..
+    } = match &macos.os {
+        OsRoots::Macos(m) => m.clone(),
+        _ => unreachable!("built as macOS above"),
+    };
+    assert_eq!(
+        canonical(&io, &macos),
+        Ok(CanonicalRoots {
+            roots: AllowedRoots {
+                os: OsRoots::Macos(MacosRoots {
+                    vst3: canon.join("vst3"),
+                    components: canon.join("missing").join("Components"),
+                    presets: canon.join("missing").join("Krate Audio"),
+                    applications: canon.join("Applications"),
+                    volume,
+                    run_base,
+                    run_ancestors,
+                }),
+                state_dir: StateDirRule::Exact(canon.join(STATE_DIR_NAME)),
+            },
+        })
+    );
+
+    let windows = AllowedRoots {
+        os: OsRoots::Windows(WindowsRoots {
+            vst3_vendor: base.join("vst3").join("Krate Audio"),
+            preset_vendor: base.join("ProgramData").join("Krate Audio"),
+            run_base: base.join("run"),
+        }),
+        state_dir: StateDirRule::UnderProfiles(base.to_path_buf()),
+    };
+    assert_eq!(
+        canonical(&io, &windows),
+        Ok(CanonicalRoots {
+            roots: AllowedRoots {
+                os: OsRoots::Windows(WindowsRoots {
+                    vst3_vendor: canon.join("vst3").join("Krate Audio"),
+                    preset_vendor: canon.join("ProgramData").join("Krate Audio"),
+                    run_base: base.join("run"),
+                }),
+                state_dir: StateDirRule::UnderProfiles(canon.clone()),
+            },
+        })
+    );
+
+    let linux = AllowedRoots {
+        os: OsRoots::None,
+        state_dir: StateDirRule::SameOwnerNonRoot,
+    };
+    assert_eq!(
+        canonical(&io, &linux),
+        Ok(CanonicalRoots {
+            roots: linux.clone()
+        })
+    );
+}
+
+/// FR-026: a component that is not plain (`..`) or a relative root is `E_HELPER_ROOTS`.
+#[test]
+fn canonical_refuses_non_plain_components() {
+    let tmp = tempfile::TempDir::new().expect("a temp dir");
+    let base = tmp.path();
+    let world = World::new();
+    let io = TempIo::new(&world, base);
+    let with_vst3 = |vst3: PathBuf| AllowedRoots {
+        os: OsRoots::Windows(WindowsRoots {
+            vst3_vendor: vst3,
+            preset_vendor: base.join("presets"),
+            run_base: base.join("run"),
+        }),
+        state_dir: StateDirRule::Exact(base.join(STATE_DIR_NAME)),
+    };
+    for bad in [
+        base.join("missing").join("..").join("x"),
+        base.join("..").join("x"),
+        PathBuf::from("relative").join("x"),
+    ] {
+        assert_eq!(
+            canonical(&io, &with_vst3(bad.clone())),
+            Err(error::ROOTS),
+            "{}",
+            bad.display()
+        );
+    }
+    let state = AllowedRoots {
+        os: OsRoots::None,
+        state_dir: StateDirRule::Exact(base.join("missing").join("..").join(STATE_DIR_NAME)),
+    };
+    assert_eq!(canonical(&io, &state), Err(error::ROOTS));
+}

+//! The seams every OS effect goes through (plan §4.1.9; FR-053, SI-23) and their test fakes (`fake`, PD-8).
+
+use crate::classify::InnoVerdict;
+use crate::validate::LstatFacts;
+use std::ffi::OsString;
+use std::io::{self, Read};
+use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
+use std::time::Duration;
+
+/// A writer whose bytes can be made durable.
+pub trait WriteSync: io::Write {
+    fn sync_all(&mut self) -> io::Result<()>;
+}
+
+/// Every filesystem, clock and randomness step of the helper.
+pub trait HelperIo {
+    /// From the start, at most `max + 1` bytes (PD-6 open).
+    fn read_bounded(&self, path: &Path, max: u64) -> io::Result<Vec<u8>>;
+    /// The last `max` bytes (SI-28).
+    fn read_tail(&self, path: &Path, max: u64) -> io::Result<Vec<u8>>;
+    fn canonicalize(&self, path: &Path) -> io::Result<PathBuf>;
+    /// `None` = absent; `uid` / `mode` `None` on Windows.
+    fn lstat(&self, path: &Path) -> io::Result<Option<LstatFacts>>;
+    /// PD-6: no final link, no blocking, a regular file only.
+    fn open_source(&self, path: &Path) -> io::Result<Box<dyn Read + '_>>;
+    fn create_new(&self, path: &Path) -> io::Result<Box<dyn WriteSync + '_>>;
+    /// PD-26 (macOS); `Ok` when present.
+    fn create_helper_folder(&self, path: &Path) -> io::Result<()>;
+    /// D7: fails on an existing name.
+    fn create_run_dir(&self, path: &Path) -> io::Result<()>;
+    fn list_dir(&self, dir: &Path) -> io::Result<Vec<OsString>>;
+    fn remove_file(&self, path: &Path) -> io::Result<()>;
+    fn remove_dir(&self, path: &Path) -> io::Result<()>;
+    /// 32 lower-case hex digits (PD-19).
+    fn random_hex(&self) -> String;
+    fn sleep(&self, d: Duration);
+    /// Monotonic since an arbitrary origin (FR-041).
+    fn now(&self) -> Duration;
+}
+
+/// A spawned child, by number.
+#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+pub struct ChildId(pub u64);
+
+/// Child processes, never through a shell (FR-030 to FR-033).
+pub trait ProcessRunner {
+    fn spawn(&self, argv: &[OsString]) -> io::Result<ChildId>;
+    /// `None` = still running at the timeout; the child is never killed (FR-042).
+    fn wait(&self, child: ChildId, timeout: Duration) -> io::Result<Option<i32>>;
+    fn output(&self, argv: &[OsString], timeout: Duration) -> io::Result<Option<(i32, Vec<u8>)>>;
+}
+
+/// HKLM reads, 64-bit view (FR-031).
+pub trait Registry {
+    /// `(UninstallString, InstallLocation)`; `None` = no key.
+    fn uninstall_values(&self, subkey: &str) -> Option<(Option<String>, Option<String>)>;
+    fn key_exists(&self, subkey: &str) -> bool;
+}
+
+/// The seams of one run (FR-053).
+pub struct Seams<'a> {
+    pub io: &'a dyn HelperIo,
+    pub runner: &'a dyn ProcessRunner,
+    pub registry: &'a dyn Registry,
+    pub classify: fn(i32, &[String]) -> InnoVerdict,
+}
+
+/// The test fakes (plan §4.1.9, tasks TA-3): one shared `World` with a fake clock, the real filesystem under a
+/// sandbox base, a scripted runner and a map registry.
+#[cfg(any(test, feature = "test-support"))]
+pub mod fake {
+    use super::{ChildId, HelperIo, ProcessRunner, Registry, WriteSync};
+    use crate::os::open_regular_no_follow;
+    use crate::validate::{Kind, LstatFacts};
+    use std::collections::BTreeMap;
+    use std::ffi::OsString;
+    use std::fs::{self, File, Metadata};
+    use std::io::{self, Read, Seek, SeekFrom, Write};
+    use std::path::{Component, Path, PathBuf};
+    use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex, MutexGuard, PoisonError};
+    use std::time::Duration;
+
+    /// The mode `root_owned` gives a run directory created under it (D7).
+    const RUN_DIR_MODE: u32 = 0o700;
+    /// The mode `root_owned` gives its directory and everything else created under it.
+    const ROOT_MODE: u32 = 0o755;
+
+    /// One fake key's `(UninstallString, InstallLocation)`.
+    type KeyValues = (Option<String>, Option<String>);
+
+    /// A world change that fires when the clock reaches its time.
+    #[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+    pub enum Event {
+        RemovePath(PathBuf),
+        RemoveKey(String),
+    }
+
+    /// An injected I/O failure; `name` is the final path component.
+    #[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+    pub enum Fault {
+        CreateRunDir(io::ErrorKind),
+        CreateNew {
+            name: OsString,
+            kind: io::ErrorKind,
+        },
+        WriteAfter {
+            name: OsString,
+            bytes: u64,
+            kind: io::ErrorKind,
+        },
+        RemoveFile(PathBuf),
+    }
+
+    /// A change to a source around its copy.
+    #[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+    pub enum Hook {
+        RewriteAfterCopy {
+            src: PathBuf,
+            bytes: Vec<u8>,
+        },
+        RewriteDuringCopy {
+            src: PathBuf,
+            bytes: Vec<u8>,
+        },
+        LinkBeforeOpen {
+            src: PathBuf,
+            target: PathBuf,
+        },
+        #[cfg(unix)]
+        FifoBeforeOpen {
+            src: PathBuf,
+        },
+    }
+
+    impl Hook {
+        fn src(&self) -> &Path {
+            match self {
+                Hook::RewriteAfterCopy { src, .. }
+                | Hook::RewriteDuringCopy { src, .. }
+                | Hook::LinkBeforeOpen { src, .. } => src,
+                #[cfg(unix)]
+                Hook::FifoBeforeOpen { src } => src,
+            }
+        }
+
+        /// Fires before the open (a link or a FIFO in place of the source), not around the copy.
+        fn before_open(&self) -> bool {
+            match self {
+                Hook::LinkBeforeOpen { .. } => true,
+                #[cfg(unix)]
+                Hook::FifoBeforeOpen { .. } => true,
+                Hook::RewriteAfterCopy { .. } | Hook::RewriteDuringCopy { .. } => false,
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// One recorded seam call.
+    #[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+    pub enum Call {
+        Spawn(Vec<OsString>),
+        Wait { child: ChildId, timeout: Duration },
+        Output(Vec<OsString>),
+        Sleep(Duration),
+        CreateNew(PathBuf),
+        CreateHelperFolder(PathBuf),
+        CreateRunDir(PathBuf),
+        RemoveFile(PathBuf),
+        RemoveDir(PathBuf),
+        UninstallValues(String),
+        KeyExists(String),
+    }
+
+    /// A lock that survives a panic in another holder (the sandbox rows panic on purpose).
+    fn lock<T>(m: &Mutex<T>) -> MutexGuard<'_, T> {
+        m.lock().unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner)
+    }
+
+    /// The spelling the world keys overrides, faults and hooks by: the canonical parent plus the final name (the final
+    /// component is never followed), so a temp path and its canonical form (`\\?\C:\…` on Windows, `/private/var/…` on
+    /// macOS) meet; the path as given when the parent does not resolve.
+    fn key(path: &Path) -> PathBuf {
+        match (path.parent(), path.file_name()) {
+            (Some(parent), Some(name)) if !parent.as_os_str().is_empty() => {
+                fs::canonicalize(parent).map_or_else(|_| path.to_path_buf(), |p| p.join(name))
+            }
+            _ => fs::canonicalize(path).unwrap_or_else(|_| path.to_path_buf()),
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// Lexical strict descendant, as `Roots::check_sandboxed` (`crates/core/src/roots.rs:173-184`); never through `..`.
+    fn strictly_under(path: &Path, base: &Path) -> bool {
+        path != base
+            && path.starts_with(base)
+            && !path.components().any(|c| c == Component::ParentDir)
+    }
+
+    fn injected(kind: io::ErrorKind) -> io::Error {
+        io::Error::new(kind, "seams::fake injected fault")
+    }
+
+    /// The shared state of one test: clock, events, owner overrides, faults, hooks, calls, the registry map.
+    #[derive(Default)]
+    pub struct World {
+        clock: Mutex<Duration>,
+        events: Mutex<Vec<(Duration, Event)>>,
+        /// (uid, mode) by `key`.
+        owners: Mutex<BTreeMap<PathBuf, (u32, u32)>>,
+        /// The `root_owned` directories, by `key`.
+        root_dirs: Mutex<Vec<PathBuf>>,
+        faults: Mutex<Vec<Fault>>,
+        hooks: Mutex<Vec<Hook>>,
+        calls: Mutex<Vec<Call>>,
+        /// `(UninstallString, InstallLocation)` by full subkey (TA-17).
+        registry: Mutex<BTreeMap<String, KeyValues>>,
+        /// Every `TempIo` base, as given and canonical: where `Event::RemovePath` may act.
+        bases: Mutex<Vec<PathBuf>>,
+        hex: Mutex<u128>,
+    }
+
+    impl World {
+        pub fn new() -> World {
+            World::default()
+        }
+
+        pub fn now(&self) -> Duration {
+            *lock(&self.clock)
+        }
+
+        /// Moves the clock and fires every event whose time it has reached, earliest first.
+        pub fn advance(&self, d: Duration) {
+            let now = {
+                let mut clock = lock(&self.clock);
+                *clock += d;
+                *clock
+            };
+            self.fire_due(now);
+        }
+
+        /// Fires at the first clock move that reaches `when`.
+        pub fn at(&self, when: Duration, event: Event) {
+            lock(&self.events).push((when, event));
+        }
+
+        pub fn owner(&self, path: &Path, uid: u32, mode: u32) {
+            let k = key(path);
+            lock(&self.owners).insert(k, (uid, mode));
+        }
+
+        /// `dir` and everything `TempIo` later creates under it report uid 0: `0o700` for a run directory, else
+        /// `0o755`.
+        pub fn root_owned(&self, dir: &Path) {
+            let k = key(dir);
+            lock(&self.root_dirs).push(k.clone());
+            lock(&self.owners).insert(k, (0, ROOT_MODE));
+        }
+
+        /// Holds until `clear_faults`.
+        pub fn fault(&self, fault: Fault) {
+            lock(&self.faults).push(fault);
+        }
+
+        pub fn clear_faults(&self) {
+            lock(&self.faults).clear();
+        }
+
+        /// Fires once, at the next open of its source.
+        pub fn hook(&self, hook: Hook) {
+            lock(&self.hooks).push(hook);
+        }
+
+        pub fn calls(&self) -> Vec<Call> {
+            lock(&self.calls).clone()
+        }
+
+        /// The argv of every `spawn` call, in order.
+        pub fn spawns(&self) -> Vec<Vec<OsString>> {
+            lock(&self.calls)
+                .iter()
+                .filter_map(|c| match c {
+                    Call::Spawn(argv) => Some(argv.clone()),
+                    _ => None,
+                })
+                .collect()
+        }
+
+        /// The path of every `remove_file` and `remove_dir` call, in order.
+        pub fn removals(&self) -> Vec<PathBuf> {
+            lock(&self.calls)
+                .iter()
+                .filter_map(|c| match c {
+                    Call::RemoveFile(p) | Call::RemoveDir(p) => Some(p.clone()),
+                    _ => None,
+                })
+                .collect()
+        }
+
+        fn record(&self, call: Call) {
+            lock(&self.calls).push(call);
+        }
+
+        fn fire_due(&self, now: Duration) {
+            let due = {
+                let mut events = lock(&self.events);
+                let all = std::mem::take(&mut *events);
+                let (mut due, keep): (Vec<_>, Vec<_>) =
+                    all.into_iter().partition(|(when, _)| *when <= now);
+                *events = keep;
+                due.sort_by_key(|(when, _)| *when);
+                due
+            };
+            for (_, event) in due {
+                match event {
+                    Event::RemovePath(path) => self.remove_real(&path),
+                    Event::RemoveKey(subkey) => {
+                        lock(&self.registry).remove(&subkey);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        fn remove_real(&self, path: &Path) {
+            let inside = lock(&self.bases).iter().any(|b| strictly_under(path, b));
+            if !inside {
+                panic!(
+                    "Event::RemovePath: {} is outside the test sandbox",
+                    path.display()
+                );
+            }
+            let removed = match fs::symlink_metadata(path) {
+                Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return,
+                Err(e) => Err(e),
+                Ok(meta) if meta.is_dir() => fs::remove_dir_all(path),
+                Ok(_) => fs::remove_file(path),
+            };
+            if let Err(e) = removed {
+                panic!("Event::RemovePath {}: {e}", path.display());
+            }
+        }
+
+        fn mark_created(&self, path: &Path, mode: u32) {
+            let k = key(path);
+            let under = lock(&self.root_dirs)
+                .iter()
+                .any(|root| strictly_under(&k, root));
+            if under {
+                lock(&self.owners).insert(k, (0, mode));
+            }
+        }
+
+        fn owner_of(&self, path: &Path) -> Option<(u32, u32)> {
+            let k = key(path);
+            lock(&self.owners).get(&k).copied()
+        }
+
+        fn find_fault<T>(&self, f: impl Fn(&Fault) -> Option<T>) -> Option<T> {
+            lock(&self.faults).iter().find_map(f)
+        }
+
+        /// Removes and returns the hooks on `path` that `pick` selects.
+        fn take_hooks(&self, path: &Path, pick: impl Fn(&Hook) -> bool) -> Vec<Hook> {
+            let k = key(path);
+            let mut hooks = lock(&self.hooks);
+            let all = std::mem::take(&mut *hooks);
+            let (taken, keep): (Vec<_>, Vec<_>) =
+                all.into_iter().partition(|h| pick(h) && key(h.src()) == k);
+            *hooks = keep;
+            taken
+        }
+
+        fn next_hex(&self) -> String {
+            let mut n = lock(&self.hex);
+            *n += 1;
+            format!("{:032x}", *n)
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// The real filesystem under `base` plus the world's overrides; panics "outside the test sandbox".
+    pub struct TempIo<'w> {
+        world: &'w World,
+        /// `base` as given, and canonical when that differs.
+        bases: Vec<PathBuf>,
+    }
+
+    impl<'w> TempIo<'w> {
+        pub fn new(world: &'w World, base: &Path) -> TempIo<'w> {
+            let mut bases = vec![base.to_path_buf()];
+            if let Ok(canonical) = fs::canonicalize(base)
+                && canonical != base
+            {
+                bases.push(canonical);
+            }
+            lock(&world.bases).extend(bases.iter().cloned());
+            TempIo { world, bases }
+        }
+
+        fn check(&self, op: &str, path: &Path) {
+            if !self.bases.iter().any(|b| strictly_under(path, b)) {
+                panic!(
+                    "TempIo::{op}: {} is outside the test sandbox {}",
+                    path.display(),
+                    self.bases[0].display()
+                );
+            }
+        }
+
+        /// The sandbox check, then the before-open hooks; the open itself is the production one (PD-6).
+        fn open(&self, op: &str, path: &Path) -> io::Result<File> {
+            self.check(op, path);
+            for hook in self.world.take_hooks(path, Hook::before_open) {
+                match hook {
+                    Hook::LinkBeforeOpen { src, target } => replace_with_link(&src, &target),
+                    #[cfg(unix)]
+                    Hook::FifoBeforeOpen { src } => replace_with_fifo(&src),
+                    Hook::RewriteAfterCopy { .. } | Hook::RewriteDuringCopy { .. } => {}
+                }
+            }
+            open_regular_no_follow(path)
+        }
+    }
+
+    fn remove_if_present(path: &Path) {
+        match fs::remove_file(path) {
+            Ok(()) => {}
+            Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {}
+            Err(e) => panic!("seams::fake hook: cannot remove {}: {e}", path.display()),
+        }
+    }
+
+    fn replace_with_link(src: &Path, target: &Path) {
+        remove_if_present(src);
+        #[cfg(unix)]
+        let made = std::os::unix::fs::symlink(target, src);
+        #[cfg(windows)]
+        let made = if target.is_dir() {
+            std::os::windows::fs::symlink_dir(target, src)
+        } else {
+            std::os::windows::fs::symlink_file(target, src)
+        };
+        if let Err(e) = made {
+            panic!("Hook::LinkBeforeOpen: cannot link {}: {e}", src.display());
+        }
+    }
+
+    #[cfg(unix)]
+    fn replace_with_fifo(src: &Path) {
+        remove_if_present(src);
+        let status = std::process::Command::new("/usr/bin/mkfifo")
+            .arg(src)
+            .status();
+        if !status.as_ref().is_ok_and(std::process::ExitStatus::success) {
+            panic!("Hook::FifoBeforeOpen: mkfifo {}: {status:?}", src.display());
+        }
+    }
+
+    #[cfg(unix)]
+    fn real_owner(meta: &Metadata) -> (Option<u32>, Option<u32>) {
+        use std::os::unix::fs::MetadataExt;
+        (Some(meta.uid()), Some(meta.mode()))
+    }
+
+    #[cfg(not(unix))]
+    fn real_owner(_meta: &Metadata) -> (Option<u32>, Option<u32>) {
+        (None, None)
+    }
+
+    #[cfg(unix)]
+    fn make_run_dir(path: &Path) -> io::Result<()> {
+        use std::os::unix::fs::DirBuilderExt;
+        fs::DirBuilder::new().mode(RUN_DIR_MODE).create(path)
+    }
+
+    #[cfg(not(unix))]
+    fn make_run_dir(path: &Path) -> io::Result<()> {
+        fs::create_dir(path)
+    }
+
+    /// The source being copied, with the copy hooks: a rewrite after the first chunk, a rewrite at end of file.
+    struct CopyReader {
+        file: File,
+        src: PathBuf,
+        during: Option<Vec<u8>>,
+        after: Option<Vec<u8>>,
+    }
+
+    impl Read for CopyReader {
+        fn read(&mut self, buf: &mut [u8]) -> io::Result<usize> {
+            if buf.is_empty() {
+                return Ok(0);
+            }
+            let n = self.file.read(buf)?;
+            let rewrite = if n > 0 {
+                self.during.take()
+            } else {
+                self.after.take()
+            };
+            if let Some(bytes) = rewrite
+                && let Err(e) = fs::write(&self.src, bytes)
+            {
+                panic!(
+                    "seams::fake copy hook: cannot rewrite {}: {e}",
+                    self.src.display()
+                );
+            }
+            Ok(n)
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// A created file; with a `WriteAfter` fault, every write past its budget fails.
+    struct FaultWriter {
+        file: File,
+        budget: Option<(u64, io::ErrorKind)>,
+    }
+
+    impl Write for FaultWriter {
+        fn write(&mut self, buf: &[u8]) -> io::Result<usize> {
+            let Some((left, kind)) = self.budget else {
+                return self.file.write(buf);
+            };
+            if buf.is_empty() {
+                return Ok(0);
+            }
+            if left == 0 {
+                return Err(injected(kind));
+            }
+            let n = usize::try_from(left).map_or(buf.len(), |l| l.min(buf.len()));
+            let written = self.file.write(&buf[..n])?;
+            self.budget = Some((left - written as u64, kind));
+            Ok(written)
+        }
+
+        fn flush(&mut self) -> io::Result<()> {
+            self.file.flush()
+        }
+    }
+
+    impl WriteSync for FaultWriter {
+        fn sync_all(&mut self) -> io::Result<()> {
+            self.file.sync_all()
+        }
+    }
+
+    impl HelperIo for TempIo<'_> {
+        fn read_bounded(&self, path: &Path, max: u64) -> io::Result<Vec<u8>> {
+            let file = self.open("read_bounded", path)?;
+            let mut out = Vec::new();
+            file.take(max.saturating_add(1)).read_to_end(&mut out)?;
+            Ok(out)
+        }
+
+        fn read_tail(&self, path: &Path, max: u64) -> io::Result<Vec<u8>> {
+            let mut file = self.open("read_tail", path)?;
+            let len = file.metadata()?.len();
+            file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(len.saturating_sub(max)))?;
+            let mut out = Vec::new();
+            file.read_to_end(&mut out)?;
+            Ok(out)
+        }
+
+        fn canonicalize(&self, path: &Path) -> io::Result<PathBuf> {
+            fs::canonicalize(path)
+        }
+
+        fn lstat(&self, path: &Path) -> io::Result<Option<LstatFacts>> {
+            let meta = match fs::symlink_metadata(path) {
+                Ok(meta) => meta,
+                Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(None),
+                Err(e) => return Err(e),
+            };
+            let ft = meta.file_type();
+            let kind = if ft.is_symlink() {
+                Kind::Symlink
+            } else if ft.is_dir() {
+                Kind::Dir
+            } else if ft.is_file() {
+                Kind::File
+            } else {
+                Kind::Other
+            };
+            let (uid, mode) = match self.world.owner_of(path) {
+                Some((uid, mode)) => (Some(uid), Some(mode)),
+                None => real_owner(&meta),
+            };
+            Ok(Some(LstatFacts { kind, uid, mode }))
+        }
+
+        fn open_source(&self, path: &Path) -> io::Result<Box<dyn Read + '_>> {
+            let file = self.open("open_source", path)?;
+            let mut during = None;
+            let mut after = None;
+            for hook in self.world.take_hooks(path, |h| !h.before_open()) {
+                match hook {
+                    Hook::RewriteDuringCopy { bytes, .. } => during = Some(bytes),
+                    Hook::RewriteAfterCopy { bytes, .. } => after = Some(bytes),
+                    Hook::LinkBeforeOpen { .. } => {}
+                    #[cfg(unix)]
+                    Hook::FifoBeforeOpen { .. } => {}
+                }
+            }
+            Ok(Box::new(CopyReader {
+                file,
+                src: path.to_path_buf(),
+                during,
+                after,
+            }))
+        }
+
+        fn create_new(&self, path: &Path) -> io::Result<Box<dyn WriteSync + '_>> {
+            self.check("create_new", path);
+            self.world.record(Call::CreateNew(path.to_path_buf()));
+            let name = path.file_name();
+            if let Some(kind) = self.world.find_fault(|f| match f {
+                Fault::CreateNew { name: n, kind } if name == Some(n.as_os_str()) => Some(*kind),
+                _ => None,
+            }) {
+                return Err(injected(kind));
+            }
+            let budget = self.world.find_fault(|f| match f {
+                Fault::WriteAfter {
+                    name: n,
+                    bytes,
+                    kind,
+                } if name == Some(n.as_os_str()) => Some((*bytes, *kind)),
+                _ => None,
+            });
+            let file = fs::OpenOptions::new()
+                .write(true)
+                .create_new(true)
+                .open(path)?;
+            self.world.mark_created(path, ROOT_MODE);
+            Ok(Box::new(FaultWriter { file, budget }))
+        }
+
+        fn create_helper_folder(&self, path: &Path) -> io::Result<()> {
+            self.check("create_helper_folder", path);
+            self.world
+                .record(Call::CreateHelperFolder(path.to_path_buf()));
+            match fs::create_dir(path) {
+                Ok(()) => {}
+                Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::AlreadyExists && path.is_dir() => {}
+                Err(e) => return Err(e),
+            }
+            self.world.mark_created(path, ROOT_MODE);
+            Ok(())
+        }
+
+        fn create_run_dir(&self, path: &Path) -> io::Result<()> {
+            self.check("create_run_dir", path);
+            self.world.record(Call::CreateRunDir(path.to_path_buf()));
+            if let Some(kind) = self.world.find_fault(|f| match f {
+                Fault::CreateRunDir(kind) => Some(*kind),
+                _ => None,
+            }) {
+                return Err(injected(kind));
+            }
+            make_run_dir(path)?;
+            self.world.mark_created(path, RUN_DIR_MODE);
+            Ok(())
+        }
+
+        fn list_dir(&self, dir: &Path) -> io::Result<Vec<OsString>> {
+            let mut names = fs::read_dir(dir)?
+                .map(|entry| entry.map(|e| e.file_name()))
+                .collect::<io::Result<Vec<_>>>()?;
+            names.sort();
+            Ok(names)
+        }
+
+        fn remove_file(&self, path: &Path) -> io::Result<()> {
+            self.check("remove_file", path);
+            self.world.record(Call::RemoveFile(path.to_path_buf()));
+            let k = key(path);
+            let faulted = self.world.find_fault(|f| match f {
+                Fault::RemoveFile(p) if key(p) == k => Some(()),
+                _ => None,
+            });
+            if faulted.is_some() {
+                return Err(injected(io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied));
+            }
+            fs::remove_file(path)
+        }
+
+        fn remove_dir(&self, path: &Path) -> io::Result<()> {
+            self.check("remove_dir", path);
+            self.world.record(Call::RemoveDir(path.to_path_buf()));
+            fs::remove_dir(path)
+        }
+
+        fn random_hex(&self) -> String {
+            self.world.next_hex()
+        }
+
+        fn sleep(&self, d: Duration) {
+            self.world.record(Call::Sleep(d));
+            self.world.advance(d);
+        }
+
+        fn now(&self) -> Duration {
+            self.world.now()
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// Which calls a script answers: an argv prefix, the n-th `spawn` / `output` call (0-based), or any.
+    #[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
+    pub enum Match {
+        Prefix(Vec<OsString>),
+        Nth(usize),
+        Any,
+    }
+
+    /// A script's side effect on the world, given the argv.
+    pub type Effect<'w> = Box<dyn Fn(&[OsString]) + Send + Sync + 'w>;
+
+    /// One scripted child; `Default` exits 0 at once with no output and no effect.
+    #[derive(Default)]
+    pub struct Script<'w> {
+        pub exit: i32,
+        pub takes: Duration,
+        pub never_exits: bool,
+        pub spawn_error: Option<io::ErrorKind>,
+        pub stdout: Vec<u8>,
+        pub effect: Option<Effect<'w>>,
+    }
+
+    /// One spawned child: its argv, its script (`None` = no rule), the time it still takes.
+    struct Child<'w> {
+        argv: Vec<OsString>,
+        script: Option<Arc<Script<'w>>>,
+        left: Duration,
+        exited: bool,
+    }
+
+    /// A `ProcessRunner` over scripts; the first matching rule in insertion order wins.
+    pub struct ScriptedRunner<'w> {
+        world: &'w World,
+        rules: Mutex<Vec<(Match, Arc<Script<'w>>)>>,
+        /// `spawn` and `output` calls so far (`Match::Nth`).
+        count: Mutex<usize>,
+        children: Mutex<BTreeMap<u64, Child<'w>>>,
+    }
+
+    impl<'w> ScriptedRunner<'w> {
+        pub fn new(world: &'w World) -> Self {
+            ScriptedRunner {
+                world,
+                rules: Mutex::new(Vec::new()),
+                count: Mutex::new(0),
+                children: Mutex::new(BTreeMap::new()),
+            }
+        }
+
+        pub fn on(&self, m: Match, s: Script<'w>) {
+            lock(&self.rules).push((m, Arc::new(s)));
+        }
+
+        fn pick(&self, argv: &[OsString]) -> Option<Arc<Script<'w>>> {
+            let n = {
+                let mut count = lock(&self.count);
+                let n = *count;
+                *count += 1;
+                n
+            };
+            lock(&self.rules)
+                .iter()
+                .find(|(m, _)| match m {
+                    Match::Prefix(prefix) => argv.starts_with(prefix),
+                    Match::Nth(i) => *i == n,
+                    Match::Any => true,
+                })
+                .map(|(_, s)| Arc::clone(s))
+        }
+    }
+
+    impl ProcessRunner for ScriptedRunner<'_> {
+        fn spawn(&self, argv: &[OsString]) -> io::Result<ChildId> {
+            self.world.record(Call::Spawn(argv.to_vec()));
+            let script = self.pick(argv);
+            if let Some(kind) = script.as_ref().and_then(|s| s.spawn_error) {
+                return Err(injected(kind));
+            }
+            let mut children = lock(&self.children);
+            let id = children.len() as u64 + 1;
+            children.insert(
+                id,
+                Child {
+                    argv: argv.to_vec(),
+                    left: script.as_ref().map_or(Duration::ZERO, |s| s.takes),
+                    script,
+                    exited: false,
+                },
+            );
+            Ok(ChildId(id))
+        }
+
+        /// Advances the clock by what the child still takes (all of `timeout` when it never exits); the effect runs
+        /// when it exits.
+        fn wait(&self, child: ChildId, timeout: Duration) -> io::Result<Option<i32>> {
+            self.world.record(Call::Wait { child, timeout });
+            let (step, outcome, finished) = {
+                let mut children = lock(&self.children);
+                let Some(c) = children.get_mut(&child.0) else {
+                    return Err(io::Error::new(
+                        io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput,
+                        "seams::fake: unknown child",
+                    ));
+                };
+                let exit = c.script.as_ref().map_or(0, |s| s.exit);
+                if c.exited {
+                    return Ok(Some(exit));
+                }
+                if c.script.as_ref().is_some_and(|s| s.never_exits) {
+                    (timeout, None, None)
+                } else if c.left <= timeout {
+                    let step = c.left;
+                    c.left = Duration::ZERO;
+                    c.exited = true;
+                    let finished = c.script.clone().map(|s| (s, c.argv.clone()));
+                    (step, Some(exit), finished)
+                } else {
+                    c.left -= timeout;
+                    (timeout, None, None)
+                }
+            };
+            self.world.advance(step);
+            if let Some((script, argv)) = finished
+                && let Some(effect) = &script.effect
+            {
+                effect(&argv);
+            }
+            Ok(outcome)
+        }
+
+        fn output(
+            &self,
+            argv: &[OsString],
+            timeout: Duration,
+        ) -> io::Result<Option<(i32, Vec<u8>)>> {
+            self.world.record(Call::Output(argv.to_vec()));
+            let Some(script) = self.pick(argv) else {
+                return Ok(Some((0, Vec::new())));
+            };
+            if let Some(kind) = script.spawn_error {
+                return Err(injected(kind));
+            }
+            if script.never_exits || script.takes > timeout {
+                self.world.advance(timeout);
+                return Ok(None);
+            }
+            self.world.advance(script.takes);
+            if let Some(effect) = &script.effect {
+                effect(argv);
+            }
+            Ok(Some((script.exit, script.stdout.clone())))
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// A `Registry` over the world's map (tasks TA-17: `subkey` is the full subkey).
+    pub struct FakeRegistry<'w> {
+        world: &'w World,
+    }
+
+    impl<'w> FakeRegistry<'w> {
+        pub fn new(world: &'w World) -> Self {
+            FakeRegistry { world }
+        }
+
+        pub fn set(
+            &self,
+            subkey: &str,
+            uninstall_string: Option<&str>,
+            install_location: Option<&str>,
+        ) {
+            lock(&self.world.registry).insert(
+                subkey.to_owned(),
+                (
+                    uninstall_string.map(str::to_owned),
+                    install_location.map(str::to_owned),
+                ),
+            );
+        }
+    }
+
+    impl Registry for FakeRegistry<'_> {
+        fn uninstall_values(&self, subkey: &str) -> Option<(Option<String>, Option<String>)> {
+            self.world.record(Call::UninstallValues(subkey.to_owned()));
+            lock(&self.world.registry).get(subkey).cloned()
+        }
+
+        fn key_exists(&self, subkey: &str) -> bool {
+            self.world.record(Call::KeyExists(subkey.to_owned()));
+            lock(&self.world.registry).contains_key(subkey)
+        }
+    }
+}

+//! `seams::tests` (owner T007; plan §7.1).
+
+use super::fake::{Call, Event, FakeRegistry, Fault, Match, Script, ScriptedRunner, TempIo, World};
+use super::{HelperIo, ProcessRunner, Registry};
+use crate::ops::{INNO_KEY_SUFFIX, UNINSTALL_KEY};
+use crate::validate::{Kind, LstatFacts};
+use std::ffi::OsString;
+use std::fs;
+use std::io::{ErrorKind, Write};
+use std::panic::{AssertUnwindSafe, catch_unwind};
+use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
+use std::time::Duration;
+
+fn argv(parts: &[&str]) -> Vec<OsString> {
+    parts.iter().map(OsString::from).collect()
+}
+
+fn secs(s: u64) -> Duration {
+    Duration::from_secs(s)
+}
+
+fn temp() -> tempfile::TempDir {
+    tempfile::tempdir().expect("temp dir")
+}
+
+fn fixture_key(app_id: &str) -> String {
+    format!("{UNINSTALL_KEY}\\{app_id}{INNO_KEY_SUFFIX}")
+}
+
+/// The panic text of `f`, `None` when it returns.
+fn panic_text(f: impl FnOnce()) -> Option<String> {
+    let payload = catch_unwind(AssertUnwindSafe(f)).err()?;
+    let text = payload
+        .downcast_ref::<String>()
+        .cloned()
+        .or_else(|| payload.downcast_ref::<&str>().map(|s| (*s).to_owned()));
+    Some(text.unwrap_or_default())
+}
+
+fn assert_sandbox_panic(what: &str, f: impl FnOnce()) {
+    let text = panic_text(f).unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{what} did not panic"));
+    assert!(
+        text.contains("outside the test sandbox"),
+        "{what} panicked with {text:?}"
+    );
+}
+
+fn create_kind(io: &TempIo<'_>, path: &Path) -> Option<ErrorKind> {
+    io.create_new(path).err().map(|e| e.kind())
+}
+
+fn dir(kind: Kind, uid: u32, mode: u32) -> Option<LstatFacts> {
+    Some(LstatFacts {
+        kind,
+        uid: Some(uid),
+        mode: Some(mode),
+    })
+}
+
+/// The clock advances by `sleep`, by `wait`'s scripted `takes` (split over several waits) and by the full timeout of a
+/// child that never exits; `output` follows the same rules.
+fn clock_cases() {
+    let world = World::new();
+    let base = temp();
+    let io = TempIo::new(&world, base.path());
+    let runner = ScriptedRunner::new(&world);
+    assert_eq!(world.now(), Duration::ZERO);
+    io.sleep(secs(2));
+    assert_eq!(io.now(), secs(2));
+    assert_eq!(world.now(), secs(2));
+    runner.on(
+        Match::Prefix(argv(&["slow"])),
+        Script {
+            exit: 3,
+            takes: secs(3),
+            ..Script::default()
+        },
+    );
+    runner.on(
+        Match::Prefix(argv(&["hang"])),
+        Script {
+            never_exits: true,
+            ..Script::default()
+        },
+    );
+    let slow = runner.spawn(&argv(&["slow", "x"])).expect("spawn slow");
+    assert_eq!(world.now(), secs(2), "spawn takes no time");
+    assert_eq!(runner.wait(slow, secs(1)).expect("wait"), None);
+    assert_eq!(world.now(), secs(3));
+    assert_eq!(runner.wait(slow, secs(10)).expect("wait"), Some(3));
+    assert_eq!(world.now(), secs(5), "only the remaining 2 s of `takes`");
+    assert_eq!(runner.wait(slow, secs(10)).expect("wait"), Some(3));
+    assert_eq!(world.now(), secs(5), "an exited child takes no time");
+    let hang = runner.spawn(&argv(&["hang"])).expect("spawn hang");
+    assert_eq!(runner.wait(hang, secs(7)).expect("wait"), None);
+    assert_eq!(world.now(), secs(12));
+    world.advance(secs(1));
+    assert_eq!(world.now(), secs(13));
+    assert_eq!(
+        runner.output(&argv(&["slow"]), secs(10)).expect("output"),
+        Some((3, Vec::new()))
+    );
+    assert_eq!(world.now(), secs(16));
+    assert_eq!(
+        runner.output(&argv(&["slow"]), secs(1)).expect("output"),
+        None
+    );
+    assert_eq!(world.now(), secs(17));
+    assert_eq!(
+        runner.output(&argv(&["hang"]), secs(2)).expect("output"),
+        None
+    );
+    assert_eq!(world.now(), secs(19));
+}
+
+/// `Event::RemovePath` removes the real path (a file, a directory tree) once the clock reaches its time, not before;
+/// outside every sandbox base it panics and removes nothing.
+fn event_cases() {
+    let world = World::new();
+    let base = temp();
+    let io = TempIo::new(&world, base.path());
+    let file = base.path().join("unins000.exe");
+    let tree = base.path().join("Alpha.vst3");
+    fs::write(&file, b"x").expect("write");
+    fs::create_dir_all(tree.join("Contents")).expect("mkdir");
+    fs::write(tree.join("Contents").join("f"), b"y").expect("write");
+    world.at(secs(3), Event::RemovePath(file.clone()));
+    world.at(secs(3), Event::RemovePath(tree.clone()));
+    world.advance(secs(2));
+    assert!(file.exists() && tree.exists(), "not before 3 s");
+    world.advance(Duration::from_millis(999));
+    assert!(file.exists() && tree.exists(), "not at 2.999 s");
+    world.advance(Duration::from_millis(2));
+    assert!(!file.exists(), "removed after 3 s");
+    assert!(!tree.exists(), "the tree removed after 3 s");
+    let late = base.path().join("late");
+    fs::write(&late, b"z").expect("write");
+    world.at(secs(5), Event::RemovePath(late.clone()));
+    io.sleep(secs(1));
+    assert!(late.exists());
+    io.sleep(secs(1));
+    assert!(!late.exists(), "sleep fires events too");
+
+    let other = temp();
+    let keep = other.path().join("keep");
+    fs::write(&keep, b"k").expect("write");
+    world.at(world.now(), Event::RemovePath(keep.clone()));
+    assert_sandbox_panic("Event::RemovePath outside the base", || {
+        world.advance(Duration::ZERO)
+    });
+    assert!(keep.exists(), "nothing removed outside the sandbox");
+    world.advance(secs(1));
+    assert!(keep.exists(), "a fired event does not fire again");
+}
+
+/// `FakeRegistry` reads the world's map; `Event::RemoveKey` empties the entry.
+fn registry_cases() {
+    let world = World::new();
+    let reg = FakeRegistry::new(&world);
+    let key = fixture_key("{AAAAAAAA-BBBB-CCCC-DDDD-EEEEEEEEEEEE}");
+    let bare = fixture_key("{11111111-2222-3333-4444-555555555555}");
+    assert_eq!(reg.uninstall_values(&key), None);
+    assert!(!reg.key_exists(&key));
+    reg.set(&key, Some("unins000.exe"), Some("install"));
+    reg.set(&bare, None, None);
+    assert_eq!(
+        reg.uninstall_values(&key),
+        Some((Some("unins000.exe".to_owned()), Some("install".to_owned())))
+    );
+    assert_eq!(reg.uninstall_values(&bare), Some((None, None)));
+    assert!(reg.key_exists(&key) && reg.key_exists(&bare));
+    world.at(secs(1), Event::RemoveKey(key.clone()));
+    world.advance(Duration::from_millis(500));
+    assert!(reg.key_exists(&key), "not before 1 s");
+    world.advance(Duration::from_millis(600));
+    assert!(!reg.key_exists(&key));
+    assert_eq!(reg.uninstall_values(&key), None);
+    assert!(reg.key_exists(&bare), "only its own key");
+}

+/// `Match::Nth` counts `spawn` and `output` together; the first matching rule wins; no rule exits 0; a spawn error;
+/// the effect runs, with the argv, when the child exits.
+fn runner_cases() {
+    let world = World::new();
+    let base = temp();
+    let runner = ScriptedRunner::new(&world);
+    runner.on(
+        Match::Nth(1),
+        Script {
+            exit: 9,
+            stdout: b"second".to_vec(),
+            ..Script::default()
+        },
+    );
+    runner.on(
+        Match::Any,
+        Script {
+            exit: 4,
+            ..Script::default()
+        },
+    );
+    let first = runner.spawn(&argv(&["p"])).expect("spawn");
+    assert_eq!(runner.wait(first, secs(1)).expect("wait"), Some(4));
+    assert_eq!(
+        runner.output(&argv(&["q"]), secs(1)).expect("output"),
+        Some((9, b"second".to_vec()))
+    );
+    let third = runner.spawn(&argv(&["p"])).expect("spawn");
+    assert_eq!(runner.wait(third, secs(1)).expect("wait"), Some(4));
+
+    let made = base.path().join("made");
+    let seen = made.clone();
+    let runner = ScriptedRunner::new(&world);
+    runner.on(
+        Match::Prefix(argv(&["tool", "--a"])),
+        Script {
+            exit: 5,
+            ..Script::default()
+        },
+    );
+    runner.on(
+        Match::Prefix(argv(&["missing"])),
+        Script {
+            spawn_error: Some(ErrorKind::NotFound),
+            ..Script::default()
+        },
+    );
+    runner.on(
+        Match::Prefix(argv(&["make"])),
+        Script {
+            takes: secs(1),
+            effect: Some(Box::new(move |args: &[OsString]| {
+                fs::write(&made, args.len().to_string()).expect("effect write");
+            })),
+            ..Script::default()
+        },
+    );
+    let a = runner.spawn(&argv(&["tool", "--a", "x"])).expect("spawn");
+    assert_eq!(runner.wait(a, secs(1)).expect("wait"), Some(5));
+    let b = runner.spawn(&argv(&["tool", "--b"])).expect("spawn");
+    assert_eq!(runner.wait(b, secs(1)).expect("wait"), Some(0), "no rule");
+    assert_eq!(
+        runner.spawn(&argv(&["missing"])).err().map(|e| e.kind()),
+        Some(ErrorKind::NotFound)
+    );
+    assert_eq!(
+        runner
+            .output(&argv(&["missing"]), secs(1))
+            .err()
+            .map(|e| e.kind()),
+        Some(ErrorKind::NotFound)
+    );
+    let m = runner.spawn(&argv(&["make", "it"])).expect("spawn");
+    assert!(!seen.exists(), "no effect before the exit");
+    assert_eq!(
+        runner.wait(m, Duration::from_millis(500)).expect("wait"),
+        None
+    );
+    assert!(!seen.exists(), "no effect while running");
+    assert_eq!(runner.wait(m, secs(1)).expect("wait"), Some(0));
+    assert_eq!(fs::read_to_string(&seen).expect("effect ran"), "2");
+}
+
+/// `Fault::CreateNew` hits only its file name; `WriteAfter` fails after exactly N bytes; `RemoveFile`, `CreateRunDir`;
+/// `clear_faults`.
+fn fault_cases() {
+    let world = World::new();
+    let base = temp();
+    let io = TempIo::new(&world, base.path());
+    let sub = base.path().join("sub");
+    fs::create_dir(&sub).expect("mkdir");
+    world.fault(Fault::CreateNew {
+        name: "r.json".into(),
+        kind: ErrorKind::PermissionDenied,
+    });
+    assert_eq!(
+        create_kind(&io, &base.path().join("r.json")),
+        Some(ErrorKind::PermissionDenied)
+    );
+    assert_eq!(
+        create_kind(&io, &sub.join("r.json")),
+        Some(ErrorKind::PermissionDenied)
+    );
+    assert!(!base.path().join("r.json").exists());
+    assert_eq!(create_kind(&io, &base.path().join("other.json")), None);
+    assert_eq!(
+        create_kind(&io, &base.path().join("other.json")),
+        Some(ErrorKind::AlreadyExists),
+        "create_new fails on an existing name"
+    );
+
+    world.fault(Fault::WriteAfter {
+        name: "w.bin".into(),
+        bytes: 5,
+        kind: ErrorKind::StorageFull,
+    });
+    {
+        let mut w = io.create_new(&base.path().join("w.bin")).expect("create");
+        let e = w.write_all(b"0123456789").expect_err("a write fault");
+        assert_eq!(e.kind(), ErrorKind::StorageFull);
+    }
+    assert_eq!(fs::read(base.path().join("w.bin")).expect("read"), b"01234");
+    {
+        let mut w = io.create_new(&sub.join("w.bin")).expect("create");
+        w.write_all(b"abcde").expect("exactly N bytes fit");
+        w.sync_all().expect("sync");
+        let e = w.write_all(b"f").expect_err("byte N + 1 fails");
+        assert_eq!(e.kind(), ErrorKind::StorageFull);
+    }
+    {
+        let mut w = io
+            .create_new(&base.path().join("free.bin"))
+            .expect("create");
+        w.write_all(&[7u8; 64]).expect("no fault on another name");
+    }
+
+    let doomed = base.path().join("doomed");
+    fs::write(&doomed, b"d").expect("write");
+    world.fault(Fault::RemoveFile(doomed.clone()));
+    assert!(io.remove_file(&doomed).is_err());
+    assert!(doomed.exists());
+    world.fault(Fault::CreateRunDir(ErrorKind::PermissionDenied));
+    assert_eq!(
+        io.create_run_dir(&base.path().join("run-f"))
+            .err()
+            .map(|e| e.kind()),
+        Some(ErrorKind::PermissionDenied)
+    );
+    assert!(!base.path().join("run-f").exists());
+
+    world.clear_faults();
+    assert_eq!(create_kind(&io, &base.path().join("r.json")), None);
+    io.remove_file(&doomed)
+        .expect("no fault after clear_faults");
+    io.create_run_dir(&base.path().join("run-f"))
+        .expect("no fault after clear_faults");
+}
+
+/// `root_owned(d)`: `d` and every directory `TempIo` creates under it report uid 0, `0o755`, a run directory `0o700`,
+/// by the given or the canonical spelling; `owner` overrides apply; the real kind; an absent path is `None`.
+fn owner_cases() {
+    let world = World::new();
+    let base = temp();
+    let io = TempIo::new(&world, base.path());
+    let d = base.path().join("lib");
+    fs::create_dir(&d).expect("mkdir");
+    world.root_owned(&d);
+    assert_eq!(io.lstat(&d).expect("lstat"), dir(Kind::Dir, 0, 0o755));
+    let canonical_d = io.canonicalize(&d).expect("canonicalize");
+    assert_eq!(
+        io.lstat(&canonical_d).expect("lstat"),
+        dir(Kind::Dir, 0, 0o755)
+    );
+    let helper = d.join("helper");
+    io.create_helper_folder(&helper).expect("helper folder");
+    io.create_helper_folder(&helper)
+        .expect("helper folder when present");
+    assert_eq!(io.lstat(&helper).expect("lstat"), dir(Kind::Dir, 0, 0o755));
+    let run = helper.join("run-0001");
+    io.create_run_dir(&run).expect("run dir");
+    assert_eq!(io.lstat(&run).expect("lstat"), dir(Kind::Dir, 0, 0o700));
+    assert!(
+        io.create_run_dir(&run).is_err(),
+        "fails on an existing name"
+    );
+    let canonical_run = canonical_d.join("run-0002");
+    io.create_run_dir(&canonical_run)
+        .expect("run dir, canonical");
+    assert_eq!(
+        io.lstat(&d.join("run-0002")).expect("lstat"),
+        dir(Kind::Dir, 0, 0o700)
+    );
+
+    let f = base.path().join("f.txt");
+    fs::write(&f, b"f").expect("write");
+    world.owner(&f, 501, 0o644);
+    assert_eq!(io.lstat(&f).expect("lstat"), dir(Kind::File, 501, 0o644));
+    world.owner(&d, 0, 0o775);
+    assert_eq!(io.lstat(&d).expect("lstat"), dir(Kind::Dir, 0, 0o775));
+    assert_eq!(io.lstat(&base.path().join("absent")).expect("lstat"), None);
+    let plain = io.lstat(base.path()).expect("lstat").expect("present");
+    assert_eq!(plain.kind, Kind::Dir);
+    #[cfg(windows)]
+    assert_eq!((plain.uid, plain.mode), (None, None), "no uid on Windows");
+    #[cfg(unix)]
+    assert!(plain.uid.is_some() && plain.mode.is_some());
+}
+
+/// Every mutating call and every open outside `base` (or on `base` itself, or through `..`) panics; `canonicalize`,
+/// `lstat` and `list_dir` do not.
+fn sandbox_cases() {
+    let world = World::new();
+    let base = temp();
+    let io = TempIo::new(&world, base.path());
+    let other = temp();
+    let outside = other.path().join("x");
+    fs::write(&outside, b"keep").expect("write");
+    let o = other.path();
+    assert_sandbox_panic("create_new", || {
+        let _ = io.create_new(&o.join("new"));
+    });
+    assert_sandbox_panic("create_helper_folder", || {
+        let _ = io.create_helper_folder(&o.join("h"));
+    });
+    assert_sandbox_panic("create_run_dir", || {
+        let _ = io.create_run_dir(&o.join("run-x"));
+    });
+    assert_sandbox_panic("remove_file", || {
+        let _ = io.remove_file(&outside);
+    });
+    assert_sandbox_panic("remove_dir", || {
+        let _ = io.remove_dir(o);
+    });
+    assert_sandbox_panic("remove_dir of base", || {
+        let _ = io.remove_dir(base.path());
+    });
+    assert_sandbox_panic("open_source", || {
+        let _ = io.open_source(&outside);
+    });
+    assert_sandbox_panic("read_bounded", || {
+        let _ = io.read_bounded(&outside, 10);
+    });
+    assert_sandbox_panic("read_tail", || {
+        let _ = io.read_tail(&outside, 10);
+    });
+    let escape: PathBuf = base.path().join("..").join("escape");
+    assert_sandbox_panic("create_new through ..", || {
+        let _ = io.create_new(&escape);
+    });
+    assert!(outside.exists() && !o.join("new").exists() && !o.join("h").exists());
+    assert!(!o.join("run-x").exists() && !escape.exists());
+
+    assert!(io.canonicalize(o).is_ok());
+    assert_eq!(
+        io.lstat(&outside).expect("lstat").map(|f| f.kind),
+        Some(Kind::File)
+    );
+    fs::write(o.join("a"), b"a").expect("write");
+    assert_eq!(
+        io.list_dir(o).expect("list_dir"),
+        vec![OsString::from("a"), OsString::from("x")]
+    );
+}
+
+/// `calls()` records in order; `spawns()` and `removals()` filter it; `random_hex` is counter-based.
+fn record_cases() {
+    let world = World::new();
+    let base = temp();
+    let io = TempIo::new(&world, base.path());
+    let runner = ScriptedRunner::new(&world);
+    let reg = FakeRegistry::new(&world);
+    let file = base.path().join("c.json");
+    let run = base.path().join("run-1");
+    let folder = base.path().join("hf");
+    io.sleep(secs(1));
+    drop(io.create_new(&file).expect("create"));
+    let child = runner.spawn(&argv(&["a"])).expect("spawn");
+    runner.wait(child, secs(2)).expect("wait");
+    runner.output(&argv(&["b"]), secs(3)).expect("output");
+    io.create_run_dir(&run).expect("run dir");
+    io.create_helper_folder(&folder).expect("folder");
+    io.remove_file(&file).expect("remove");
+    io.remove_dir(&run).expect("remove dir");
+    reg.uninstall_values("k");
+    reg.key_exists("k");
+    assert_eq!(
+        world.calls(),
+        vec![
+            Call::Sleep(secs(1)),
+            Call::CreateNew(file.clone()),
+            Call::Spawn(argv(&["a"])),
+            Call::Wait {
+                child,
+                timeout: secs(2)
+            },
+            Call::Output(argv(&["b"])),
+            Call::CreateRunDir(run.clone()),
+            Call::CreateHelperFolder(folder),
+            Call::RemoveFile(file.clone()),
+            Call::RemoveDir(run.clone()),
+            Call::UninstallValues("k".to_owned()),
+            Call::KeyExists("k".to_owned()),
+        ]
+    );
+    assert_eq!(world.spawns(), vec![argv(&["a"])]);
+    assert_eq!(world.removals(), vec![file, run]);
+
+    let first = io.random_hex();
+    let second = io.random_hex();
+    for h in [&first, &second] {
+        assert_eq!(h.len(), 32);
+        assert!(
+            h.chars()
+                .all(|c| c.is_ascii_digit() || ('a'..='f').contains(&c))
+        );
+    }
+    assert_ne!(first, second);
+    let again = World::new();
+    assert_eq!(TempIo::new(&again, base.path()).random_hex(), first);
+}
+
+/// The core's loopback holds the fakes behind `Elevation: Sync` (plan §4.1.9).
+fn assert_sync<T: Sync>() {}
+
+#[test]
+fn fake_world_cases() {
+    assert_sync::<World>();
+    assert_sync::<TempIo<'_>>();
+    assert_sync::<ScriptedRunner<'_>>();
+    assert_sync::<FakeRegistry<'_>>();
+    clock_cases();
+    event_cases();
+    registry_cases();
+    runner_cases();
+    fault_cases();
+    owner_cases();
+    sandbox_cases();
+    record_cases();
+}
