+//! SC-011 (D1, PD-11): jobs the core writes parse under the helper's schema for each op's own OS, their hashes agree,
+//! a result the helper writes maps through the core's FR-076 reader, one changed byte either way fails, and the core's
+//! copies of the helper's codes, timeouts and state-dir name equal the helper's.
+//!
+//! The fakes (`seams::fake`) exist only with the helper's `test-support` feature (PD-8), which `cargo test --workspace`
+//! turns on through core's dev-dependency; a `-p krate-manager-helper` run needs `--features test-support`, else this
+//! file compiles to no test.
+#![cfg(feature = "test-support")]
+
+use krate_manager_core::install::FsInstallIo;
+use krate_manager_core::install::job::{
+    self as core_job, JobOp, OpReport, ResultError, WrittenJob,
+};
+use krate_manager_core::platform::APP_IDENTIFIER;
+use krate_manager_helper::classify::classify_inno;
+use krate_manager_helper::cli::Args;
+use krate_manager_helper::result::{self, OpEntry, OpOutcome, ResultFile};
+use krate_manager_helper::roots::{
+    AllowedRoots, MacosRoots, OsRoots, STATE_DIR_NAME, StateDirRule, WindowsRoots,
+};
+use krate_manager_helper::seams::fake::{
+    FakeRegistry, Match, Script, ScriptedRunner, TempIo, World,
+};
+use krate_manager_helper::seams::{Seams, WriteSync};
+use krate_manager_helper::{Exit, HelperOs, digest, error, ops, run};
+use std::ffi::OsString;
+use std::io;
+use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
+
+/// An in-memory `WriteSync` for `result::write`.
+#[derive(Default)]
+struct Sink(Vec<u8>);
+
+impl io::Write for Sink {
+    fn write(&mut self, buf: &[u8]) -> io::Result<usize> {
+        self.0.extend_from_slice(buf);
+        Ok(buf.len())
+    }
+
+    fn flush(&mut self) -> io::Result<()> {
+        Ok(())
+    }
+}
+
+impl WriteSync for Sink {
+    fn sync_all(&mut self) -> io::Result<()> {
+        Ok(())
+    }
+}
+
+/// The temp layout: `<base>/<APP_IDENTIFIER>/{jobs,staging}` and every root under `<base>`.
+struct Layout {
+    _td: tempfile::TempDir,
+    base: PathBuf,
+    state: PathBuf,
+    staging: PathBuf,
+}
+
+fn mkdirs(dir: &Path) {
+    std::fs::create_dir_all(dir).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("cannot create {}: {e}", dir.display()));
+}
+
+fn layout() -> Layout {
+    let td = tempfile::TempDir::new().expect("cannot create a TempDir");
+    // Canonical from the start: the helper requires canonical job and source paths (FR-020, FR-027).
+    let base = std::fs::canonicalize(td.path()).expect("the TempDir canonicalizes");
+    let state = base.join(APP_IDENTIFIER);
+    let staging = state.join("staging");
+    mkdirs(&state.join(core_job::JOBS_DIR));
+    mkdirs(&staging.join("downloads"));
+    for dir in ["vst3_vendor", "preset_vendor", "run_base"] {
+        mkdirs(&base.join("win").join(dir));
+    }
+    for dir in ["vst3", "components", "presets", "applications", "volume"] {
+        mkdirs(&base.join("mac").join(dir));
+    }
+    mkdirs(&base.join("mac_root"));
+    Layout {
+        _td: td,
+        base,
+        state,
+        staging,
+    }
+}
+
+/// The roots for `os`, every one under the temp base; the state dir is the layout's, exactly.
+fn roots_for(l: &Layout, os: HelperOs) -> AllowedRoots {
+    let win = l.base.join("win");
+    let mac = l.base.join("mac");
+    let os_roots = match os {
+        HelperOs::Windows => OsRoots::Windows(WindowsRoots {
+            vst3_vendor: win.join("vst3_vendor"),
+            preset_vendor: win.join("preset_vendor"),
+            run_base: win.join("run_base"),
+        }),
+        HelperOs::Macos => OsRoots::Macos(MacosRoots {
+            vst3: mac.join("vst3"),
+            components: mac.join("components"),
+            presets: mac.join("presets"),
+            applications: mac.join("applications"),
+            volume: mac.join("volume"),
+            run_base: l.base.join("mac_root").join("helper"),
+            run_ancestors: vec![l.base.join("mac_root")],
+        }),
+        HelperOs::Linux => OsRoots::None,
+    };
+    AllowedRoots {
+        os: os_roots,
+        state_dir: StateDirRule::Exact(l.state.clone()),
+    }
+}
+
+fn args_for(written: &WrittenJob) -> Args {
+    Args {
+        job: written.path.clone(),
+        job_sha256: digest::parse_hex64(&written.sha256)
+            .expect("the core's hash is 64 lower-case hex"),
+        #[cfg(feature = "ci-temp-roots")]
+        ci_roots: None,
+    }
+}
+
+/// A file under staging, as a real download leaves it (Phase 7 layout).
+fn staged(l: &Layout, name: &str, bytes: &[u8]) -> (String, String) {
+    let path = l.staging.join("downloads").join(name);
+    std::fs::write(&path, bytes).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("cannot write {}: {e}", path.display()));
+    let file = path.to_str().expect("a UTF-8 temp path").to_owned();
+    (file, digest::hex(&digest::sha256(bytes)))
+}
+
+/// The first `needle` in `bytes`.
+fn find(bytes: &[u8], needle: &[u8]) -> usize {
+    bytes
+        .windows(needle.len())
+        .position(|w| w == needle)
+        .expect("the needle is present")
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn core_written_jobs_parse() {
+    let l = layout();
+    let world = World::new();
+    // D7's macOS chain: the run directory's ancestors are root-owned (PD-5), on every leg.
+    world.root_owned(&l.base.join("mac_root"));
+    let io = TempIo::new(&world, &l.base);
+    let runner = ScriptedRunner::new(&world);
+    // No receipt: `pkgutil --pkg-info` exits 1 (FR-033).
+    runner.on(
+        Match::Prefix(vec![
+            OsString::from(ops::PKGUTIL),
+            OsString::from("--pkg-info"),
+        ]),
+        Script {
+            exit: 1,
+            ..Script::default()
+        },
+    );
+    // Every other child exits 0 at once; an Inno setup leaves its `/LOG=` file, as a real one does.
+    runner.on(
+        Match::Any,
+        Script {
+            effect: Some(Box::new(|argv: &[OsString]| {
+                for arg in argv {
+                    if let Some(log) = arg.to_str().and_then(|a| a.strip_prefix("/LOG=")) {
+                        std::fs::write(log, b"Log opened.\r\nInstallation process succeeded.\r\n")
+                            .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("cannot write the fake log {log}: {e}"));
+                    }
+                }
+            })),
+            ..Script::default()
+        },
+    );
+    let registry = FakeRegistry::new(&world);
+    let seams = Seams {
+        io: &io,
+        runner: &runner,
+        registry: &registry,
+        classify: classify_inno,
+    };
+
+    let (exe, exe_sha) = staged(&l, "a.exe", b"MZ fixture inno setup");
+    let (pkg, pkg_sha) = staged(&l, "a.pkg", b"xar! fixture pkg");
+    // Each of the four kinds, with its own OS (D18: both values in the one test, on every leg), and the outcome the
+    // helper's validation and the fakes lead to: the installers run their copies, the uninstalls pass their id checks
+    // and meet no key / no receipt.
+    type Expect = fn(&OpReport) -> bool;
+    let cases: [(JobOp, HelperOs, Expect); 4] = [
+        (
+            JobOp::RunInnoInstaller {
+                file: exe.clone(),
+                sha256: exe_sha.clone(),
+            },
+            HelperOs::Windows,
+            |r| *r == OpReport::Done,
+        ),
+        (
+            JobOp::RunInnoUninstaller {
+                app_id: "{0A1B2C3D-0000-4000-8000-00000000A1FA}".to_owned(),
+            },
+            HelperOs::Windows,
+            |r| {
+                matches!(
+                    r,
+                    OpReport::Failed {
+                        code: "E_HELPER_NO_KEY",
+                        ..
+                    }
+                )
+            },
+        ),
+        (
+            JobOp::RunPkgInstaller {
+                file: pkg.clone(),
+                sha256: pkg_sha.clone(),
+            },
+            HelperOs::Macos,
+            |r| *r == OpReport::Done,
+        ),
+        (
+            JobOp::UninstallPkg {
+                pkg_id: "com.krateaudio.alpha".to_owned(),
+            },
+            HelperOs::Macos,
+            |r| {
+                matches!(
+                    r,
+                    OpReport::Failed {
+                        code: "E_HELPER_PKG_RECEIPT",
+                        ..
+                    }
+                )
+            },
+        ),
+    ];
+
+    let mut good_result: Option<(WrittenJob, Vec<u8>)> = None;
+    for (op, os, expect) in cases {
+        let written = core_job::write(&FsInstallIo, &l.state, vec![op.clone()])
+            .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("the core writes {op:?}: {e}"));
+        assert_eq!(written.ops, vec![op.clone()]);
+        // The core's hash is the hash of the bytes on disk.
+        let job_bytes = std::fs::read(&written.path).expect("the job is on disk");
+        assert_eq!(
+            written.sha256,
+            digest::hex(&digest::sha256(&job_bytes)),
+            "{op:?}"
+        );
+
+        let exit = run(&args_for(&written), os, &roots_for(&l, os), &seams);
+        assert_eq!(
+            exit,
+            Exit {
+                status: 0,
+                code: None
+            },
+            "{op:?} on {os:?}"
+        );
+        // The result lies where the core looks for it, and binds to the core's hash.
+        let bytes = std::fs::read(&written.result)
+            .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("no result at {}: {e}", written.result.display()));
+        let value: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).expect("the result is JSON");
+        assert_eq!(
+            value["job_error"],
+            serde_json::Value::Null,
+            "{op:?}: {value}"
+        );
+        assert_eq!(value["job_sha256"], written.sha256.as_str(), "{op:?}");
+        let reports = core_job::read_result(&bytes, &written).unwrap_or_else(|e| {
+            panic!("the core reads the helper's result for {op:?}: {e:?} {value}")
+        });
+        assert_eq!(reports.len(), 1, "{op:?}");
+        assert!(expect(&reports[0]), "{op:?} on {os:?}: {reports:?}");
+        good_result.get_or_insert((written, bytes));
+    }
+
+    // One changed byte in the job: the hash binding refuses it (exit 3) and the core reads the refusal.
+    let written = core_job::write(
+        &FsInstallIo,
+        &l.state,
+        vec![JobOp::UninstallPkg {
+            pkg_id: "com.krateaudio.alpha".to_owned(),
+        }],
+    )
+    .expect("the core writes the job");
+    let mut job_bytes = std::fs::read(&written.path).expect("the job is on disk");
+    let at = find(&job_bytes, b"alpha");
+    job_bytes[at] = b'b';
+    std::fs::write(&written.path, &job_bytes).expect("the job is rewritten");
+    let exit = run(
+        &args_for(&written),
+        HelperOs::Macos,
+        &roots_for(&l, HelperOs::Macos),
+        &seams,
+    );
+    assert_eq!(
+        exit,
+        Exit {
+            status: 3,
+            code: Some("E_HELPER_JOB_HASH")
+        }
+    );
+    let bytes = std::fs::read(&written.result).expect("an exit-3 result is written");
+    let value: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).expect("the result is JSON");
+    assert_eq!(value["job_error"], "E_HELPER_JOB_HASH");
+    assert_eq!(value["ops"], serde_json::json!([]));
+    assert_eq!(
+        core_job::read_result(&bytes, &written),
+        Err(ResultError::Helper("E_HELPER_JOB_HASH"))
+    );
+
+    // One changed byte in a good result: the core refuses it.
+    let (written, mut bytes) = good_result.expect("a good result was read");
+    let at = find(&bytes, written.sha256.as_bytes());
+    bytes[at] = if bytes[at] == b'0' { b'1' } else { b'0' };
+    assert_eq!(
+        core_job::read_result(&bytes, &written),
+        Err(ResultError::NoResult)
+    );
+
+    // `result::write`'s output maps through the core's reader, every outcome kind once.
+    let written = core_job::write(
+        &FsInstallIo,
+        &l.state,
+        vec![
+            JobOp::RunInnoInstaller {
+                file: exe.clone(),
+                sha256: exe_sha.clone(),
+            },
+            JobOp::RunInnoInstaller {
+                file: exe,
+                sha256: exe_sha,
+            },
+            JobOp::RunPkgInstaller {
+                file: pkg,
+                sha256: pkg_sha,
+            },
+            JobOp::RunInnoUninstaller {
+                app_id: "{0A1B2C3D-0000-4000-8000-00000000A1FA}".to_owned(),
+            },
+            JobOp::UninstallPkg {
+                pkg_id: "com.krateaudio.alpha".to_owned(),
+            },
+        ],
+    )
+    .expect("the core writes the job");
+    let entry = |index: u32, op: &'static str, outcome, code, exit_code| OpEntry {
+        index,
+        op,
+        outcome,
+        code,
+        exit_code,
+        seconds: 1.25,
+        log_tail: vec!["a line".to_owned()],
+    };
+    let file = ResultFile {
+        result_version: 1,
+        job_sha256: written.sha256.clone(),
+        job_error: None,
+        ops: vec![
+            entry(0, "run_inno_installer", OpOutcome::Done, None, Some(0)),
+            entry(
+                1,
+                "run_inno_installer",
+                OpOutcome::Locked,
+                Some(error::LOCKED.code),
+                Some(5),
+            ),
+            entry(
+                2,
+                "run_pkg_installer",
+                OpOutcome::Failed,
+                Some(error::EXIT.code),
+                Some(1),
+            ),
+            entry(
+                3,
+                "run_inno_uninstaller",
+                OpOutcome::Refused,
+                Some(error::APP_ID.code),
+                None,
+            ),
+            entry(
+                4,
+                "uninstall_pkg",
+                OpOutcome::NotRun,
+                Some(error::AFTER_TIMEOUT.code),
+                None,
+            ),
+        ],
+    };
+    let mut sink = Sink::default();
+    result::write(&mut sink, &file).expect("the result serializes");
+    assert_eq!(
+        core_job::read_result(&sink.0, &written),
+        Ok(vec![
+            OpReport::Done,
+            OpReport::Locked,
+            OpReport::Failed {
+                code: "E_HELPER_EXIT",
+                exit_code: Some(1)
+            },
+            OpReport::Refused {
+                code: "E_HELPER_APP_ID"
+            },
+            OpReport::NotRun {
+                code: "E_HELPER_AFTER_TIMEOUT"
+            },
+        ])
+    );
+    // The same file with one byte changed in its hash is refused.
+    let mut changed = sink.0.clone();
+    let at = find(&changed, written.sha256.as_bytes());
+    changed[at] = if changed[at] == b'0' { b'1' } else { b'0' };
+    assert_eq!(
+        core_job::read_result(&changed, &written),
+        Err(ResultError::NoResult)
+    );
+
+    // PD-11: the core's copies of the code catalogue, the timeouts and the state-dir name.
+    for code in error::CODES {
+        assert_eq!(core_job::helper_code(code), Some(code), "{code}");
+    }
+    assert_eq!(core_job::helper_code("E_HELPER_NOT_A_CODE"), None);
+    assert_eq!(core_job::INNO_INSTALL_TIMEOUT, ops::INNO_INSTALL_TIMEOUT);
+    assert_eq!(core_job::PKG_INSTALL_TIMEOUT, ops::PKG_INSTALL_TIMEOUT);
+    assert_eq!(
+        core_job::UNINSTALL_POLL_TIMEOUT,
+        ops::UNINSTALL_POLL_TIMEOUT
+    );
+    assert_eq!(
+        core_job::UNINSTALL_POLL_INTERVAL,
+        ops::UNINSTALL_POLL_INTERVAL
+    );
+    assert_eq!(core_job::PKGUTIL_TIMEOUT, ops::PKGUTIL_TIMEOUT);
+    assert_eq!(STATE_DIR_NAME, APP_IDENTIFIER);
+}

+//! SC-023 (FR-005, D15): the built helper refuses every malformed argv with exit 2, empty stdout, the one usage line
+//! on stderr, and writes nothing. No row passes a valid argv, so no job is ever read and nothing runs.
+
+use krate_manager_helper::cli::USAGE;
+use std::fs;
+use std::process::Command;
+
+const HEX: &str = "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef";
+
+#[test]
+fn helper_usage_exits_2() {
+    let cases: [&[&str]; 4] = [
+        &[],
+        &["--anything"],
+        &["--job", "job.json"],
+        &["--job", "a.json", "--job", "b.json", "--job-sha256", HEX],
+    ];
+    for args in cases {
+        let dir = tempfile::TempDir::new().expect("a temp working directory");
+        let output = Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_krate-manager-helper"))
+            .args(args)
+            .current_dir(dir.path())
+            .output()
+            .expect("the helper binary runs");
+        assert_eq!(output.status.code(), Some(2), "exit code for {args:?}");
+        assert!(output.stdout.is_empty(), "stdout is empty for {args:?}");
+        assert_eq!(
+            String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr),
+            format!("{USAGE}\n"),
+            "exactly one usage line on stderr for {args:?}"
+        );
+        let left = fs::read_dir(dir.path())
+            .expect("the working directory lists")
+            .count();
+        assert_eq!(
+            left, 0,
+            "nothing created in the working directory for {args:?}"
+        );
+    }
+}

+#!/usr/bin/env node
+// Builds the Krate Manager bundle with its elevated helper as a Tauri sidecar (spec FR-092, FR-104;
+// plan §4.6, PD-20): `cargo build -p krate-manager-helper --release --locked` (Windows and macOS only;
+// no Linux helper binary is ever built, D18), copies it to
+// `src-tauri/binaries/krate-manager-helper-<host tuple>[.exe]`, then runs `tauri build` with
+// `src-tauri/tauri.helper.conf.json` (Windows, macOS) or plain `tauri build` (Linux).
+// Child stdio is inherited and a failing step's exit code is this script's exit code.
+//
+// usage: node scripts/bundle.js
+import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import { copyFileSync, mkdirSync } from 'node:fs';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import process from 'node:process';
+import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
+
+const USAGE = 'usage: node scripts/bundle.js';
+const HELPER = 'krate-manager-helper';
+const APP_ROOT = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, '..');
+
+/**
+ * The sidecar file name Tauri's `externalBin` entry `binaries/krate-manager-helper` looks for:
+ * the base name, `-`, the target tuple, and `.exe` on a Windows tuple.
+ * @param {string} hostTuple a Rust target tuple, e.g. `x86_64-pc-windows-msvc`
+ * @returns {string}
+ */
+export function sidecarName(hostTuple) {
+  return `${HELPER}-${hostTuple}${exeSuffix(hostTuple)}`;
+}
+
+/**
+ * The `tauri` CLI arguments for `platform` (a `process.platform` value): the helper config off
+ * Linux, plain `build` on Linux (no helper is bundled there).
+ * @param {string} platform
+ * @returns {string[]}
+ */
+export function tauriArgs(platform) {
+  return platform === 'linux' ? ['build'] : ['build', '--config', 'src-tauri/tauri.helper.conf.json'];
+}
+
+/**
+ * @param {string} tuple
+ * @returns {string} `.exe` for a Windows tuple, else empty
+ */
+function exeSuffix(tuple) {
+  return tuple.includes('-windows-') ? '.exe' : '';
+}
+
+/**
+ * @param {string} message
+ * @returns {never}
+ */
+function fail(message) {
+  process.stderr.write(`bundle: ${message}\n`);
+  process.exit(1);
+}
+
+/**
+ * Runs `command` in the app root with inherited stdio; exits with its code when it fails.
+ * @param {string} command
+ * @param {string[]} args
+ */
+function run(command, args) {
+  const r = spawnSync(command, args, { cwd: APP_ROOT, stdio: 'inherit' });
+  if (r.error !== undefined) fail(`cannot run ${command}: ${r.error.message}`);
+  const status = r.status ?? 1;
+  if (status !== 0) process.exit(status);
+}
+
+/** @returns {string} the tuple `rustc --print host-tuple` prints */
+function hostTuple() {
+  const r = spawnSync('rustc', ['--print', 'host-tuple'], {
+    cwd: APP_ROOT,
+    encoding: 'utf8',
+    stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'inherit'],
+  });
+  if (r.error !== undefined) fail(`cannot run rustc: ${r.error.message}`);
+  const status = r.status ?? 1;
+  if (status !== 0) process.exit(status);
+  const tuple = r.stdout.trim();
+  if (tuple === '') fail('rustc --print host-tuple printed nothing');
+  return tuple;
+}
+
+function main() {
+  if (process.argv.length > 2) {
+    process.stderr.write(`${USAGE}\n`);
+    process.exit(2);
+  }
+  if (process.platform !== 'linux') {
+    const tuple = hostTuple();
+    run('cargo', ['build', '-p', HELPER, '--release', '--locked']);
+    // cargo honours CARGO_TARGET_DIR, so the built helper is read from there when it is set.
+    const targetDir = path.resolve(APP_ROOT, process.env.CARGO_TARGET_DIR ?? 'target');
+    const built = path.join(targetDir, 'release', `${HELPER}${exeSuffix(tuple)}`);
+    const binaries = path.join(APP_ROOT, 'src-tauri', 'binaries');
+    mkdirSync(binaries, { recursive: true });
+    copyFileSync(built, path.join(binaries, sidecarName(tuple)));
+  }
+  // The local `tauri` CLI entry point (`@tauri-apps/cli` package.json `bin.tauri`) run with this
+  // Node: what `npx tauri` resolves to, without a shell (a `.cmd` shim needs one on Windows).
+  const cli = path.join(APP_ROOT, 'node_modules', '@tauri-apps', 'cli', 'tauri.js');
+  run(process.execPath, [cli, ...tauriArgs(process.platform)]);
+}
+
+const entry = process.argv.at(1);
+if (entry !== undefined && import.meta.url === pathToFileURL(entry).href) main();

+// node:test coverage of bundle.js's path logic (spec FR-092, FR-109; plan §4.6, PD-20).
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import test from 'node:test';
+
+import { sidecarName, tauriArgs } from './bundle.js';
+
+await test('bundle sidecar name per host tuple', () => {
+  assert.equal(
+    sidecarName('x86_64-pc-windows-msvc'),
+    'krate-manager-helper-x86_64-pc-windows-msvc.exe',
+  );
+  assert.equal(sidecarName('aarch64-pc-windows-msvc'), 'krate-manager-helper-aarch64-pc-windows-msvc.exe');
+  assert.equal(sidecarName('aarch64-apple-darwin'), 'krate-manager-helper-aarch64-apple-darwin');
+  assert.equal(sidecarName('x86_64-apple-darwin'), 'krate-manager-helper-x86_64-apple-darwin');
+  assert.equal(sidecarName('x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu'), 'krate-manager-helper-x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu');
+
+  const withHelper = ['build', '--config', 'src-tauri/tauri.helper.conf.json'];
+  assert.deepEqual(tauriArgs('win32'), withHelper);
+  assert.deepEqual(tauriArgs('darwin'), withHelper);
+  assert.deepEqual(tauriArgs('linux'), ['build']);
+});

+// node:test: the CI fixture Inno script keeps the plugin installers' behaviour-deciding lines
+// (spec FR-101; plan §4.6). Both scripts are normalised by their own `#define AppName "<name>"` line,
+// so this source names neither the plugin nor the fixture (the roster scan, `fixture_names_confined`).
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import test from 'node:test';
+
+const APP_ROOT = path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, '..');
+const REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(APP_ROOT, '..', '..');
+const FIXTURE_ISS = path.join(APP_ROOT, 'tests', 'fixtures', 'inno', 'fixture.iss');
+const PLUGIN_ISS = path.join(REPO_ROOT, 'plugins', 'iterum', 'installers', 'windows', 'setup.iss');
+
+/** The `[Setup]` directives whose whole line must be equal (`setup.iss:50`, `:53`, `:63-64`). */
+const DIRECTIVES = [
+  'PrivilegesRequired',
+  'UsePreviousAppDir',
+  'ArchitecturesAllowed',
+  'ArchitecturesInstallIn64BitMode',
+];
+/** Inno roots no fixture line may name: it must write only under `{#Root}` (FR-111). */
+const BANNED = ['{commonpf}', '{commonappdata}', '{pf}', '{sys}', '{win}'];
+
+/**
+ * `text` split into lines with its AppName replaced by `<APP>`, its AppId by `<APPID>`, and the
+ * plugin roots by the fixture's (`{commonpf}\Common Files` → `{#Root}`, `{commonappdata}` →
+ * `{#Root}\ProgramData`).
+ * @param {string} text
+ * @param {string} label
+ * @returns {string[]}
+ */
+function normalise(text, label) {
+  const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/);
+  const names = lines.flatMap((l) => /^#define AppName "([^"]+)"$/.exec(l)?.slice(1) ?? []);
+  const name = names.at(0);
+  assert.ok(names.length === 1 && name !== undefined, `${label}: want one #define AppName line, got ${String(names.length)}`);
+  return lines.map((l) =>
+    l
+      .split(name)
+      .join('<APP>')
+      .replace(/^AppId=.*$/, 'AppId=<APPID>')
+      .split('{commonpf}\\Common Files')
+      .join('{#Root}')
+      .split('{commonappdata}')
+      .join('{#Root}\\ProgramData'),
+  );
+}
+
+/**
+ * Every entry line (not blank, not a `;` comment) per `[Section]`, with its 0-based line index.
+ * @param {string[]} lines
+ * @returns {Map<string, { index: number, text: string }[]>}
+ */
+function sections(lines) {
+  /** @type {Map<string, { index: number, text: string }[]>} */
+  const out = new Map();
+  /** @type {{ index: number, text: string }[]} */
+  let current = [];
+  lines.forEach((raw, index) => {
+    const text = raw.trim();
+    const header = /^\[([A-Za-z]+)\]$/.exec(text);
+    if (header !== null) {
+      const key = header.at(1) ?? '';
+      current = out.get(key) ?? [];
+      out.set(key, current);
+    } else if (text !== '' && !text.startsWith(';')) {
+      current.push({ index, text });
+    }
+  });
+  return out;
+}
+
+/**
+ * The listed lines of a normalised script: each directive's lines, every `[Files]` entry's
+ * `Flags:` value, every `[UninstallDelete]` entry.
+ * @param {string[]} lines
+ */
+function listed(lines) {
+  const s = sections(lines);
+  const setup = s.get('Setup') ?? [];
+  return {
+    directives: DIRECTIVES.map((key) => setup.filter((e) => e.text.startsWith(`${key}=`)).map((e) => e.text)),
+    flags: (s.get('Files') ?? []).map((e) => /(?:^|;)\s*Flags:\s*([^;]*)/.exec(e.text)?.[1]?.trim() ?? '(no Flags)'),
+    uninstallDelete: (s.get('UninstallDelete') ?? []).map((e) => e.text),
+  };
+}
+
+/**
+ * Every drift of the fixture script from the plugin script, one message per listed line.
+ * @param {string} fixtureText
+ * @param {string} pluginText
+ * @returns {string[]}
+ */
+function drifts(fixtureText, pluginText) {
+  const f = listed(normalise(fixtureText, 'fixture.iss'));
+  const p = listed(normalise(pluginText, 'setup.iss'));
+  const out = [];
+  DIRECTIVES.forEach((key, i) => {
+    const fl = f.directives[i] ?? [];
+    const pl = p.directives[i] ?? [];
+    if (pl.length !== 1) out.push(`${key}: the plugin script has ${String(pl.length)} lines`);
+    if (fl.length !== 1 || fl[0] !== pl[0]) out.push(`${key}: fixture ${JSON.stringify(fl)} vs plugin ${JSON.stringify(pl)}`);
+  });
+  if (p.flags.length === 0) out.push('[Files] Flags: the plugin script has no [Files] entry');
+  for (let i = 0; i < Math.max(f.flags.length, p.flags.length); i++) {
+    const [a, b] = [f.flags.at(i), p.flags.at(i)];
+    if (a !== b) out.push(`[Files] entry ${String(i + 1)} Flags: fixture ${String(a)} vs plugin ${String(b)}`);
+  }
+  if (p.uninstallDelete.length === 0) out.push('[UninstallDelete]: the plugin script has no entry');
+  for (let i = 0; i < Math.max(f.uninstallDelete.length, p.uninstallDelete.length); i++) {
+    const [a, b] = [f.uninstallDelete.at(i), p.uninstallDelete.at(i)];
+    if (a !== b) out.push(`[UninstallDelete] entry ${String(i + 1)}: fixture ${String(a)} vs plugin ${String(b)}`);
+  }
+  fixtureText.split(/\r?\n/).forEach((line, i) => {
+    for (const token of BANNED) {
+      if (line.toLowerCase().includes(token)) out.push(`banned root ${token}: fixture.iss:${String(i + 1)}`);
+    }
+  });
+  return out;
+}
+
+/**
+ * `text` with line `index` (0-based) replaced by `edit(line)`.
+ * @param {string} text
+ * @param {number} index
+ * @param {(line: string) => string} edit
+ */
+function plant(text, index, edit) {
+  const lines = text.split('\n');
+  lines[index] = edit(lines[index] ?? '');
+  return lines.join('\n');
+}
+
+await test('fixture inno script keeps the plugin directives', () => {
+  const fixtureText = readFileSync(FIXTURE_ISS, 'utf8');
+  const pluginText = readFileSync(PLUGIN_ISS, 'utf8');
+  assert.deepEqual(drifts(fixtureText, pluginText), []);
+
+  const fixtureLines = fixtureText.split('\n');
+  const s = sections(fixtureLines.map((l) => l.replace(/\r$/, '')));
+  const setup = s.get('Setup') ?? [];
+  const files = s.get('Files') ?? [];
+  const uninstallDelete = s.get('UninstallDelete') ?? [];
+  assert.ok(files.length > 0 && uninstallDelete.length > 0, 'the fixture has [Files] and [UninstallDelete] entries');
+
+  /** @type {[string, string][]} each planted fixture text and the label its drift must carry */
+  const plants = [];
+  for (const key of DIRECTIVES) {
+    const entry = setup.find((e) => e.text.startsWith(`${key}=`));
+    assert.ok(entry !== undefined, `fixture.iss has ${key}`);
+    plants.push([plant(fixtureText, entry.index, (l) => l.replace('=', '=drift')), `${key}:`]);
+    plants.push([plant(fixtureText, entry.index, () => ''), `${key}:`]);
+  }
+  files.forEach((entry, i) => {
+    plants.push([
+      plant(fixtureText, entry.index, (l) => l.replace(/Flags:\s*/, 'Flags: uninsneveruninstall ')),
+      `[Files] entry ${String(i + 1)} Flags:`,
+    ]);
+  });
+  uninstallDelete.forEach((entry, i) => {
+    plants.push([plant(fixtureText, entry.index, (l) => l.replace('.vst3', '.vst3\\drift')), `[UninstallDelete] entry ${String(i + 1)}:`]);
+  });
+  for (const token of BANNED) {
+    plants.push([`${fixtureText}\n; ${token}\n`, `banned root ${token}:`]);
+    plants.push([`${fixtureText}\n; ${token.toUpperCase()}\n`, `banned root ${token}:`]);
+  }
+  for (const [text, label] of plants) {
+    const found = drifts(text, pluginText);
+    assert.ok(
+      found.some((m) => m.startsWith(label)),
+      `a planted drift labelled "${label}" is reported; got ${JSON.stringify(found)}`,
+    );
+  }
+});

+#!/usr/bin/env node
+// Writes one helper job for the elevated CI jobs (spec FR-001, FR-021, FR-027, FR-100; plan §4.6).
+// CI only: the job lands at `<S>/jobs/job-<32 hex>.json` with FR-001's keys in order (compact JSON),
+// and `<job path> <sha256 of the job bytes>` is printed on stdout. Every id is an argument; this
+// script names no plug-in and no fixture.
+//
+// Windows path form (FR-027, FR-021): the helper accepts only verbatim `\\?\` paths for `file` and
+// `--job`, so on win32 `--state` and every `--file` are canonicalized with `fs.realpathSync.native`
+// and written / printed in verbatim form. Elsewhere every path is used as given.
+//
+// usage: node scripts/write-test-job.js --state <S> (--op <kind> [--file <path> --sha256 <hex> | --app-id <id> | --pkg-id <id>])...
+import { Buffer } from 'node:buffer';
+import { randomBytes, createHash } from 'node:crypto';
+import { mkdirSync, realpathSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import process from 'node:process';
+import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
+
+const USAGE =
+  'usage: node scripts/write-test-job.js --state <S> (--op <kind> [--file <path> --sha256 <hex> | --app-id <id> | --pkg-id <id>])...';
+
+/**
+ * Each op kind's keys after `op`, in FR-001's order, with the flag that supplies each.
+ * @type {Record<string, [string, string][]>}
+ */
+const KINDS = {
+  run_inno_installer: [['file', '--file'], ['sha256', '--sha256']],
+  run_inno_uninstaller: [['app_id', '--app-id']],
+  run_pkg_installer: [['file', '--file'], ['sha256', '--sha256']],
+  uninstall_pkg: [['pkg_id', '--pkg-id']],
+};
+const OP_FLAGS = new Set(['--file', '--sha256', '--app-id', '--pkg-id']);
+
+/** A malformed argument list (exit 2); any other failure is exit 1. */
+class UsageError extends Error {}
+
+/**
+ * The Windows verbatim form of `p` on `win32`: `\\?\` + a drive path, `\\?\UNC\` + the rest of a
+ * `\\server\share\…` path, an already verbatim `\\?\…` path unchanged. Any other platform: `p`.
+ * @param {string} p
+ * @param {string} platform a `process.platform` value
+ * @returns {string}
+ */
+export function verbatim(p, platform) {
+  if (platform !== 'win32' || p.startsWith('\\\\?\\')) return p;
+  if (p.startsWith('\\\\')) return `\\\\?\\UNC\\${p.slice(2)}`;
+  return `\\\\?\\${p}`;
+}
+
+/**
+ * @param {string} message
+ * @returns {never}
+ */
+function usage(message) {
+  throw new UsageError(`${message}\n${USAGE}`);
+}
+
+/**
+ * @param {string[]} argv
+ * @returns {{ state: string, ops: { kind: string, values: Map<string, string> }[] }}
+ */
+function parseArgs(argv) {
+  /** @type {string | undefined} */
+  let state;
+  /** @type {{ kind: string, values: Map<string, string> }[]} */
+  const ops = [];
+  for (let i = 0; i < argv.length; i += 2) {
+    const flag = argv.at(i) ?? '';
+    const value = argv.at(i + 1);
+    if (value === undefined) usage(`missing value after ${flag}`);
+    if (flag === '--state') {
+      if (state !== undefined) usage('--state given twice');
+      state = value;
+    } else if (flag === '--op') {
+      if (!Object.hasOwn(KINDS, value)) usage(`unknown op ${value}`);
+      ops.push({ kind: value, values: new Map() });
+    } else if (OP_FLAGS.has(flag)) {
+      const op = ops.at(-1);
+      if (op === undefined) usage(`${flag} before any --op`);
+      if (op.values.has(flag)) usage(`${flag} given twice for one --op`);
+      op.values.set(flag, value);
+    } else {
+      usage(`unknown argument ${flag}`);
+    }
+  }
+  if (state === undefined) usage('--state is required');
+  if (ops.length === 0) usage('at least one --op is required');
+  for (const op of ops) {
+    const want = (KINDS[op.kind] ?? []).map(([, flag]) => flag);
+    const got = [...op.values.keys()];
+    if (got.length !== want.length || !want.every((flag) => op.values.has(flag))) {
+      usage(`--op ${op.kind} takes exactly ${want.join(' ')}, got ${got.join(' ') || 'none'}`);
+    }
+  }
+  return { state, ops };
+}
+
+/**
+ * Writes the job `argv` describes and returns the line to print, `<job path> <sha256>`.
+ * @param {string[]} argv the arguments after the script name
+ * @param {string} platform a `process.platform` value (forced in the test)
+ * @returns {string}
+ */
+export function run(argv, platform) {
+  const { state, ops } = parseArgs(argv);
+  /** @param {string} p */
+  const canonical = (p) => (platform === 'win32' ? realpathSync.native(p) : p);
+  const job = {
+    job_version: 1,
+    ops: ops.map(({ kind, values }) => {
+      /** @type {Record<string, string>} */
+      const op = { op: kind };
+      for (const [key, flag] of KINDS[kind] ?? []) {
+        const value = values.get(flag) ?? '';
+        op[key] = key === 'file' ? verbatim(canonical(value), platform) : value;
+      }
+      return op;
+    }),
+  };
+  const bytes = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(job), 'utf8');
+  const jobs = path.join(canonical(state), 'jobs');
+  mkdirSync(jobs, { recursive: true });
+  const file = path.join(jobs, `job-${randomBytes(16).toString('hex')}.json`);
+  writeFileSync(file, bytes, { flag: 'wx' });
+  return `${verbatim(file, platform)} ${createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex')}`;
+}
+
+function main() {
+  let line;
+  try {
+    line = run(process.argv.slice(2), process.platform);
+  } catch (e) {
+    process.stderr.write(`write-test-job: ${e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)}\n`);
+    process.exit(e instanceof UsageError ? 2 : 1);
+  }
+  process.stdout.write(`${line}\n`);
+}
+
+const entry = process.argv.at(1);
+if (entry !== undefined && import.meta.url === pathToFileURL(entry).href) main();

+// node:test coverage of write-test-job.js (spec FR-001, FR-027, FR-100; plan §4.6).
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
+import { mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, readdirSync, readFileSync, realpathSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
+import os from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import test from 'node:test';
+
+import { run, verbatim } from './write-test-job.js';
+
+await test('write test job prints verbatim windows paths', () => {
+  // `verbatim` rows: drive, UNC, already verbatim, and every non-win32 platform unchanged.
+  assert.equal(verbatim('C:\\Users\\u\\a.exe', 'win32'), '\\\\?\\C:\\Users\\u\\a.exe');
+  assert.equal(verbatim('D:\\a\\_temp\\real', 'win32'), '\\\\?\\D:\\a\\_temp\\real');
+  assert.equal(verbatim('\\\\server\\share\\x', 'win32'), '\\\\?\\UNC\\server\\share\\x');
+  assert.equal(verbatim('\\\\?\\C:\\x\\y.exe', 'win32'), '\\\\?\\C:\\x\\y.exe');
+  assert.equal(verbatim('\\\\?\\UNC\\server\\share\\x', 'win32'), '\\\\?\\UNC\\server\\share\\x');
+  for (const platform of ['linux', 'darwin']) {
+    assert.equal(verbatim('C:\\x\\y.exe', platform), 'C:\\x\\y.exe');
+    assert.equal(verbatim('\\\\server\\share\\x', platform), '\\\\server\\share\\x');
+    assert.equal(verbatim('/tmp/x/y.pkg', platform), '/tmp/x/y.pkg');
+  }
+
+  // A job written into a temp state dir with the platform forced to win32.
+  const state = mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'km-write-test-job-'));
+  try {
+    const staging = path.join(state, 'staging');
+    mkdirSync(staging);
+    const exe = path.join(staging, 'a.exe');
+    const pkg = path.join(staging, 'b.pkg');
+    writeFileSync(exe, 'exe');
+    writeFileSync(pkg, 'pkg');
+    const sha = 'a'.repeat(64);
+    const appId = '{01234567-89AB-CDEF-0123-456789ABCDEF}';
+    const line = run(
+      [
+        '--state', state,
+        '--op', 'run_inno_installer', '--file', exe, '--sha256', sha,
+        '--op', 'run_inno_uninstaller', '--app-id', appId,
+        '--op', 'run_pkg_installer', '--sha256', sha, '--file', pkg,
+        '--op', 'uninstall_pkg', '--pkg-id', 'com.krateaudio.alpha',
+      ],
+      'win32',
+    );
+
+    const jobsDir = path.join(realpathSync.native(state), 'jobs');
+    const names = readdirSync(jobsDir);
+    assert.equal(names.length, 1, `one job file, got ${names.join(', ')}`);
+    const name = names[0] ?? '';
+    assert.match(name, /^job-[0-9a-f]{32}\.json$/);
+    const written = path.join(jobsDir, name);
+    const bytes = readFileSync(written);
+
+    const space = line.lastIndexOf(' ');
+    const printedPath = line.slice(0, space);
+    const printedSha = line.slice(space + 1);
+    assert.equal(printedPath, verbatim(written, 'win32'));
+    assert.ok(printedPath.startsWith('\\\\?\\'), printedPath);
+    assert.equal(printedSha, createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex'));
+
+    const text = bytes.toString('utf8');
+    assert.ok(text.startsWith('{"job_version":1,"ops":[{"op":"run_inno_installer","file":'), text);
+    /** @type {unknown} */
+    const parsed = JSON.parse(text);
+    const job = /** @type {{job_version: number, ops: Record<string, string>[]}} */ (parsed);
+    assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(job), ['job_version', 'ops']);
+    assert.equal(job.job_version, 1);
+    assert.deepEqual(
+      job.ops.map((op) => Object.keys(op)),
+      [['op', 'file', 'sha256'], ['op', 'app_id'], ['op', 'file', 'sha256'], ['op', 'pkg_id']],
+    );
+    const [inno, uninst, pkgOp, forget] = job.ops;
+    assert.deepEqual(inno, {
+      op: 'run_inno_installer',
+      file: verbatim(realpathSync.native(exe), 'win32'),
+      sha256: sha,
+    });
+    assert.ok(inno.file.startsWith('\\\\?\\'), inno.file);
+    assert.deepEqual(uninst, { op: 'run_inno_uninstaller', app_id: appId });
+    assert.deepEqual(pkgOp, {
+      op: 'run_pkg_installer',
+      file: verbatim(realpathSync.native(pkg), 'win32'),
+      sha256: sha,
+    });
+    assert.deepEqual(forget, { op: 'uninstall_pkg', pkg_id: 'com.krateaudio.alpha' });
+
+    // Malformed argument lists are refused before anything is written.
+    for (const bad of [
+      [],
+      ['--state', state],
+      ['--state', state, '--op', 'run_shell', '--file', exe, '--sha256', sha],
+      ['--state', state, '--op', 'run_inno_installer', '--file', exe],
+      ['--state', state, '--op', 'run_inno_uninstaller', '--pkg-id', 'com.krateaudio.alpha'],
+      ['--state', state, '--op', 'uninstall_pkg', '--pkg-id', 'a', '--pkg-id', 'b'],
+      ['--op', 'uninstall_pkg', '--pkg-id', 'a'],
+      ['--state', state, '--file', exe],
+    ]) {
+      assert.throws(() => run(bad, 'win32'), /usage/, bad.join(' '));
+    }
+    assert.equal(readdirSync(jobsDir).length, 1);
+  } finally {
+    rmSync(state, { recursive: true, force: true });
+  }
+});

+//! FR-105 (plan §4.5, SI-14): the manual elevation check a person runs by hand; no test, gate or workflow runs it.
+//!
+//! `cargo build -p krate-manager-helper`, then
+//! `cargo run -p krate-manager --example manual_elevation --features manual-elevation -- <empty dir>`.
+//!
+//! It raises the real elevation prompt once, for a job whose one op the helper refuses before any spawn, registry read
+//! or filesystem effect, prints the `LaunchOutcome` and the result file, and removes only what it created; `<empty dir>`
+//! itself stays. Expected: Windows `Exited(4)` (`E_HELPER_JOB_LOCATION`: `<empty dir>` is not under the profiles
+//! folder, FR-025); macOS `Exited(0)` with exactly one `refused E_HELPER_PKG_ID`.
+
+use krate_manager_core::install::job::{self, JobOp, WrittenJob};
+use krate_manager_core::install::{ElevatedCommand, Elevation, FsInstallIo};
+use krate_manager_core::platform::{APP_IDENTIFIER, NativePlatform, Os, Platform};
+use krate_manager_lib::resolve_elevation;
+use std::fs;
+use std::io;
+use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
+use std::process::ExitCode;
+use std::time::Duration;
+
+const USAGE: &str = "usage: manual_elevation <existing empty directory>";
+/// SI-14: the bound of the one launch.
+const LAUNCH_BOUND: Duration = Duration::from_secs(120);
+
+fn main() -> ExitCode {
+    let mut args = std::env::args_os().skip(1);
+    let (Some(arg), None) = (args.next(), args.next()) else {
+        eprintln!("{USAGE}");
+        return ExitCode::from(2);
+    };
+    let Some(dir) = empty_dir(Path::new(&arg)) else {
+        eprintln!(
+            "refused: {} is not an existing, empty directory that is not a link; nothing written",
+            Path::new(&arg).display()
+        );
+        return ExitCode::from(2);
+    };
+    // SI-14: one op the helper's own matcher refuses (`is_app_id` / `is_pkg_id`), never an installer op, so no run
+    // directory is ever created.
+    let op = match NativePlatform.os() {
+        Os::Windows => JobOp::RunInnoUninstaller {
+            app_id: "not-a-guid".to_owned(),
+        },
+        Os::Macos => JobOp::UninstallPkg {
+            pkg_id: "com.krateaudio.NOT-VALID".to_owned(),
+        },
+        Os::Linux => {
+            eprintln!("Linux has no elevated helper; nothing written");
+            return ExitCode::from(1);
+        }
+    };
+    // PD-23: `Path::parent` is lexical, so the synthetic exe sits in the profile dir (`target/debug`), where
+    // `cargo build -p krate-manager-helper` puts the helper, not in `target/debug/examples`.
+    let profile = match std::env::current_exe() {
+        Ok(exe) => exe.parent().and_then(Path::parent).map(Path::to_path_buf),
+        Err(e) => {
+            eprintln!("cannot read this example's path: {e}; nothing written");
+            return ExitCode::from(1);
+        }
+    };
+    let Some(profile) = profile else {
+        eprintln!("this example's path has no profile directory; nothing written");
+        return ExitCode::from(1);
+    };
+    let elevation = resolve_elevation(Some(&profile.join("manual_elevation")));
+    let Some(helper) = elevation.helper() else {
+        eprintln!(
+            "no helper beside {}: run `cargo build -p krate-manager-helper` first; nothing written",
+            profile.display()
+        );
+        return ExitCode::from(1);
+    };
+    let mut created = Vec::new();
+    let mut written = None;
+    let code = launch_once(&elevation, helper, op, &dir, &mut created, &mut written);
+    if remove_created(written.as_ref(), &created) {
+        code
+    } else {
+        ExitCode::from(1)
+    }
+}
+
+/// SI-14 step 1: `arg` exists, is a directory (by `symlink_metadata`, so a link or junction is refused) and has no
+/// entry; its canonical form, so the job path is the verbatim `\\?\` form on Windows (FR-027).
+fn empty_dir(arg: &Path) -> Option<PathBuf> {
+    if !fs::symlink_metadata(arg).ok()?.file_type().is_dir() {
+        return None;
+    }
+    if fs::read_dir(arg).ok()?.next().is_some() {
+        return None;
+    }
+    fs::canonicalize(arg).ok()
+}
+
+/// SI-14 steps 2, 3 and 5: the two directories (each recorded in `created` once made), the job (in `written`), the one
+/// launch, and what it printed.
+fn launch_once(
+    elevation: &dyn Elevation,
+    helper: &Path,
+    op: JobOp,
+    dir: &Path,
+    created: &mut Vec<PathBuf>,
+    written: &mut Option<WrittenJob>,
+) -> ExitCode {
+    let state = dir.join(APP_IDENTIFIER);
+    for path in [state.clone(), state.join(job::JOBS_DIR)] {
+        if let Err(e) = fs::create_dir(&path) {
+            eprintln!("cannot create {}: {e}", path.display());
+            return ExitCode::from(1);
+        }
+        created.push(path);
+    }
+    let job = match job::write(&FsInstallIo, &state, vec![op]) {
+        Ok(job) => written.insert(job),
+        Err(e) => {
+            eprintln!("cannot write the job: {e}");
+            return ExitCode::from(1);
+        }
+    };
+    let Some(job_path) = job.path.to_str() else {
+        eprintln!("the job path {} is not UTF-8", job.path.display());
+        return ExitCode::from(1);
+    };
+    let command = ElevatedCommand {
+        program: helper.to_path_buf(),
+        args: vec![
+            "--job".to_owned(),
+            job_path.to_owned(),
+            "--job-sha256".to_owned(),
+            job.sha256.clone(),
+        ],
+    };
+    println!("launching {} {:?}", command.program.display(), command.args);
+    let outcome = elevation.launch(&command, LAUNCH_BOUND);
+    println!("outcome: {outcome:?}");
+    match fs::read(&job.result) {
+        Ok(bytes) => println!(
+            "result {}:\n{}",
+            job.result.display(),
+            String::from_utf8_lossy(&bytes)
+        ),
+        Err(e) => println!("no result at {}: {e}", job.result.display()),
+    }
+    ExitCode::SUCCESS
+}
+
+/// SI-14 step 6: the job file, its result (if the helper wrote one), then the recorded directories innermost first,
+/// each with `remove_file` / `remove_dir` (never a tree removal). An error is printed, not retried.
+fn remove_created(job: Option<&WrittenJob>, created: &[PathBuf]) -> bool {
+    let mut clean = true;
+    let mut report = |path: &Path, removed: io::Result<()>| {
+        if let Err(e) = removed {
+            eprintln!("cannot remove {}: {e}", path.display());
+            clean = false;
+        }
+    };
+    if let Some(job) = job {
+        report(&job.path, fs::remove_file(&job.path));
+        if fs::symlink_metadata(&job.result).is_ok() {
+            report(&job.result, fs::remove_file(&job.result));
+        }
+    }
+    for path in created.iter().rev() {
+        report(path, fs::remove_dir(path));
+    }
+    clean
+}

diff --git a/apps/krate-manager/src-tauri/src/os_elevation.rs b/apps/krate-manager/src-tauri/src/os_elevation.rs

+//! The native elevation launcher (Phase 10 plan §4.5; FR-090, FR-091; PD-18, PD-23): built only by
+//! `crate::resolve_elevation`. It executes exactly the core's `ElevatedCommand`; every rule that decides anything
+//! (`windows_parameters`, `osascript_outcome`, `helper_beside`) is core's, tested there.
+
+use krate_manager_core::install::{ElevatedCommand, Elevation, ElevationGuard, LaunchOutcome};
+use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
+use std::time::Duration;
+
+#[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
+use macos::launch as launch_native;
+#[cfg(windows)]
+use windows::launch as launch_native;
+
+/// The resolved helper (fixed for the process, D19) and the process's one elevation guard (PD-9).
+pub struct NativeElevation {
+    helper: Option<PathBuf>,
+    guard: ElevationGuard,
+}
+
+impl NativeElevation {
+    pub fn new(helper: Option<PathBuf>) -> NativeElevation {
+        NativeElevation {
+            helper,
+            guard: ElevationGuard::new(),
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+impl Elevation for NativeElevation {
+    fn helper(&self) -> Option<&Path> {
+        self.helper.as_deref()
+    }
+
+    fn launch(&self, command: &ElevatedCommand, timeout: Duration) -> LaunchOutcome {
+        launch_native(command, timeout)
+    }
+
+    fn guard(&self) -> &ElevationGuard {
+        &self.guard
+    }
+}
+
+/// FR-091: Linux has no elevated launch; the core never calls it there (FR-071).
+#[cfg(not(any(windows, target_os = "macos")))]
+fn launch_native(_command: &ElevatedCommand, _timeout: Duration) -> LaunchOutcome {
+    LaunchOutcome::Failed("E_ELEVATION_NONE")
+}
+
+/// FR-090 (Windows): one `ShellExecuteExW` "runas" of the helper on its own COM thread; the timeout runs from the
+/// call's return, which `SEE_MASK_NOASYNC` holds until the consent prompt is answered (PD-18).
+#[cfg(windows)]
+mod windows {
+    use krate_manager_core::install::{self, ElevatedCommand, InstallError, LaunchOutcome};
+    use std::ffi::OsStr;
+    use std::os::windows::ffi::OsStrExt;
+    use std::ptr::null;
+    use std::time::Duration;
+    use windows_sys::Win32::Foundation::{
+        CloseHandle, ERROR_CANCELLED, GetLastError, HANDLE, WAIT_OBJECT_0, WAIT_TIMEOUT,
+    };
+    use windows_sys::Win32::System::Com::{
+        COINIT_APARTMENTTHREADED, COINIT_DISABLE_OLE1DDE, CoInitializeEx, CoUninitialize,
+    };
+    use windows_sys::Win32::System::Threading::{GetExitCodeProcess, WaitForSingleObject};
+    use windows_sys::Win32::UI::Shell::{
+        SEE_MASK_FLAG_NO_UI, SEE_MASK_NOASYNC, SEE_MASK_NOCLOSEPROCESS, SHELLEXECUTEINFOW,
+        ShellExecuteExW,
+    };
+
+    /// The elevation verb (FR-090).
+    const VERB: &str = "runas";
+    /// The longest finite wait: `u32::MAX` is `INFINITE`, which a timeout must never become.
+    const LONGEST_WAIT_MS: u32 = u32::MAX - 1;
+
+    /// UTF-16 plus a NUL.
+    fn wide(s: &OsStr) -> Vec<u16> {
+        s.encode_wide().chain(Some(0)).collect()
+    }
+
+    /// The process handle `ShellExecuteExW` returned under `SEE_MASK_NOCLOSEPROCESS`, closed exactly once when dropped.
+    struct Process(HANDLE);
+
+    impl Drop for Process {
+        fn drop(&mut self) {
+            // SAFETY: `self.0` is the non-NULL process handle a successful ShellExecuteExW returned; only
+            // `shell_execute` builds a `Process`, once per launch, and nothing uses the handle after this drop. Closing
+            // it leaves the process itself running (a timed-out helper is left alone, FR-090).
+            unsafe { CloseHandle(self.0) };
+        }
+    }
+
+    pub fn launch(command: &ElevatedCommand, timeout: Duration) -> LaunchOutcome {
+        let parameters = match install::windows_parameters(&command.args) {
+            Ok(parameters) => parameters,
+            Err(InstallError::Elevation { code }) => return LaunchOutcome::Failed(code),
+            Err(_) => return LaunchOutcome::Failed("E_ELEVATION_ARGS"),
+        };
+        // FR-090: on its own thread, so the apartment this launch initialises is never the caller's.
+        std::thread::scope(|scope| {
+            scope
+                .spawn(|| on_own_thread(command, &parameters, timeout))
+                .join()
+        })
+        .unwrap_or(LaunchOutcome::Failed("E_ELEVATION_SHELLEXECUTE"))
+    }
+
+    fn on_own_thread(
+        command: &ElevatedCommand,
+        parameters: &str,
+        timeout: Duration,
+    ) -> LaunchOutcome {
+        // SAFETY: the reserved pointer is NULL, as the CoInitializeEx page requires; this thread is new and has never
+        // initialised COM. A success (S_OK or S_FALSE, both non-negative) is balanced by the CoUninitialize below on
+        // this same thread; a failure is not balanced, and ShellExecuteExW is still tried.
+        let initialised = unsafe {
+            CoInitializeEx(
+                null(),
+                (COINIT_APARTMENTTHREADED | COINIT_DISABLE_OLE1DDE) as u32,
+            )
+        } >= 0;
+        let outcome = shell_execute(command, parameters, timeout);
+        if initialised {
+            // SAFETY: balances the successful CoInitializeEx above, on the thread that made it, after every COM use.
+            unsafe { CoUninitialize() };
+        }
+        outcome
+    }
+
+    fn shell_execute(
+        command: &ElevatedCommand,
+        parameters: &str,
+        timeout: Duration,
+    ) -> LaunchOutcome {
+        let verb = wide(OsStr::new(VERB));
+        let file = wide(command.program.as_os_str());
+        let parameters = wide(OsStr::new(parameters));
+        let mut info = SHELLEXECUTEINFOW {
+            cbSize: size_of::<SHELLEXECUTEINFOW>() as u32,
+            fMask: SEE_MASK_NOCLOSEPROCESS | SEE_MASK_NOASYNC | SEE_MASK_FLAG_NO_UI,
+            lpVerb: verb.as_ptr(),
+            lpFile: file.as_ptr(),
+            lpParameters: parameters.as_ptr(),
+            nShow: 0, // SW_HIDE
+            ..Default::default()
+        };
+        // SAFETY: `info` is a valid, writable SHELLEXECUTEINFOW whose `cbSize` names its own size; `verb`, `file` and
+        // `parameters` are NUL-terminated UTF-16 buffers that outlive the call; every other pointer and handle is
+        // NULL, which the SHELLEXECUTEINFOW page allows. The call writes only `info`'s output fields.
+        if unsafe { ShellExecuteExW(&mut info) } == 0 {
+            // SAFETY: GetLastError has no preconditions; it is read on this thread straight after the failed call.
+            return if unsafe { GetLastError() } == ERROR_CANCELLED {
+                LaunchOutcome::Refused
+            } else {
+                LaunchOutcome::Failed("E_ELEVATION_SHELLEXECUTE")
+            };
+        }
+        if info.hProcess.is_null() {
+            return LaunchOutcome::Failed("E_ELEVATION_NO_PROCESS");
+        }
+        let process = Process(info.hProcess);
+        let ms = u32::try_from(timeout.as_millis())
+            .map_or(LONGEST_WAIT_MS, |ms| ms.min(LONGEST_WAIT_MS));
+        // SAFETY: `process.0` is the live process handle ShellExecuteExW returned, open until `process` drops below.
+        match unsafe { WaitForSingleObject(process.0, ms) } {
+            WAIT_TIMEOUT => LaunchOutcome::TimedOut,
+            WAIT_OBJECT_0 => {
+                let mut code: u32 = 0;
+                // SAFETY: `process.0` is live (as above) and its process has exited; `code` is a valid, writable u32
+                // for the duration of the call.
+                if unsafe { GetExitCodeProcess(process.0, &mut code) } == 0 {
+                    LaunchOutcome::Failed("E_ELEVATION_SHELLEXECUTE")
+                } else {
+                    LaunchOutcome::Exited(code as i32)
+                }
+            }
+            _ => LaunchOutcome::Failed("E_ELEVATION_SHELLEXECUTE"),
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+/// FR-090 (macOS): the fixed `OSASCRIPT_LINES` with the helper and its four arguments as `argv`, never as script text;
+/// the timeout runs from the spawn, so the password dialog counts against it (PD-18).
+#[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
+mod macos {
+    use krate_manager_core::install::{self, ElevatedCommand, LaunchOutcome};
+    use std::io::Read;
+    use std::process::{Command, Stdio};
+    use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
+
+    /// PD-14: the one home of the osascript path.
+    const OSASCRIPT: &str = "/usr/bin/osascript";
+    /// How often a running osascript is polled.
+    const POLL: Duration = Duration::from_millis(100);
+
+    pub fn launch(command: &ElevatedCommand, timeout: Duration) -> LaunchOutcome {
+        let [line0, line1, line2] = install::OSASCRIPT_LINES;
+        let spawned = Command::new(OSASCRIPT)
+            .args(["-e", line0, "-e", line1, "-e", line2])
+            .arg(&command.program)
+            .args(&command.args)
+            .stdout(Stdio::null())
+            .stderr(Stdio::piped())
+            .spawn();
+        let Ok(mut child) = spawned else {
+            return LaunchOutcome::Failed("E_ELEVATION_OSASCRIPT");
+        };
+        let started = Instant::now();
+        loop {
+            match child.try_wait() {
+                Ok(Some(status)) => {
+                    let mut stderr = Vec::new();
+                    if let Some(mut pipe) = child.stderr.take() {
+                        // A short read leaves less text to classify; osascript_outcome decides on what was read.
+                        let _ = pipe.read_to_end(&mut stderr);
+                    }
+                    return install::osascript_outcome(
+                        status.code(),
+                        &String::from_utf8_lossy(&stderr),
+                    );
+                }
+                // FR-090: the child is left running; dropping `Child` never kills it.
+                Ok(None) if started.elapsed() >= timeout => return LaunchOutcome::TimedOut,
+                Ok(None) => std::thread::sleep(POLL),
+                Err(_) => return LaunchOutcome::Failed("E_ELEVATION_OSASCRIPT"),
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}

+//! Phase 10 FR-100 / SC-017: the stub installer of the `helper-elevated (windows-latest)` CI job. Run through
+//! `run_inno_installer`, it writes one JSON object to the compile-time path `ARGV_RECORDER_OUT`:
+//! `{"args": [...], "current_exe": "...", "sddl": "..."}` (`args` is the whole of `args_os`, argv[0] included, lossy;
+//! `sddl` is the SDDL of its own directory, read with `Get-Acl`), then exits 0. Never shipped, never run outside that
+//! job.
+
+use std::path::Path;
+use std::process::Command;
+
+/// The record file, baked in at build time (a path under `$RUNNER_TEMP`).
+const OUT: &str = env!("ARGV_RECORDER_OUT");
+
+fn json_string(s: &str) -> String {
+    let mut out = String::with_capacity(s.len() + 2);
+    out.push('"');
+    for c in s.chars() {
+        match c {
+            '"' => out.push_str("\\\""),
+            '\\' => out.push_str("\\\\"),
+            '\n' => out.push_str("\\n"),
+            '\r' => out.push_str("\\r"),
+            '\t' => out.push_str("\\t"),
+            c if u32::from(c) < 0x20 => out.push_str(&format!("\\u{:04x}", u32::from(c))),
+            c => out.push(c),
+        }
+    }
+    out.push('"');
+    out
+}
+
+fn dir_sddl(dir: &Path) -> String {
+    let script = format!(
+        "(Get-Acl -LiteralPath '{}').Sddl",
+        dir.to_string_lossy().replace('\'', "''")
+    );
+    match Command::new("pwsh")
+        .args(["-NoProfile", "-Command", &script])
+        .output()
+    {
+        Ok(o) => String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout).trim().to_owned(),
+        Err(e) => format!("<pwsh failed: {e}>"),
+    }
+}
+
+fn main() {
+    let args: Vec<String> = std::env::args_os()
+        .map(|a| json_string(&a.to_string_lossy()))
+        .collect();
+    let exe = std::env::current_exe().ok();
+    let exe_text = exe
+        .as_deref()
+        .map(|p| p.to_string_lossy().into_owned())
+        .unwrap_or_default();
+    let sddl = exe
+        .as_deref()
+        .and_then(Path::parent)
+        .map(dir_sddl)
+        .unwrap_or_default();
+    let record = format!(
+        "{{\"args\":[{}],\"current_exe\":{},\"sddl\":{}}}\n",
+        args.join(","),
+        json_string(&exe_text),
+        json_string(&sddl)
+    );
+    std::fs::write(OUT, record).expect("the record file is writable");
+}

+; Krate Manager CI fixture installer (spec FR-060, FR-100, FR-101, FR-102)
+; Inno Setup Script
+;
+; Compiled only by the manager-ci jobs helper-elevated and inno-locked-file with
+; Minionguyjpro/Inno-Setup-Action and installed only on their ephemeral runners; never run elsewhere.
+; It copies the plugin scripts' behaviour-deciding lines verbatim (PrivilegesRequired,
+; UsePreviousAppDir, the two Architectures lines, every [Files] Flags value, the [UninstallDelete]
+; entry; checked by scripts/inno-fixture.test.js). Only the AppId, the names and the roots differ:
+; every path lies under {#Root}, so no run writes the runner's real Program Files or ProgramData.
+;
+; Defines, passed with /D:
+;   Version  the fixture version (1.0.0, 1.0.1)
+;   Root     the root standing in for the system folders, e.g. $RUNNER_TEMP\real\root
+;   Payload  the staged inputs:
+;              {#Payload}\Fixture.vst3\Contents\x86_64-win\Fixture.vst3  the locked-dll cdylib, renamed
+;              {#Payload}\version.txt                                    the unlocked file, a text file holding the version
+;              {#Payload}\presets\                                       at least one preset file
+
+#define AppName "Fixture"
+#define AppPublisher "Krate Audio"
+#ifndef Version
+  #define Version "0.0.0"
+#endif
+#ifndef Root
+  #error Root is required: /DRoot=<a directory under the runner temp dir>
+#endif
+#ifndef Payload
+  #error Payload is required: /DPayload=<the staged input directory>
+#endif
+
+[Setup]
+; Fixed fixture application ID (GUID); its uninstall key is {2258598C-E668-45BD-A814-B9C5D1D2C5CD}_is1
+AppId={{2258598C-E668-45BD-A814-B9C5D1D2C5CD}
+AppName={#AppName}
+AppVersion={#Version}
+AppPublisher={#AppPublisher}
+
+; Installation directory - the plugin layout under {#Root}
+DefaultDirName={#Root}\VST3\Krate Audio\Fixture
+DisableProgramGroupPage=yes
+
+; Output file naming
+OutputDir=.
+OutputBaseFilename=Fixture-{#Version}-Windows-x64
+
+; Compression settings
+Compression=lzma2
+SolidCompression=yes
+
+; Copied from the plugin scripts
+PrivilegesRequired=admin
+
+; Copied from the plugin scripts
+UsePreviousAppDir=yes
+
+; Installer appearance
+WizardStyle=modern
+SetupIconFile=compiler:SetupClassicIcon.ico
+
+; Architecture - copied from the plugin scripts
+ArchitecturesAllowed=x64compatible
+ArchitecturesInstallIn64BitMode=x64compatible
+
+[Languages]
+Name: "english"; MessagesFile: "compiler:Default.isl"
+
+[Files]
+; The bundle, with the DLL at Fixture.vst3\Contents\x86_64-win\Fixture.vst3 (a real plug-in's layout)
+Source: "{#Payload}\Fixture.vst3\*"; DestDir: "{app}\Fixture.vst3"; Flags: ignoreversion recursesubdirs createallsubdirs
+; The unlocked file, a text file holding the version
+Source: "{#Payload}\version.txt"; DestDir: "{app}\Fixture.vst3"; Flags: ignoreversion
+; Presets, under {#Root}\ProgramData in place of the system-wide presets location
+Source: "{#Payload}\presets\*"; DestDir: "{#Root}\ProgramData\Krate Audio\Fixture"; Flags: ignoreversion recursesubdirs createallsubdirs
+
+[UninstallDelete]
+; Clean up the entire plugin directory on uninstall
+Type: filesandordirs; Name: "{app}\Fixture.vst3"

+//! Phase 10 FR-060 / FR-101: the fixture plug-in DLL of the `inno-locked-file` CI job. It embeds the version it was
+//! built with (`LOCKED_DLL_VERSION`, set by the job to 1.0.0 or 1.0.1), so the two fixture setups ship different bytes,
+//! as a real update does. Never shipped, never loaded outside that job.
+
+use core::ffi::c_char;
+
+/// The build-time version, NUL-terminated so a C caller can read it.
+static VERSION: &str = concat!(env!("LOCKED_DLL_VERSION"), "\0");
+
+/// Returns the version this DLL was built with, as a static NUL-terminated string.
+#[unsafe(no_mangle)]
+pub extern "C" fn fixture_version() -> *const c_char {
+    VERSION.as_ptr().cast()
+}
